+/**
+ * A SNIPPET'S OUTPUT TOO BIG TO HAND OVER GOES TO A FILE (the developer, 2026-10-11: "The agent just tried ... looping
+ * through hundreds of tables ... It only saved the first 5 and omitted the rest of the output. ... if it exceeds an
+ * amount we should write it to a file in a scratch area and give it the file name.")
+ *
+ *   [1] a snippet that RETURNS ~300 KB: the model gets a file in .code_boss/tmp and none of the text (2026-10-20:
+ *       it used to get the start, then read the file and have it twice); the file has all of it; a later read()
+ *       of the file reaches the last line
+ *   [1b] a big file read and returned: the record of the read is one line with its size, no line of the file
+ *   [2] a snippet that LOGS thousands of lines: the record says what it left out is in the snippet's log, names the
+ *       file, and does not say "log less"; the log has every line
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-snippet-result-overflow.mjs
+ */

+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;

+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const PORT = 29820 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 20);

+const requests = [];
+const adapter = {

+    return m ? `<remote-workspace><js>const t = await read("${m[1]}", { from: 398, to: 400 }); return t;</js></remote-workspace>` : 'No file was named.';

=== add test/test-spinner-revive.mjs
new 0c14d3f3f35d307f22f01fd5
+/**
+ * A frozen spinner during live work. startSpinTick() ran only when a run STARTED, while several paths stop the
+ * tick mid-run (the sub-agent background bar; a 'done' that lands before the work is really over). After that the
+ * glyph and the elapsed seconds are frozen while the label and the token counter keep updating — reported live as
+ * "Agent working" with dots that never move and tokens still coming down, which reads as a hang.
+ *
+ * Any phase event means work is in flight, so it must revive the tick. Run: node test/test-spinner-revive.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29233;
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+  (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '').split(String.fromCharCode(92)).join('/') + '/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+  'C:/Program Files (x86)/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+
+const slow = ['Thinking about this for a while.'];
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream() { for (const c of slow) { await sleep(200); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: c } }] }; } await sleep(6000); },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: slow.join('') } }] }; },
+};
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP: Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-spin-'));
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8'))
+    .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1200, height: 850 });
+    const errs = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+    await sleep(300);
+    const statusText = () => page.evaluate(() => (document.querySelector('#log .status')?.innerText || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim());
+
+    console.log('\n[A] the spinner ticks during a normal run');
+    await page.type('#input', 'take your time');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => !!document.querySelector('#log .status'), { timeout: 15000 });
+    const a1 = await statusText(); await sleep(1400); const a2 = await statusText();
+    ok(a1 !== a2, `the status row advances on its own (${JSON.stringify(a1)} -> ${JSON.stringify(a2)})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[B] a stopped tick is REVIVED by the next phase event');
+    // The mid-run stop is real (the sub-agent background bar does it; so does a 'done' landing before the work is
+    // over). It can no longer be OBSERVED as a freeze here, because the fix means the very next phase event brings
+    // the tick back — which is the point. So: stop it, then prove the row is moving again shortly after.
+    await page.evaluate(() => stopSpinTick());
+    const b1 = await statusText();
+    await sleep(1500);
+    const b2 = await statusText();
+    ok(b1 !== b2, `a stopped tick comes back on the next phase event (${JSON.stringify(b1)} -> ${JSON.stringify(b2)})`);
+    // And the contract, so the revive cannot later be deleted as redundant.
+    const src = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8');
+    const phaseBranch = src.slice(src.indexOf("      case 'phase':"), src.indexOf("      case 'tokens':"));
+    ok(/startSpinTick\(\)/.test(phaseBranch), 'the phase handler starts the tick, not only the run-start path');
+    ok(/!== 'canceled' && status\.phase !== 'done'/.test(phaseBranch), 'but never over a canceled or finished run');
+    ok(errs.length === 0, `no page errors (${errs.slice(0, 2).join(' | ')})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { await browser.close(); server.close(); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();
+
=== add test/test-spinner-stale-state.mjs
new 9f88ef6c40c91245d3ce5b66
+/**
+ * "AGENT WORKING…" AFTER THE RUN ENDED (live round 7, 2026-10-28: 229 s and counting, the server long done). A state
+ * fetched mid-run that arrives after the run's "done" starts the spinner again (renderSessionInChat, status running);
+ * the next state, not running, stopped only the elapsed clock - the glyph timer and its stall check ticked on, so the
+ * row said "Agent working" for good. A state that says the run is over now settles the row as the status event does.
+ *   [1] the sequence: a run ends; a running state arrives late; then a done one - the row is done and stays still
+ *   [2] a cancel's own row is left as it is
+ * Run: node test/test-spinner-stale-state.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29237;
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+  (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '').split(String.fromCharCode(92)).join('/') + '/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+  'C:/Program Files (x86)/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'Done quickly.' } }] }; },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'Done quickly.' } }] }; },
+};
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP: Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  process.env.CB_HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-spinstale-home-'));
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-spinstale-'));
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8'))
+    .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1200, height: 850 });
+    const errs = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+    await sleep(300);
+    const statusText = () => page.evaluate(() => (document.querySelector('#log .status')?.innerText || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim());
+
+    console.log('\n[1] a late running state, then a done one');
+    await page.type('#input', 'hello');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => session.status === 'done' && !!document.querySelector('#log .status'), { timeout: 15000 });
+    await sleep(500);
+    // the state fetched mid-run arrives now; then the next one, the run over
+    await page.evaluate(() => { renderSessionInChat({ ...session, status: 'running' }); });
+    await sleep(2200);
+    const mid = await statusText();
+    ok(/Agent working/.test(mid), `the late running state starts the row again, as before (${mid.slice(0, 50)})`);
+    await page.evaluate(() => { renderSessionInChat({ ...session, status: 'done' }); });
+    await sleep(300);
+    const a = await statusText(); await sleep(1500); const b = await statusText();
+    ok(!/Agent working/.test(b) && a === b, `the done state settles it: no longer working, and it stays still (${JSON.stringify(b.slice(0, 50))})`);
+    ok(await page.evaluate(() => status.phase === 'done'), 'the row is done');
+
+    console.log('\n[2] a cancel keeps its own row');
+    await page.evaluate(() => { status.setCanceled ? status.setCanceled() : setCanceled(); renderSessionInChat({ ...session, status: 'idle' }); });
+    ok(await page.evaluate(() => status.phase === 'canceled'), 'still canceled');
+    ok(errs.length === 0, `no page errors (${errs.slice(0, 2).join(' | ')})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { await browser.close(); server.close(); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();

=== add test/test-sse-catchup-e2e.mjs
new 2cb5f7820721e866974a8576
+/**
+ * SSE CATCH-UP: a frame broadcast while a tab's stream is DOWN is no longer lost. Every frame carries an `id:`
+ * (a server sequence) and sits in a bounded ring; a reconnect that presents the last id it saw (Last-Event-ID on the
+ * browser's own auto-reconnect, ?since= on a manual one) gets exactly the missed frames replayed — no full init, no
+ * re-render — and a gap the ring cannot cover falls back to the full init snapshot. Drives the real UI: drops the
+ * stream, runs a scripted turn while it is down, reconnects, and checks what arrived and how.
+ * Run: node test/test-sse-catchup-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29266;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+const TURNS = ['First reply, sent while the stream was down. CATCHUP_A', 'Second reply over the live stream. CATCHUP_B'];
+let call = 0;
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream() { const t = TURNS[Math.min(call++, TURNS.length - 1)]; yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t } }] }; },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP — Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-sse-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'README.md'), 'hello\n');
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8'))
+    .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  const logs = [];
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    page.on('console', (m) => logs.push(m.text()));
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1200, height: 800 });
+    const errs = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+    await sleep(300);
+
+    console.log('\n[A] every frame carries an id, and the tab tracks the last one it saw');
+    const id0 = await page.evaluate(() => _lastSseId);
+    ok(/^\d+$/.test(String(id0)), `the init frame carried a numeric id (${id0})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[B] the stream DROPS; a whole turn runs while it is down; a reconnect replays exactly the gap');
+    await page.evaluate(() => { try { _es.close(); } catch {} _es = null; });   // simulate a network drop
+    await page.evaluate(() => fetch('/api/message', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text: 'hi' }) }));
+    // The turn completes server-side with NO subscriber — its frames exist only in the ring.
+    await page.waitForFunction(async () => { const s = await (await fetch('/api/state')).json(); return (s.events || []).some((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text' && /CATCHUP_A/.test(e.content || '')) && (s.status === 'idle' || s.status === 'done'); }, { timeout: 15000, polling: 200 });
+    ok(await page.evaluate(() => !document.body.innerText.includes('CATCHUP_A')), 'before reconnecting, the tab has NOT seen the reply (the stream was down)');
+    const logsBefore = logs.length;
+    // Count INIT snapshots (a full resync) — the invariant is "a resumed reconnect needs no init". (clearLog is not
+    // the right probe: a replayed event-patch legitimately re-renders via the client's normal patch path.)
+    await page.evaluate(() => { window.__clears = 0; window.__inits = 0; const o = clearLog; window.clearLog = (...a) => { window.__clears++; return o(...a); }; const i = onInit; window.onInit = (...a) => { window.__inits++; return i(...a); }; connectStream(); });
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.body.innerText.includes('CATCHUP_A'), { timeout: 10000 });
+    await sleep(300);
+    const resumed = logs.slice(logsBefore).find((l) => /\[sse\] resumed at #\d+ — (\d+) missed/.test(l)) || '';
+    const missed = Number((resumed.match(/— (\d+) missed/) || [])[1] || 0);
+    ok(!!resumed && missed > 0, `the server RESUMED the tab and replayed the gap (${resumed || 'no resumed marker'})`);
+    ok(await page.evaluate(() => window.__inits) === 0, 'the missed reply arrived WITHOUT a full init snapshot (onInit never ran)');
+    ok(!logs.slice(logsBefore).some((l) => /SSE gap/.test(l)), 'no version gap was detected — the replay was contiguous');
+    const st = await page.evaluate(async () => ({ v: (await (await fetch('/api/state')).json()).version, next: expectedNextVersion, has: (session.events || []).some((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text' && /CATCHUP_A/.test(e.content || '')) }));
+    ok(st.next === st.v + 1, `the tab's version cursor is exactly current (expected next ${st.next}, server at ${st.v})`);
+    ok(st.has, 'the replayed assistant-text is in session.events');
+
+    console.log('\n[C] a gap the ring cannot cover falls back to the full init snapshot');
+    const logsC = logs.length;
+    await page.evaluate(() => { try { _es.close(); } catch {} _es = null; _lastSseId = '999999'; connectStream(); });   // an id from a "previous life"
+    await page.waitForFunction((n) => n < 0 || true, { timeout: 100 }, 0).catch(() => {});
+    await sleep(800);

+/**
+ * A TOOL LEFT MID-FLIGHT MUST NOT PULSE FOR EVER ON A FRESH PAGE. Live, the run-end 'status' frame sweeps a tool
+ * row whose result never came; a fresh page replays history and got no such frame, so the last tool use before the
+ * app closed showed as running indefinitely (developer report, 2026-09-16). Now the replay settles it.
+ *   [A] a hanging tool → the row pulses on the live page; cancel → the live page settles it (regression)
+ *   [B] a FRESH page over the same conversation: the replayed call is settled — no pulsing row, no running
+ *       placeholder; the placeholder reads "no result recorded"
+ *   [C] no page errors

=== add test/test-stall-cut.mjs
new 3ff4f9048051818379a3d527
+/**
+ * THE STALL-CUT: a reply that goes silent mid-stream is taken as finished and handed back to the model to continue.
+ *
+ * Measured on the production endpoint: every clean reply streamed with no silence over 1.5s, and every reply
+ * that repeated itself went silent for 8-38s mid-stream and restated its plan when the stream came back. Replayed
+ * offline (#78), a model handed its own reply cut at the stall continued from there 2 of 2 times - straight to
+ * the tool block, no restatement, no apology. So: silence past the cutoff, AFTER the first content, ends the
+ * stream; the partial stays on screen; the worker pushes it as the model's own finished turn with the note and
+ * calls again. Bounded per run. An empty continuation means the partial was complete.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-stall-cut.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-stallcut-home-'));
+process.env.CB_STALL_CUT_MS = '300';
+process.env.CB_STALL_CUT_BLOCK_MS = '300';   // the worker-driven cuts below land INSIDE a <js> snippet; keep their timing (the real default is max(4×, 45s))
+const { runTurnStreaming, streamDiagSnapshot, stallCutNote, unclosedBlockAt, seamOverlap } = await import('../src/agent/turn-stream.mjs');
+const { createHttpAdapter } = await import('../src/llm/openai-http.mjs');
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const chunk = (s) => ({ id: 'c1', choices: [{ delta: { content: s } }] });
+/** Hangs until the request's signal aborts, then throws the way a real reader does. */
+const hangUntilAbort = (signal) => new Promise((_, rej) => {
+  const die = () => rej(Object.assign(new Error('aborted'), { name: 'AbortError' }));
+  if (signal?.aborted) return die();
+  signal?.addEventListener?.('abort', die, { once: true });
+});
+
+async function main() {
+  console.log('\n[1] silence after the first content, past the cutoff, ends the reply with what arrived');
+  {
+    const adapter = { async *completeStream(req) { yield chunk('I will read the file first. '); await hangUntilAbort(req.signal); } };
+    let cut = null; let prose = '';
+    const t0 = Date.now();
+    const text = await runTurnStreaming(adapter, { model: 'm', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'x' }] }, {
+      onStallCut: (s) => { cut = s; }, onProseSegmentEnd: ({ content }) => { prose += content; },
+    });
+    ok(text === 'I will read the file first. ', `the partial is the reply (${JSON.stringify(text)})`);
+    ok(cut && cut.at === text.length && cut.ms >= 300 && cut.ms < 2000, `onStallCut reports where and how long (${JSON.stringify(cut)})`);
+    ok(Date.now() - t0 < 2000, 'and it did not wait for the endpoint');
+    const rec = streamDiagSnapshot().slice(-1)[0];
+    ok(rec && rec.stallCut && rec.stallCut.at === text.length, 'the stream record carries the cut');
+    ok(prose.trim() === text.trim(), 'the prose reached the UI as a finished segment');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[1b] empty keepalive chunks during the silence do not reset the cut');
+  {
+    // Seen in production on the wrong build: a 20s silence recorded as two gaps, which a 5s cut should never have
+    // let happen. An endpoint that sends empty deltas while the model is silent would reset a timer that counts
+    // chunks; the cut counts content.
+    const empty = () => ({ id: 'c1', choices: [{ delta: {} }] });
+    const adapter = { async *completeStream(req) {
+      yield chunk('text, then silence. ');
+      for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) { await sleep(100); yield empty(); }   // 800ms of keepalives, no text
+      yield chunk('never seen');
+    } };
+    let cut = null;
+    const text = await runTurnStreaming(adapter, { model: 'm', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'x' }], stallCutMs: 300 }, { onStallCut: (s) => { cut = s; } });
+    ok(text === 'text, then silence. ', `cut despite the keepalives (${JSON.stringify(text)})`);
+    ok(cut && cut.ms >= 300 && cut.ms < 700, `at the content deadline, not the chunk deadline (${cut && cut.ms}ms)`);
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[1c] the default is 8s, and the env is the reader\'s source');
+  {
+    const saved = process.env.CB_STALL_CUT_MS;
+    delete process.env.CB_STALL_CUT_MS;
+    const { stallCutMsDefault } = await import('../src/agent/turn-stream.mjs');
+    ok(stallCutMsDefault() === 8000, `unset: 8000 (${stallCutMsDefault()})`);
+    process.env.CB_STALL_CUT_MS = '0'; ok(stallCutMsDefault() === 0, '0: off');
+    process.env.CB_STALL_CUT_MS = saved;
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[2] the first-byte wait is not a stall: a slow start streams to the end');
+  {
+    const adapter = { async *completeStream() { await sleep(600); yield chunk('slow to start, '); yield chunk('then fine.'); } };
+    let cut = null;
+    const text = await runTurnStreaming(adapter, { model: 'm', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'x' }] }, { onStallCut: (s) => { cut = s; } });
+    ok(text === 'slow to start, then fine.' && cut === null, 'a 600ms first-byte wait against a 300ms cutoff: no cut, whole reply');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[3] the cutoff is a knob, and 0 turns it off');
+  {
+    const adapter = { async *completeStream() { yield chunk('a'); await sleep(500); yield chunk('b'); } };
+    const withCut = await runTurnStreaming(adapter, { model: 'm', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'x' }], stallCutMs: 300 }, {});
+    ok(withCut === 'a', `a 500ms silence against 300ms: cut at "a" (${JSON.stringify(withCut)})`);
+    const off = await runTurnStreaming(adapter, { model: 'm', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'x' }], stallCutMs: 0 }, {});
+    ok(off === 'ab', 'stallCutMs 0: the same silence is waited out');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[3b] inside a tool block the allowance is longer: the same silence that cuts prose is waited out mid-snippet');
+  {
+    // gemini-3.8-flash on the genai gateway delivered a 40 KB write in bursts with 10-20s silences; an 8s cut
+    // fired inside the block twice, the partial write was refused each time and the model restarted the file.
+    const inBlock = { async *completeStream() { yield chunk('<remote-workspace><js>await write("a.txt", `line one'); await sleep(500); yield chunk(' line two`);</js></remote-workspace>'); } };
+    let cut = null;
+    const t1 = await runTurnStreaming(inBlock, { model: 'm', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'x' }], stallCutMs: 300, stallCutBlockMs: 1500 }, { onStallCut: (s) => { cut = s; } });
+    ok(/line two`\);<\/js>/.test(t1) && cut === null, `a 500ms silence INSIDE a block against a 300ms cut: waited out (block allowance 1500ms) — the write arrived whole`);
+    const tooLong = { async *completeStream() { yield chunk('<remote-workspace><js>await write("a.txt", `line one'); await sleep(2000); yield chunk(' never'); } };
+    cut = null;
+    const t2 = await runTurnStreaming(tooLong, { model: 'm', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'x' }], stallCutMs: 300, stallCutBlockMs: 1500 }, { onStallCut: (s) => { cut = s; } });
+    ok(!/never/.test(t2) && cut && cut.inBlock === true && cut.thresholdMs === 1500 && cut.ms >= 1500, `a silence past the block allowance still cuts, and says it was in a block (${JSON.stringify(cut)})`);
+    const { stallCutNote } = await import('../src/agent/turn-stream.mjs');
+    const n = stallCutNote(9, 1234, { blockOpener: '<js>' });
+    ok(/INSIDE a tool block that opened with <js>/.test(n) && /CLOSE it with <\/js><\/remote-workspace>/.test(n) && /no new block/.test(n), 'the in-block note names the opener and the closing tag, and forbids a fresh block first');
+    ok(!/INSIDE a tool block/.test(stallCutNote(9, 1234)), 'the plain note is unchanged');
+    // The default allowance: 4× the setting, at least 45s — read the same way the runner reads it.
+    const saved = process.env.CB_STALL_CUT_BLOCK_MS; delete process.env.CB_STALL_CUT_BLOCK_MS;
+    ok(Math.max(8000 * 4, 45000) === 45000 && Math.max(20000 * 4, 45000) === 80000, 'the default block allowance is max(4× setting, 45s)');
+    process.env.CB_STALL_CUT_BLOCK_MS = saved;
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[4] the adapter records the cut as its own ending, not as a cancel');
+  {
+    const txs = [];
+    const enc = new TextEncoder();
+    const a = createHttpAdapter({ baseURL: 'http://x/v1', onTransaction: (t) => txs.push(t), fetchImpl: async (url, init) => new Response(new ReadableStream({
+      start(c) { c.enqueue(enc.encode(`data: ${JSON.stringify(chunk('partial '))}` + String.fromCharCode(10, 10))); init.signal?.addEventListener?.('abort', () => { try { c.error(Object.assign(new Error('aborted'), { name: 'AbortError' })); } catch {} }); },
+    }), { status: 200, headers: { 'content-type': 'text/event-stream' } }) });
+    const text = await runTurnStreaming(a, { model: 'm', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'x' }], stallCutMs: 300 }, {});
+    await sleep(100);
+    ok(text === 'partial ', 'the partial came through the real adapter');
+    ok(txs.length === 1 && txs[0].endedEarly === true && txs[0].endedBecause === 'stall-cut', `recorded as stall-cut (${txs[0] && txs[0].endedBecause})`);
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[5] through the worker: the partial stays, the note goes in, the model continues');
+  {
+    const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-stallcut-'));
+    const seen = [];
+    let mode = 'continue';
+    const adapter = {
+      async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+      async *completeStream(req) {
+        seen.push(req);
+        // The flat transport sends the WHOLE transcript in one message (priming demo included, with its own tool
+        // result), so the current turn is read off the tail, the way the real endpoint reads the latest turn.
+        const full = String(req.messages[req.messages.length - 1].content);
+        // The latest developer turn - the note, or the tool results. An OPENING heading sits at a line start; the
+        // closing one is "/--- developer", which a bare lastIndexOf would find instead.
+        const body = full.slice(full.lastIndexOf(String.fromCharCode(10) + '--- developer'));
+        if (/Your previous reply went silent/.test(body)) {
+          if (mode === 'empty') { return; }                                      // nothing to add: it was complete
+          if (mode === 'orphan') { mode = 'after-orphan'; yield chunk('<js>return await list(".");</js>'); return; }   // a call outside its wrapper: no prose, no call parsed
+          if (mode === 'always-stall') { yield chunk('more, '); await hangUntilAbort(req.signal); return; }
+          yield chunk('<remote-workspace><js>return await list(".");</js></remote-workspace>'); return;   // picks up: straight to the tool
+        }
+        if (/<remote-workspace-result/.test(body)) { yield chunk('Done: listed the project.'); return; }
+        if (mode === 'after-orphan') { mode = 'done-orphan'; yield chunk('<remote-workspace><js>return await list(".");</js></remote-workspace>'); return; }   // asked again: the call, wrapped
+        yield chunk('I will list the project first. ');
+        await hangUntilAbort(req.signal);                                        // the stall
+      },
+    };
+    const PORT = 29491;
+    const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+    const B = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+    const send = async (text) => fetch(B + '/api/message', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text, model: 'm', critic: false, phaseAttack: false }) });
+    const settle = async () => { for (let i = 0; i < 400; i++) { const s = await (await fetch(B + '/api/state')).json(); if (s.status !== 'running') return s; await sleep(50); } return null; };
+    try {
+      await send('list it');
+      const s = await settle();
+      ok(s && s.status !== 'running', `the run settled (${s && s.status})`);
+      const ev = s.events || [];
+      const nudge = ev.find((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && e.verb === 'nudge' && /went silent/.test(JSON.stringify(e.args || {})));
+      ok(!!nudge, 'a muted row says the stream went silent and the reply was taken as finished');
+      const noteRow = ev.find((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.verb === 'nudge' && /Do not apologise/.test(e.content || ''));
+      ok(!!noteRow, 'and shows the exact note sent');
+      const texts = ev.filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text').map((e) => e.content);
+      ok(texts.some((t) => /I will list the project first/.test(t)), 'the partial stayed on screen (not retracted)');
+      ok(texts.some((t) => /Done: listed the project/.test(t)), 'and the run carried on to a real answer');
+      ok(ev.some((e) => e.type === 'js-step' && e.fn === 'list' && e.status === 'ok'), 'the continuation ran the tool it picked up with');
+      const cont = seen.find((q) => /went silent/.test(String(q.messages[q.messages.length - 1].content)));
+      const body = String(cont.messages[cont.messages.length - 1].content);
+      const tag = (/--- you ([0-9a-z]{3,8}) ---/.exec(body) || [])[1];
+      ok(tag && body.includes('--- you ' + tag + ' ---\nI will list the project first.') && body.includes('I will list the project first.\n/--- you ' + tag + ' ---'), 'the model saw its partial as its own closed turn');
+      ok(body.includes(stallCutNote(1, 'I will list the project first.'.length).slice(0, 60)), 'followed by the note as a developer turn');
+
+      // An empty continuation: the model closed with nothing to add, so the partial was the whole answer.
+      mode = 'empty'; seen.length = 0;
+      await send('list it again');
+      const s2 = await settle();
+      const ev2 = (s2.events || []).slice(ev.length);
+      ok(s2 && s2.status !== 'running', 'the run settled');
+      ok(!ev2.some((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text' && /don't have a written reply|came back without a written reply/.test(e.content || '')), 'no synthesized "no reply" closer was shown');
+      ok(ev2.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && e.verb === 'nudge').length === 1, 'one continuation was attempted');
+
+      // A continuation that is ONLY a call the parser did not take (outside its wrapper) cleans to no prose - and is
+      // not "nothing to add" (the developer, 2026-10-11: "a nudge happens, but it just stops"). The model is asked
+      // again and the run goes on.
+      mode = 'orphan'; seen.length = 0;
+      const before2b = (s2.events || []).length;
+      await send('list it, carefully');
+      const s2b = await settle();
+      const ev2b = (s2b.events || []).slice(before2b);
+      ok(s2b && s2b.status !== 'running' && mode === 'done-orphan', `after a continuation that was only an unwrapped call, the model was called again (${seen.length} calls)`);
+      ok(ev2b.some((e) => e.type === 'js-step' && e.fn === 'list' && e.status === 'ok') && ev2b.some((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text' && /Done: listed the project/.test(e.content || '')), 'the call ran and the run reached its answer');
+      ev.push(...ev2b);
+
+      // Bounded: a stream that stalls every time gets a few continuations, then the run ends normally.
+      mode = 'always-stall'; seen.length = 0; process.env.CB_STALL_CUT_MAX = '2';

+      ok(cuts <= 2, `continuations are bounded (${cuts} of max 2)`);
+    } finally { server.close(); await rm(projectDir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); }
+  }

+  console.log('\n[6] where a reply was cut: inside a tool block, or in prose');

+  }

+    ok(a.text === seed + 'ne2\nline3\n`);</js>\n</remote-workspace>\nWrote it.', 'the text is the seed plus the continuation');
+    const b = await run(['<js>await write("a.txt", `line1\nline2\nline3\n`);</js></remote-workspace>']);
+    ok(b.blocks[0] === '\n<js>await write("a.txt", `line1\nline2\nline3\n`);</js>', `a re-typed overlap is dropped, not written twice (${JSON.stringify(b.blocks[0])})`);
+    const c = await run(['<remote-workspace>\n<js>await write("a.txt", `fresh\n`);</js></remote-workspace>']);
+    ok(c.blocks.length === 1 && c.blocks[0] === '\n<js>await write("a.txt", `fresh\n`);</js>', `a continuation that starts the block over is taken whole (${JSON.stringify(c.blocks[0])})`);
+    const rec = streamDiagSnapshot().slice(-1)[0];
+    ok(rec && rec.resumedBlock && rec.resumedBlock.restarted === true, 'and the stream record says so');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[8] through the worker: a block cut mid-way is finished by the continuation and RUNS');
+  {
+    // Reported from production: the stall-cut fired 1501 characters into a reply, inside a <js> block. The model
+    // continued exactly where it stopped - mid-script, no opening tag - so the continuation was read as prose and
+    // a page of code was printed to the chat instead of run.
+    const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-stallcut-blk-'));
+    const seen = [];
+    const adapter = {
+      async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+      async *completeStream(req) {
+        seen.push(String(req.messages[req.messages.length - 1].content));
+        const full = seen[seen.length - 1];
+        const body = full.slice(full.lastIndexOf(String.fromCharCode(10) + '--- developer'));
+        if (/Your previous reply went silent/.test(body)) { yield chunk('ne2\nline3\n`);</js>\n'); yield chunk('</remote-workspace>'); return; }
+        if (/<remote-workspace-result/.test(body)) { yield chunk('Wrote a.txt.'); return; }
+        yield chunk('I will write the file.\n<remote-workspace>\n<js>await write("a.txt", `line1\nli');
+        await hangUntilAbort(req.signal);                                        // the stall, mid-block
+      },
+    };
+    const PORT = 29492;
+    const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });

+    try {

+      ok(ev.some((e) => e.type === 'js-step' && e.fn === 'write' && e.status === 'ok'), 'the write ran');
+      const shown = ev.filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text' || e.type === 'assistant-text-chunk').map((e) => e.content || '').join('');

+      ok(ev.some((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && e.verb === 'nudge' && /went silent/.test(JSON.stringify(e.args || {}))), 'the muted row still says what happened');

+/**
+ * ONE COUNT BUBBLE, NOT TWO. Every number bubble in the status bar is the same bubble: the one a plugin status item
+ * / ▶ / 🧩 wears (.ps-badge) and the uncommitted-count on a git branch pill (.gp-count) must be the same SIZE and sit

=== add test/test-status-bar-consistency.mjs
new 31a321bf030210dd73b31a9a
+/**
+ * THE STATUS BAR HAS THREE ROLES AND ONE SET OF NUMBERS PER ROLE (the rules are written out above .side-chrome in
+ * ui.html). This drives the real bar and holds them, because these values drifted apart once already: the branch
+ * glyph sat at 12px and 0.85 opacity beside 14px full-strength icons on the same line, so the branch pills read as
+ * a lesser class of thing, and the branch pill was the only control that hovered white instead of accent.
+ *
+ *   TEXT   11px mono — project path, branch name, Edit, token count. Nothing sets its own size.
+ *   ICON   14px full strength — a plugin item, the background-process arrow, the plugin-status glyph, the gear,
+ *          and the branch glyph.
+ *   HOVER  goes accent, EXCEPT where colour already carries state (Edit is green for on, the plugin-status glyph
+ *          is red for needs-attention) — those brighten instead, so the state survives being pointed at.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-status-bar-consistency.mjs
+ */

+};\n`);

+const adapter = { async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'ok' } }] }; }, async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; }, async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; } };

+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1200, height: 800 });
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+    await page.waitForSelector('.plugin-status-btn[data-plugin="counter"] .ps-text', { timeout: 10000 });
+    // Put every element on screen at once, through the real renderer where there is one.
+    await page.evaluate(() => {
+      renderGitProjects([{ path: 'D:/x/demo', name: 'demo', branch: 'main', uncommitted: 3 }]);
+      const t = document.getElementById('tokenCounter'); if (t) { t.style.display = ''; t.textContent = '139K'; }
+      const w = document.getElementById('pluginWarn'); if (w) w.style.display = '';
+      const p = document.getElementById('projectPath'); if (p) p.textContent = 'D:/x/demo';
+      // ▶ shows only while a background process runs (2026-10-10): one is running, as far as the bar knows
+      _bgProcs = [{ id: 'bg-1', alive: true, label: 'build' }]; renderBgIcon();
+    });

+      const el = document.querySelector(s); if (!el) return null;
+      const c = getComputedStyle(el), r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
+      return { fontSize: c.fontSize, fontFamily: c.fontFamily, fontWeight: c.fontWeight, color: c.color, opacity: c.opacity, h: Math.round(r.height * 10) / 10, mid: Math.round((r.top + r.bottom) * 5) / 10 };
+    }, sel);
+
+    console.log('\n[1] TEXT — one size, one family, one weight; nothing in the bar sets its own');
+    const text = {};
+    for (const [label, sel] of Object.entries(TEXT)) text[label] = await read(sel);
+    for (const [label, m] of Object.entries(text)) ok(m && m.fontSize === '11px', `${label} is 11px (${m ? m.fontSize : 'MISSING'})`);

+    const wts = new Set(Object.values(text).map((m) => m && m.fontWeight));
+    ok(wts.size === 1, `and the same weight (${[...wts].join(', ')})`);
+

+    for (const [label, sel] of Object.entries(ICON)) icon[label] = await read(sel);
+    for (const [label, m] of Object.entries(icon)) ok(m && m.h === 14, `${label} is 14px tall (${m ? m.h : 'MISSING'})`);
+    for (const [label, m] of Object.entries(icon)) ok(m && Number(m.opacity) === 1, `${label} is at full strength, not dimmed (${m ? m.opacity : 'MISSING'})`);
+    // The specific regression: the branch glyph is an ICON, so it matches the icons and NOT the 11px text beside it.
+    ok(icon['the branch glyph'].h === icon['a plugin item'].h, `the branch glyph matches a plugin item exactly (${icon['the branch glyph'].h} vs ${icon['a plugin item'].h}) — it was 12px at 0.85 opacity`);
+    ok(icon['the branch glyph'].h > parseFloat(text['a branch name'].fontSize), 'and it is deliberately larger than the branch name it sits beside, like every other icon in the bar');
+
+    console.log('\n[3] COLOUR carries state — muted at rest, green for Edit on, red for needs-attention');
+    const muted = text['the project path'].color;
+    ok(text['a branch name'].color === muted && text['the token count'].color === muted, `the path, a branch name and the token count share one resting colour (${muted})`);
+    ok(text['the Edit toggle'].color !== muted, `Edit is not that colour — it is saying it is ON (${text['the Edit toggle'].color})`);
+    ok(icon['the plugin-status glyph'].color !== muted, `and the plugin-status glyph is not either — it is saying something needs attention (${icon['the plugin-status glyph'].color})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[4] HOVER goes accent — except where the colour is already saying something');
+    const accent = await page.evaluate(() => getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--accent').trim());
+    const hoverColor = async (sel) => { await page.hover(sel); return (await read(sel)).color; };
+    const toRgb = async (css) => page.evaluate((c) => { const d = document.createElement('div'); d.style.color = c; document.body.appendChild(d); const v = getComputedStyle(d).color; d.remove(); return v; }, css);
+    const accentRgb = await toRgb(accent);
+    for (const [label, sel] of [['the project path', '.proj-path'], ['a branch pill', '#gitProjects .gp-badge'], ['a plugin item', '.plugin-status-btn[data-plugin="counter"]'], ['the settings gear', '#settingsBtn']]) {
+      const c = await hoverColor(sel);
+      ok(c === accentRgb, `${label} goes accent on hover (${c} vs ${accentRgb})`);
+    }
+    const editHover = await hoverColor('#editToggle');

+    const warnHover = await hoverColor('#pluginWarn');
+    ok(warnHover !== accentRgb && warnHover === icon['the plugin-status glyph'].color, `the plugin-status glyph keeps its red on hover, so "needs attention" does not vanish exactly when you point at it (${warnHover})`);

+    ok(Math.max(...mids) - Math.min(...mids) < 0.75, `every element is centred on the same line (spread ${(Math.max(...mids) - Math.min(...mids)).toFixed(2)}px)`);

=== add test/test-status-row-repaint.mjs
new a140dc3b6e7843a403abc111
+/**
+ * THE WORKING LINE SURVIVES A REPAINT (the developer, 2026-10-23: "when nudges happen, it doesn't show the loading
+ * line. It looks like it's finished but after a while new messages appear").
+ *
+ * Every model call, once recorded, stamps the messages it answered with its raw-log id (an event-patch), and an
+ * event-patch re-renders the whole chat. The re-render dropped the working line and only the NEXT call's start put it
+ * back - so while the tools of a reply ran (a slow query, a build) the chat showed no working line at all.
+ * This drives the real UI: the mocked agent runs a 4 s command, and the line is looked for while it runs.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-status-row-repaint.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+const HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-repaint-home-'));
+process.env.CB_HOME = HOME;
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29563;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const SEP = String.fromCharCode(92);
+const chrome = [
+  'C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+  (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '').split(SEP).join('/') + '/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+  'C:/Program Files (x86)/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe',
+].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+
+const chunk = (s) => ({ id: 'c1', choices: [{ delta: { content: s } }] });
+
+const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-repaint-'));
+const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8'))
+  .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+let calls = 0;
+const adapter = {
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async *completeStream(req) {
+    const full = String(req.messages[req.messages.length - 1].content);
+    const tag = (full.match(/--- you (\S+) ---\s*$/) || [])[1] || 'x';
+    const close = String.fromCharCode(10) + '/--- you ' + tag + ' ---';
+    calls++;
+    if (calls === 1) { yield chunk('Running the slow one now. <remote-workspace><js>return (await node(["-e", "setTimeout(() => console.log(42), 4000)"])).stdout;</js></remote-workspace>'); return; }
+    yield chunk('It printed 42.' + close);
+  },
+};
+const { server } = await startServer({
+  html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+  defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir,
+});
+const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+try {
+  const page = await browser.newPage();
+  await page.setViewport({ width: 1200, height: 900 });
+  await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+  await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 20000 });
+  await page.waitForFunction(() => { const m = document.getElementById('model'); return m && m.value && !/^\(/.test(m.value); }, { timeout: 20000 });
+  // count the repaints: an event-patch re-renders the chat
+  await page.evaluate(() => { window.__clears = 0; const log = document.getElementById('log'); new MutationObserver((ms) => { for (const m of ms) if (m.removedNodes.length > 2) window.__clears++; }).observe(log, { childList: true }); });
+  await page.type('#input', 'run the slow one');
+  await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+  await page.waitForFunction(() => /Running the slow one/.test(document.getElementById('log').innerText), { timeout: 20000 });
+  const seen = [];
+  const t0 = Date.now();
+  while (Date.now() - t0 < 15000) {
+    const s = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      const st = [...document.querySelectorAll('#log > .status')].filter((e) => e.checkVisibility());
+      return { running: (window.__sessionRef || {}).status === 'running', line: st.map((e) => e.textContent.trim()).join(' | '), done: /It printed 42/.test(document.getElementById('log').innerText), clears: window.__clears };
+    });
+    seen.push(s);
+    if (s.done && !s.running) break;
+    await sleep(250);
+  }
+  const during = seen.filter((s) => s.running && !s.done);
+  const missing = during.filter((s) => !/working/i.test(s.line));
+  ok(during.length >= 8, `the command ran for a while with the run going (${during.length} looks)`);
+  // the raw-log ids stamped after each model call no longer redraw the chat (live, 2026-10-24: each redrew 2,500 events
+  // and froze the page 1.4-1.6 s) - the redraw that took the working line with it does not happen for them at all
+  ok(seen.every((s) => s.clears === 0), `the chat was not redrawn for the raw-log ids (${Math.max(...seen.map((s) => s.clears))} redraws)`);
+  ok(missing.length === 0, `"Agent working…" was on screen at every look while the run went on (${missing.length} of ${during.length} without it)`);
+  ok(seen.some((s) => s.done && !s.running), 'and the run finished');
+} finally {
+  await browser.close();
+  server.close();
+  await rm(projectDir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  await rm(HOME, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+}
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

+/**
+ * A message typed while the agent works, then Esc (the developer, 2026-10-12):
+ *   "I submitted a message while the agent was working and it was added to the conversation grayed out. Then I hit
+ *    esc to interrupt thinking the message would be processed, but it didn't. Then I submitted the message again and

+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-steeresc-home-'));
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };

+const sent = [];   // every request's messages
+let n = 0;
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream(req) {
+    sent.push((req?.messages || []).map((m) => ({ role: m.role, content: typeof m.content === 'string' ? m.content : JSON.stringify(m.content) })));
+    const t = n++ === 0 ? SLOW : 'Got it.';
+    for (const p of (t.match(/[\s\S]{1,40}/g) || [''])) { await sleep(2); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: p } }] }; }
+  },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+};

+async function main() {

+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8')).replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');

+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    const errs = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1200, height: 900 });
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+    await page.type('#input', 'fix the header');

+    ok(await page.$('#log .pending-steer') != null, 'the message shows, muted');
+
+    console.log('\n[B] Esc: the waiting message is not lost, and is not left waiting on a run that has stopped');

+    await page.keyboard.press('Escape');

+    await sleep(1500);
+    const afterEsc = await page.evaluate((s) => ({
+      pending: document.querySelectorAll('#log .pending-steer').length,
+      bubbles: [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .bubble.user')].filter((b) => (b.textContent || '').includes(s)).length,
+      box: document.getElementById('input').value,
+      status: window.__sessionRef?.status,
+    }), STEER);
+    console.log('    (after Esc: ' + JSON.stringify(afterEsc) + ')');

+    ok(afterEsc.pending === 0, 'no muted bubble is left waiting');
+    ok(askedAfter, 'the message went to the agent');
+    ok(afterEsc.box === 'half a thought', `what was half-typed in the box stays there (${JSON.stringify(afterEsc.box)})`);
+
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__sessionRef?.status === 'done' || window.__sessionRef?.status === 'idle', { timeout: 20000 }).catch(() => {});
+    await sleep(500);
+    const last = sent[sent.length - 1] || [];
+    const copies = last.filter((x) => x.role === 'user' && x.content.includes(STEER)).length;
+    ok(copies === 1, `the agent was sent ONE copy of it (${copies})`);
+    const shown = await page.evaluate((s) => [...document.querySelectorAll('#log .bubble.user')].filter((b) => (b.textContent || '').includes(s)).length, STEER);
+    ok(shown >= 1, `and it shows in the conversation (${shown})`);

+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}

=== add test/test-step-drop-retry.mjs
new 1fd40ab56f9bec11fd4f5333
+/**
+ * A STEP WHOSE CALL DROPPED IS TAKEN AGAIN (live, 2026-10-30: "Agent error: fetch failed" ended a run on prod - the
+ * developer: "Why can't we retry?"). Through the real server, with a stand-in model:
+ *   [1] the call fails before any reply ("fetch failed"): the step is taken again, the run ends well, a muted note says so
+ *   [2] the call drops mid-reply, text already shown: that text is withdrawn, the step taken again, one reply stands
+ *   [3] it keeps dropping: two retakes, then the run fails with the error, as before
+ *   [4] an answer is not retried: an HTTP 400 ends the run at once
+ * Run: node test/test-step-drop-retry.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-dropretry-home-'));
+process.env.CB_STEP_DROP_WAITS_MS = '200,400';
+process.env.CB_HEADS_UP_IDLE_MS = '0';
+process.env.CB_SKILLS_MAINTAINER ??= '0';
+process.env.CB_SESSION_TRANSPORT = '0';
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const PORT = 29701 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 20);
+const B = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+const C = '/--- you ab12 ---';
+
+// each test sets the plan: a list of what the next calls do
+let plan = [], calls = 0;
+const dropErr = () => { const e = new TypeError('fetch failed'); e.cause = { code: 'ECONNRESET' }; return e; };
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream() {
+    calls++;
+    const step = plan.shift() || 'ok';
+    if (step === 'drop') throw dropErr();
+    if (step === 'mid') { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'HALF-WRITTEN reply that was cut ' } }] }; await sleep(50); throw dropErr(); }
+    if (step === '400') { const e = new Error('HTTP 400: bad request (test)'); e.status = 400; throw e; }
+    yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'THE-WHOLE-REPLY.\n' + C } }] };
+  },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: '' } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+const proj = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-dropretry-proj-'));
+const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: proj });
+const state = () => fetch(B + '/api/state?limit=400').then((r) => r.json());
+const run = async (text) => {
+  await fetch(B + '/api/message', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text, model: 'm' }) });
+  for (let i = 0; i < 40 && (await state()).status !== 'running'; i++) await sleep(50);
+  for (let i = 0; i < 200 && (await state()).status === 'running'; i++) await sleep(100);
+  return state();
+};
+const since = (s, marker) => { const evs = s.events || []; const i = evs.findLastIndex((e) => e.type === 'user-message' && String(e.content).includes(marker)); return evs.slice(i); };
+
+try {
+  await sleep(800);
+  console.log('\n[1] the call fails before any reply');
+  plan = ['drop']; calls = 0;
+  let s = await run('first FIRST-ASK');
+  let evs = since(s, 'FIRST-ASK');
+  ok(calls === 2 && s.status !== 'error' && evs.some((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text' && /THE-WHOLE-REPLY/.test(e.content)), `taken again, and the run ended well (${calls} calls, ${s.status})`);
+  ok(evs.some((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && e.verb === 'retry' && /dropped/.test(e.args?.command || '')) && !evs.some((e) => e.type === 'run-interrupted'), 'a muted note says the call dropped and was taken again; no error');
+
+  console.log('\n[2] it drops mid-reply, text already shown');
+  plan = ['mid']; calls = 0;
+  s = await run('second SECOND-ASK');
+  evs = since(s, 'SECOND-ASK');
+  const shown = evs.filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text' && !e.retracted && !e.hidden);
+  ok(calls === 2 && shown.length === 1 && shown.every((e) => !/HALF-WRITTEN/.test(e.content)) && shown.some((e) => /THE-WHOLE-REPLY/.test(e.content)), `the half reply is not kept, one whole reply stands (${calls} calls, ${shown.length} reply)`);
+
+  console.log('\n[3] it keeps dropping');
+  plan = ['drop', 'drop', 'drop']; calls = 0;
+  s = await run('third THIRD-ASK');
+  evs = since(s, 'THIRD-ASK');
+  ok(calls === 3 && evs.some((e) => e.type === 'run-interrupted' && /fetch failed/.test(e.message || e.content || '')), `two retakes, then the run fails with the error (${calls} calls)`);
+
+  console.log('\n[4] an answer is not retried');
+  plan = ['400']; calls = 0;
+  s = await run('fourth FOURTH-ASK');
+  evs = since(s, 'FOURTH-ASK');
+  ok(calls === 1 && evs.some((e) => e.type === 'run-interrupted'), `an HTTP 400 ends the run at once (${calls} call)`);
+} catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+finally { server.close(); }
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

=== add test/test-stop-probe.mjs
new 20e01da4fb672bf2a8309cb0
+/**
+ * SNAPSHOT #73 — "do stop sequences work on this endpoint?"
+ *
+ * The probe exists to answer one production question with four live calls, and the answer decides whether the
+ * turn-closing delimiter can be ENFORCED or only requested. So the thing under test is the VERDICT logic: a
+ * probe that reports "honored" against an endpoint that ignores the parameter would send us to implement a
+ * tripwire that does nothing, and a probe that reports "ignored" because our own client dropped the key would
+ * send us to abandon a fix that works.
+ *
+ * Runs entirely against mock adapters — no network. The last case boots the real server and pulls #73 through
+ * /api/queries, because the tile reads `adapter` and `resolvedApiFormat` out of startServer's scope and a
+ * syntax check cannot tell whether those names are actually visible there.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-stop-probe.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-stopprobe-home-'));
+const { runStopProbe, formatStopProbe, stopProbeState, resetStopProbe } = await import('../src/agent/stop-probe.mjs');
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const PORT = 29271;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+const FULL = 'ALPHA\nBRAVO\nCHARLIE\nDELTA\nECHO';
+const DELIM = '/--- you zzzz ---';
+const reply = (content, finish = 'stop') => ({ choices: [{ message: { content }, finish_reason: finish }] });
+
+/** An endpoint that really truncates: cut the text at the first stop string, as a compliant server does. */
+function honoringAdapter({ readsKey = 'stop', matchDelimiter = true, echoStop = false } = {}) {
+  return {
+    async complete(req) {
+      const seqs = req[readsKey];
+      const body = String(req.messages[0].content).includes(DELIM) ? `ALPHA\n${DELIM}\nOMEGA` : FULL;
+      if (!Array.isArray(seqs) || !seqs.length) return reply(body);
+      for (const s of seqs) {
+        if (s === DELIM && !matchDelimiter) continue;      // token-boundary matcher that misses punctuation
+        const i = body.indexOf(s);
+        if (i >= 0) return reply(body.slice(0, echoStop ? i + s.length : i), 'stop_sequence');
+      }
+      return reply(body);
+    },
+  };
+}
+/** An endpoint that accepts the parameter and generates straight through it. */
+const ignoringAdapter = { async complete(req) { return reply(String(req.messages[0].content).includes(DELIM) ? `ALPHA\n${DELIM}\nOMEGA` : FULL); } };
+
+async function main() {
+  console.log('\n[1] an endpoint that truncates reads as HONORED');
+  {
+    const r = await runStopProbe({ adapter: honoringAdapter(), model: 'm', apiFormat: 'openai' });
+    const f = formatStopProbe({ status: 'done', model: 'm', apiFormat: 'openai', rows: r.rows });
+    ok(/^HONORED via stop\b/.test(f.body), 'verdict names the parameter that worked');
+    ok(f.severity === 'ok', 'and is not flagged as a problem (' + f.severity + ')');
+    ok(r.rows.find((x) => x.key === 'control').verdict === 'ok', 'the control established a baseline');
+    ok(r.rows.find((x) => x.key === 'delimiter').verdict === 'honored', 'the real delimiter shape truncated too');
+    ok(/stop string excluded/.test(f.body), 'and reports that the stop string was cut, not echoed');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[2] an endpoint that ignores it reads as IGNORED — the whole point of the probe');
+  {
+    const r = await runStopProbe({ adapter: ignoringAdapter, model: 'm', apiFormat: 'openai' });
+    const f = formatStopProbe({ status: 'done', model: 'm', apiFormat: 'openai', rows: r.rows });
+    ok(/^IGNORED\b/.test(f.body), 'verdict is IGNORED');
+    ok(/prompt-shape fix only/.test(f.body), 'and says what that means for the delimiter plan');
+    ok(r.rows.filter((x) => x.verdict === 'ignored').length === 3, 'every stop variant is marked ignored');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[3] the parameter NAME is reported, not assumed');
+  {
+    const r = await runStopProbe({ adapter: honoringAdapter({ readsKey: 'stop_sequences' }), model: 'm', apiFormat: 'openai' });
+    const f = formatStopProbe({ status: 'done', model: 'm', apiFormat: 'openai', rows: r.rows });
+    ok(/HONORED via stop_sequences/.test(f.body), 'an endpoint reading the Anthropic name is identified as such');
+    ok(r.rows.find((x) => x.key === 'stop').verdict === 'ignored', 'and the OpenAI name is correctly shown as a miss');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[4] a plain word working does NOT mean the real delimiter works');
+  {
+    // The trap this probe exists to avoid: an endpoint that matches on token boundaries honors CHARLIE and
+    // silently misses "/--- you zzzz ---". Reporting that as HONORED would send us to build a dead tripwire.
+    const r = await runStopProbe({ adapter: honoringAdapter({ matchDelimiter: false }), model: 'm', apiFormat: 'openai' });
+    const f = formatStopProbe({ status: 'done', model: 'm', apiFormat: 'openai', rows: r.rows });
+    ok(/^PARTIAL\b/.test(f.body), 'verdict is PARTIAL, not HONORED');
+    ok(f.body.includes(DELIM), 'and names the delimiter that failed');
+    ok(f.severity === 'warn', 'flagged as a problem (' + f.severity + ')');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[5] an echoed stop string is called out (the implementation has to strip it)');
+  {
+    const r = await runStopProbe({ adapter: honoringAdapter({ echoStop: true }), model: 'm', apiFormat: 'openai' });
+    const f = formatStopProbe({ status: 'done', model: 'm', apiFormat: 'openai', rows: r.rows });
+    ok(/stop string IS echoed/.test(f.body), 'the echo is reported rather than passed over');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[6] a model that will not follow the prompt yields INCONCLUSIVE, never a false negative');
+  {
+    const lazy = { async complete() { return reply('ALPHA'); } };
+    const r = await runStopProbe({ adapter: lazy, model: 'm', apiFormat: 'openai' });
+    const f = formatStopProbe({ status: 'done', model: 'm', apiFormat: 'openai', rows: r.rows });
+    ok(/^INCONCLUSIVE\b/.test(f.body), 'verdict is INCONCLUSIVE');
+    ok(!/^IGNORED/.test(f.body), 'and NOT reported as the endpoint ignoring the parameter');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[7] our own client dropping the key is not blamed on the endpoint');
+  {
+    // toAnthropicBody() builds an explicit body and drops unrecognised keys, stop_sequences included. Without
+    // this warning the probe would report IGNORED against a perfectly compliant endpoint.
+    const r = await runStopProbe({ adapter: ignoringAdapter, model: 'm', apiFormat: 'anthropic' });
+    const f = formatStopProbe({ status: 'done', model: 'm', apiFormat: 'anthropic', rows: r.rows });
+    ok(/ANTHROPIC adapter/.test(f.body), 'the anthropic adapter caveat is attached');
+    ok(/is the client, not the endpoint/.test(f.body), 'and states plainly that a negative here proves nothing');
+    const g = formatStopProbe({ status: 'done', model: 'm', apiFormat: 'openai', rows: r.rows });
+    ok(!/ANTHROPIC adapter/.test(g.body), 'and is absent on the openai path');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[8] states before the result exists render without throwing');
+  {
+    ok(/not run yet/.test(formatStopProbe({ status: 'idle' }).body), 'idle explains how to start it');
+    // A still frame with a rung count: the page renders once per Run, so an elapsed number that never
+    // ticks cannot tell anyone whether to wait or re-run.
+    const run = formatStopProbe({ status: 'running', startedAt: Date.now() - 3000, model: 'm', rows: [{}, {}] });
+    ok(/2 of 4 calls done/.test(run.body), 'it reports how many rungs have landed');
+    ok(/Re-run the query/.test(run.body), 'and says to re-run rather than wait for it to update');
+    ok(/probe failed: boom/.test(formatStopProbe({ status: 'done', error: 'boom' }).body), 'an error surfaces as an error');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[9] #73 is reachable through the real server, with the real adapter in scope');
+  {
+    resetStopProbe();
+    const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-stopprobe-'));
+    const { server } = await startServer({
+      html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+      defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter: honoringAdapter(), modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir,
+    });
+    try {
+      const one = async () => (await (await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/api/queries?ids=73`)).json()).tiles?.[0] ?? {};
+      // ONE run, not two. The tile waits for its own probe now - the runner is async, so "start it, then run
+      // the query again" is no longer the only way to surface a result, and that two-step read as the tile
+      // being broken.
+      const first = await one();
+      ok(first.id === 73, 'the catalog entry exists and renders (' + JSON.stringify(first.name) + ')');
+      ok(!/could not start/.test(first.body || ''), 'starting it did not throw — adapter + apiFormat are in scope');
+      ok(/^HONORED via stop\b/.test(first.body || ''), 'and the FIRST run returns the verdict, not a "come back later"');
+      ok(first.severity === 'ok', 'with the severity the tile colours from (' + first.severity + ')');
+      // Re-running is still safe and still shows the same answer, which is what a reader will do anyway.
+      const again = await one();
+      ok(/^HONORED via stop\b/.test(again.body || ''), 're-running shows the same verdict rather than starting over');
+      ok(stopProbeState().status === 'done', 'and the module state settled');
+    } finally { server.close(); await rm(projectDir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); }
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n' + pass + ' passed, ' + fail + ' failed');
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}

=== add test/test-stream-early-cut.mjs
new 5f576c6cd474efe209588958
+/**
+ * STOP READING WHEN THE TURN IS OVER.
+ *
+ * The parser already discards everything from the moment the model starts writing the developer's turn — but by
+ * then it has been generated, and we waited for it. Measured A/B against the live model: 96% of every character
+ * generated was discarded, and one call spent 189 seconds producing 50,830 characters of which 205 were real.
+ *
+ * Closing delimiters cut how OFTEN that happens (67% of calls to 41%); they do nothing about how MUCH, because
+ * once it starts it runs to the end — the worst call was 50,625 discarded before, 52,667 after.
+ *
+ * So cut the stream instead. This needs no cooperation from the endpoint, which matters because a stop sequence
+ * does and production may not offer one. We own the reader.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-stream-early-cut.mjs
+ */
+import { runTurnStreaming, streamDiagSnapshot, streamDiagClear } from '../src/agent/turn-stream.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+/**
+ * Streams `head`, then `tail`, in small pieces — and RECORDS how much of the tail it was asked for. A real
+ * endpoint stops producing when the request is aborted; this measures whether we stopped asking.
+ */
+function scriptedAdapter(head, tail, state) {
+  return {
+    async *completeStream() {
+      for (let i = 0; i < head.length; i += 20) { state.sent += Math.min(20, head.length - i); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: head.slice(i, i + 20) } }] }; await sleep(2); }
+      for (let i = 0; i < tail.length; i += 20) { state.sent += Math.min(20, tail.length - i); state.tailSent += Math.min(20, tail.length - i); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: tail.slice(i, i + 20) } }] }; await sleep(2); }
+    },
+    async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: head } }] }; },
+  };
+}
+
+const HEAD = 'Here is the real answer to your question, which is short and complete.\n';
+// What the model writes when it carries on: the developer's turn, invented, at length.
+const FORGED = '\n--- developer hi6u ---\n<remote-workspace-result op="read" status="ok">\n'
+  + 'invented file contents that nobody asked for. '.repeat(200) + '\n</remote-workspace-result>\n';
+
+console.log('\n[1] the stream is cut at a forged developer heading');
+{
+  streamDiagClear();
+  const state = { sent: 0, tailSent: 0 };
+  const text = await runTurnStreaming(scriptedAdapter(HEAD, FORGED, state), { model: 'm', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'q' }] }, {});
+  ok(/real answer/.test(text), 'the real answer is kept');
+  ok(!/invented file contents/.test(text), 'the forged continuation is not in the returned text');
+  // THE point: not merely discarded afterwards — never asked for.
+  ok(state.tailSent < FORGED.length / 4,
+    `we stopped reading almost immediately (${state.tailSent} of ${FORGED.length} forged chars pulled)`);
+}
+
+console.log('\n[2] and the overrun is recorded, so a cut that is not working would be visible');
+{
+  const rec = streamDiagSnapshot().at(-1);
+  ok(rec && typeof rec.cutOverrun === 'number', 'the turn record carries cutOverrun');
+  ok(rec.cutOverrun > 0 && rec.cutOverrun < 500, `small overrun (${rec.cutOverrun} chars past the boundary)`);
+}
+
+console.log('\n[3] a turn that CLOSES itself is cut there too');
+{
+  streamDiagClear();
+  const state = { sent: 0, tailSent: 0 };

+  ok(!/kept writing/.test(text), 'and nothing past its own closing delimiter');
+  ok(state.tailSent < tail.length / 4, `stopped reading early (${state.tailSent} of ${tail.length})`);
+}
+

+  streamDiagClear();

+  const whole = 'A perfectly normal answer. It mentions a developer, and a workspace, and even a --- separator line.\n';
+  const more = 'It goes on for a while and finishes properly. '.repeat(50);

+  ok(/finishes properly/.test(text), 'the whole reply arrives');
+  ok(state.tailSent === more.length, `every chunk was read (${state.tailSent} of ${more.length})`);
+  ok((streamDiagSnapshot().at(-1) || {}).cutOverrun === 0, 'and nothing was recorded as cut');
+}

+console.log('\n[5] a tool block is NOT a boundary — the reply continues past it');
+{
+  // <remote-workspace> is ours and legitimate; only a forged RESULT is the model answering itself.

+  const tail = 'and here is what I plan to do next, at some length. '.repeat(40);

+  ok(/plan to do next/.test(text), 'prose after a real tool block survives');
+  ok(state.tailSent === tail.length, 'the stream was read to the end');
+}
