=== add test/test-stream-end-reason.mjs
new 792f88c219ab9e62521a873d
+/**
+ * HOW A STREAM ENDED, which the raw log used to get wrong.
+ *
+ * The streaming adapter emits its transaction from a `finally`, so a call is recorded no matter how it exits —
+ * and it recorded finish_reason:'stop' for all of them. A stream the consumer cut short (which is exactly what
+ * the turn-boundary cut added in build 1700 does), and one that died on a network error, both came back looking
+ * like a reply the model chose to end. That is the same shape as a run killed by the app closing coming back
+ * looking finished: the log says everything is fine, so nothing downstream can ask whether it was.
+ *
+ * It matters right now because the reported repeating is claimed to follow a cancellation, and the raw log is
+ * where that claim gets tested. A log that cannot see cancellations can only ever answer "no".
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-stream-end-reason.mjs
+ */
+import { createHttpAdapter } from '../src/llm/openai-http.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+const sse = (lines) => new Response(lines.map((l) => `data: ${l}\n\n`).join(''), {
+  status: 200, headers: { 'content-type': 'text/event-stream' },
+});
+const delta = (s) => JSON.stringify({ id: 'c1', choices: [{ delta: { content: s } }] });
+
+/** Collects the transactions a run of `body` produces. */
+async function txOf(fetchImpl, consume) {
+  const txs = [];
+  const a = createHttpAdapter({ baseURL: 'http://x/v1', fetchImpl, onTransaction: (t) => txs.push(t) });
+  try { await consume(a.completeStream({ messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'hi' }], model: 'm' })); } catch { /* the point is the log */ }
+  return txs;
+}
+const drain = async (it) => { for await (const _ of it) { /* to the end */ } };
+
+async function main() {
+  console.log('\n[1] a stream that runs to [DONE] is a clean finish');
+  {
+    const txs = await txOf(async () => sse([delta('hello'), delta(' world'), '[DONE]']), drain);
+    ok(txs.length === 1, `one transaction (${txs.length})`);
+    ok(txs[0].response.choices[0].finish_reason === 'stop', 'finish_reason stop');
+    ok(!txs[0].endedEarly, 'not marked as ended early');
+    ok(txs[0].response.choices[0].message.content === 'hello world', 'and the text is what arrived');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[2] a stream whose reader simply runs out is also clean');
+  {
+    // The other natural ending — no [DONE] sentinel, the body just finishes. Marking this one early would flag
+    // every ordinary call on an endpoint that omits the sentinel.
+    const txs = await txOf(async () => sse([delta('hi')]), drain);
+    ok(!txs[0].endedEarly, 'not marked as ended early');
+    ok(txs[0].response.choices[0].finish_reason === 'stop', 'finish_reason stop');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[3] a consumer that breaks out mid-stream is NOT a clean finish');
+  {
+    // What the turn-boundary cut does. Before this change it was indistinguishable from [1].
+    const txs = await txOf(
+      async () => sse([delta('a'), delta('b'), delta('c'), delta('d'), '[DONE]']),
+      async (it) => { let n = 0; for await (const _ of it) { if (++n >= 2) break; } },
+    );
+    ok(txs.length === 1, `still recorded (${txs.length})`);
+    ok(txs[0].endedEarly === true, 'marked as ended early');
+    ok(txs[0].endedBecause === 'consumer-stopped', `and why (${txs[0].endedBecause})`);
+    ok(txs[0].response.choices[0].finish_reason === null, 'finish_reason is null, not an invented "stop"');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[4] a stream that throws mid-flight reports the failure, by NAME only');
+  {
+    const txs = await txOf(async () => new Response(
+      new ReadableStream({ start(c) { c.enqueue(new TextEncoder().encode(`data: ${delta('a')}\n\n`)); c.error(Object.assign(new Error('secret-bearing detail'), { name: 'NetworkError' })); } }),
+      { status: 200, headers: { 'content-type': 'text/event-stream' } },
+    ), drain);
+    ok(txs[0] && txs[0].endedEarly === true, 'marked as ended early');
+    ok(txs[0].endedBecause === 'NetworkError', `named by error name (${txs[0].endedBecause})`);
+    // The message can quote the payload back, and this lands in a file that gets screenshotted.
+    ok(!/secret-bearing/.test(JSON.stringify(txs[0])), 'and the error MESSAGE is not carried into the log');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[5] a finish_reason the endpoint actually sent is never overwritten');
+  {
+    // "length" is the one worth keeping: it says the reply was cut off by a token ceiling, which is a different
+    // fault from either a clean stop or our own cut.
+    const txs = await txOf(async () => sse([
+      delta('a'), JSON.stringify({ id: 'c1', choices: [{ delta: {}, finish_reason: 'length' }] }), '[DONE]',
+    ]), drain);
+    ok(txs[0].response.choices[0].finish_reason === 'length', 'the endpoint\'s own reason survives');
+    ok(!txs[0].endedEarly, 'and the stream still ended naturally');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[6] a non-streamed answer to stream:true is still labelled honestly');
+  {
+    // Some gateways ignore stream:true. That path reports the real payload and marks itself streamed:false — the
+    // only way a reader can tell that no client-side cut was possible on that call.
+    const txs = await txOf(async () => new Response(
+      JSON.stringify({ id: 'x', choices: [{ message: { content: 'whole thing' }, finish_reason: 'stop' }] }),
+      { status: 200, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } },
+    ), drain);
+    ok(txs.length === 1 && txs[0].streamed === false, `recorded as not streamed (${txs.length}, ${txs[0]?.streamed})`);
+    ok(!txs[0].endedEarly, 'and not mislabelled as cut short');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[7] the shape of the stream rides on the transaction');
+  {
+    // Two completion ids and two role deltas in ONE stream is what a gateway gluing two model responses
+    // together looks like, and nothing in the text can show it. Counts only.
+    const chunk = (id, delta, extra = {}) => JSON.stringify({ id, choices: [{ delta, ...extra }] });
+    const txs = await txOf(async () => sse([
+      chunk('c1', { role: 'assistant' }), chunk('c1', { content: 'I will do it.' }), chunk('c1', {}, { finish_reason: 'stop' }),
+      chunk('c2', { role: 'assistant' }), chunk('c2', { content: 'I will do it now.' }), chunk('c2', {}, { finish_reason: 'stop' }),
+      '[DONE]',
+    ]), drain);
+    const sh = txs[0].streamShape;
+    ok(sh && sh.ids === 2, `two completion ids counted (${sh && sh.ids})`);
+    ok(sh && sh.roles === 2, `two role deltas counted (${sh && sh.roles})`);
+    ok(sh && sh.finishes === 2, `two finishes counted (${sh && sh.finishes})`);
+    ok(txs[0].response.choices[0].message.content === 'I will do it.I will do it now.', 'and the text is the concatenation the developer would have seen');
+    ok(!JSON.stringify(sh).includes('I will'), 'the shape carries no text');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[8] a stall inside the stream is placed at the character it resumed on');
+  {
+    // The threshold is #74's, 1.5s. Real time, so this test costs about two seconds - worth it, because a
+    // fake clock would only prove the arithmetic, and the question is whether the read loop sees the gap.
+    const enc = new TextEncoder();
+    const txs = await txOf(async () => new Response(new ReadableStream({
+      async start(c) {
+        // A slow FIRST byte is the time to first byte, not a stall - counted as one it put a "stall" on every
+        // clean reply and hid the finding that clean replies have none mid-stream.
+        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1600));
+        c.enqueue(enc.encode(`data: ${delta('first paragraph.')}` + String.fromCharCode(10, 10)));
+        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1700));
+        c.enqueue(enc.encode(`data: ${delta('second paragraph.')}` + String.fromCharCode(10, 10)));
+        c.enqueue(enc.encode('data: [DONE]' + String.fromCharCode(10, 10)));
+        c.close();
+      },
+    }), { status: 200, headers: { 'content-type': 'text/event-stream' } }), drain);
+    const g = txs[0].streamShape.gaps;
+    ok(g.length === 1, `one stall recorded (${g.length})`);
+    ok(g[0] && g[0].at === 'first paragraph.'.length, `at the offset the stream resumed on (${g[0] && g[0].at})`);
+    ok(g[0] && g[0].ms >= 1500, `with its length (${g[0] && g[0].ms}ms)`);
+    ok(txs[0].streamShape.ttfbMs >= 1500, `the first-byte wait is recorded on its own (${txs[0].streamShape.ttfbMs}ms), not as a stall`);
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[9] keepalives, a per-chunk trace, and the identifying headers - none of it text');
+  {
+    const N2 = String.fromCharCode(10, 10);
+    const body = 'data: ' + delta('first ') + N2 + ': keepalive' + N2 + 'data: ' + delta('second') + N2 + 'data: [DONE]' + N2;
+    const txs = await txOf(async () => new Response(body, { status: 200, headers: { 'content-type': 'text/event-stream', 'x-request-id': 'req-123', 'server': 'envoy', 'authorization': 'Bearer NOPE', 'set-cookie': 'session=NOPE' } }), drain);
+    const sh = txs[0].streamShape;
+    ok(sh.comments === 1, `the SSE comment line is counted (${sh.comments})`);
+    ok(Array.isArray(sh.trace) && sh.trace.filter((e) => e[2] === 'c').length === 1, 'and placed in the trace');
+    ok(sh.trace.filter((e) => e[2] !== 'c').length === 2 && sh.trace.every((e) => typeof e[0] === 'number'), `the content chunks are traced as [ms, chars] (${JSON.stringify(sh.trace)})`);
+    ok(!JSON.stringify(sh.trace).includes('first'), 'the trace carries no text');
+    ok(sh.headers['x-request-id'] === 'req-123' && sh.headers.server === 'envoy', `identifying headers are kept (${JSON.stringify(sh.headers)})`);
+    ok(!('authorization' in sh.headers) && !('set-cookie' in sh.headers), 'and nothing that could carry a credential');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[10] only a real AbortSignal reaches fetch');
+  {
+    // A request that has been through JSON carries signal: {} - and fetch refuses that with a message that reads
+    // as the endpoint being unreachable. Seen on the first production run of #78.
+    const inits = [];

+/**
+ * THE HISTORY HELPER over the stream (src/agent/stream-history.mjs): historyAsk("question") - the bookkeeper's faded
+ * view of the record, items asked for by id and given whole, an answer with where it was found.
+ *   [1] the record it reads: the faded view, a line naming the ids not shown, no "new since the last pass" section
+ *   [2] it asks for items by id (ranges opened), gets them whole, and answers
+ *   [3] out of reads, or no answer: it says it could not; a plain reply is the answer
+ * Run: node test/test-stream-history.mjs
+ */
+const s = await import('../src/agent/stream.mjs');
+const sh = await import('../src/agent/stream-history.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+let n = 0;
+const ev = (type, x = {}) => ({ id: `e${++n}`, type, ...x });
+const dev = (text) => ev('user-message', { content: text, modelContent: text });
+const reply = (text) => ev('assistant-text', { content: text });
+const step = (out, cid) => [ev('tool-call', { callId: cid, verb: 'js', args: { command: 'js' } }), ev('tool-result', { callId: cid, verb: 'js', status: 'ok', content: out })];
+const flat = (m) => m.map((x) => String(x.content || '')).join('\n');
+
+const QUERY = "SELECT family_id FROM enrollees WHERE plan = 'HMO' AND family_size > 3";
+const events = [dev('find me a test family on the HMO plan'), ...step(`used: ${QUERY}\nfamily 4471`, 'c1'), reply('Family 4471 fits.'), dev('make the header blue'), reply('Done.'), dev('now something else')];
+const st = s.newStreamMarks();
+st.bkFaded = { e2: 'faded', e4: 'faded' };
+
+console.log('\n[1]-[2] the record, a read by id, the answer');
+{
+  const sent = [];
+  const replies = ['I need the faded step. <read ids="e2-e4"/>', '<answer>The query was ' + QUERY + ' (e2).</answer>'];
+  const r = await sh.runHistoryAsk({ st, events, question: 'what query found the HMO family?', call: async (m) => { sent.push(m); return replies[sent.length - 1]; } });
+  const first = flat(sent[0]);
+  ok(/THE RECORD OF THE SESSION/.test(first) && /\.\.\. 2 items not shown here: e2 to e4 \.\.\./.test(first) && /\[e1\] developer\nfind me a test family/.test(first) && !/NEW SINCE THE LAST PASS/.test(first), 'the faded view, the ids not shown named, no pass section');
+  ok(/THE AGENT ASKS: what query found the HMO family\?/.test(first) && /--- code_boss [0-9a-z]+ ---\nTHE AGENT ASKS/.test(first) && /--- you [0-9a-z]+ ---\s*$/.test(first), 'the question is code_boss\'s turn, last, the reply opened');
+  ok(r.read.join() === 'e2,e4' && new RegExp(QUERY.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&')).test(flat(sent[1])), `e2-e4 opened to the items in it (${r.read}), given whole`);
+  ok(r.rounds === 2 && /The query was SELECT family_id/.test(r.answer) && /\(e2\)/.test(r.answer), 'the answer, with where it came from');
+  ok(sh.historyReadIds('e1, e5-e3, e99, nonsense', s.streamUnits(events)).join() === 'e1,e4,e5', 'ids and ranges, either way round; only ids the record has');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[3] out of reads; a plain reply');
+{
+  let k = 0;
+  const r = await sh.runHistoryAsk({ st, events, question: 'q', call: async () => { k++; return '<read ids="e2"/>'; } });
+  ok(k === 4 && /could not answer/.test(r.answer), `reads asked for again and again: it stops, and says it could not (${k} calls)`);
+  const p = await sh.runHistoryAsk({ st, events, question: 'q', call: async () => 'The record does not show it.' });
+  ok(p.answer === 'The record does not show it.' && p.rounds === 1, 'a plain reply is taken as the answer');
+}
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

+// THE MEMORY TAB WITH THE ONE STREAM (ONE-STREAM-SPEC.md "The views"; replaces test-memory-panel-e2e and
+// test-projects-panel-e2e, whose task book, memory book and timelines went in 2576). A workspace whose stream is
+// marked - todos for a request, a rule and a decision (no tasks or groups since 2026-10-27) - opened in a real browser:
+//   1. the Memory tab shows what's open under the request it is for, and the rules and decisions in force
+//   2. a click on a todo's box checks it off, saved to stream-marks.json
+//   3. a rule's scope changed in its select, saved
+//   4. Drop on a decision: no longer in force, saved, and gone from the tab
+//   5. Clear asks "Keep what still stands?"; Escape keeps them - the rule and the open todo are still there
+// Run: node test/test-stream-panel-e2e.mjs
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

+/**
+ * THE BOOKKEEPER'S PASS over the stream (src/agent/stream-pass.mjs; ONE-STREAM-SPEC.md), with a scripted model.
+ *
+ *   [1] the record: the bookkeeper's own faded view of earlier items (ids where some are not shown), the new ones in
+ *       full, bounded to the run the pass is for
+ *   [2] the request: data framed as a record, code_boss's ask last, the views in it, no tasks or groups
+ *   [3] one retry for a reply with no block; one send-back with every problem in it, then what comes back is taken
+ *   [4] the developer's direction and the upgrade: their asks
+ *   [5] earlier work upgraded after later work is marked: the supersede goes on the rule in force
+ *   [6] the request: told what one is; a decision with todos sent back
+ * Run: node test/test-stream-pass.mjs
+ */
+const s = await import('../src/agent/stream.mjs');
+const sp = await import('../src/agent/stream-pass.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+let n = 0;
+const ev = (type, x = {}) => ({ id: `e${++n}`, type, ...x });
+const dev = (text) => ev('user-message', { content: text, modelContent: text });
+const reply = (text) => ev('assistant-text', { content: text });
+const step = (out, cid) => [ev('tool-call', { callId: cid, verb: 'js', args: { path: 'a.js' } }), ev('tool-result', { callId: cid, verb: 'js', status: 'ok', content: out })];
+const flat = (messages) => messages.map((m) => String(m.content || '')).join('\n');
+
+console.log('\n[1] the record');
+{
+  n = 0;

+  const st = s.newStreamMarks();

=== add test/test-stream-restart.mjs
new 4dc554699ed49d08d39224c8
+/**
+ * UPSTREAM SENT THE ANSWER TWICE. Reported from production: ~20s to generate a full response, it renders, then
+ * ~20s of silence and the SAME response appears again — one call, one raw-log entry, the text twice.
+ *
+ * That is not a model restating itself (which streams continuously) and it is not our rendering: the streaming
+ * path reads only delta.content, never message.content, so a final full-copy chunk is ignored. It is whatever
+ * sits between us and the model retrying and CONCATENATING rather than replacing.
+ *
+ * We cannot stop it upstream; we can refuse to show it twice. Both conditions are exact and both are required —
+ * a long silence AND the text after it restarting the text before it. Run: node test/test-stream-restart.mjs
+ */
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { resolveUpstreamRestart, runTurnStreaming } from '../src/agent/turn-stream.mjs';
+
+process.env.CB_STREAM_STALL_MS = '120';   // keep the test fast; the real default is 8s
+process.env.CB_DEDUPE_RESTART = '1';     // the de-duplication is OPT-IN now; this suite is what exercises it
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const ANSWER = 'Setting up the refactored layout and expanding the test data. I will rewrite testData.js to have 3x the data, then update index.html, styles.css and app.js for the new modal flow.';
+
+// Streams `parts`, pausing `stallBefore` ms before the part at that index.
+function stallingAdapter(parts, stallBefore = -1, stallMs = 300) {
+  return { async *completeStream() {
+    for (let i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) {
+      if (i === stallBefore) await sleep(stallMs);
+      yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: parts[i] } }] };
+    }
+  } };
+}
+async function run(adapter) {
+  const restarts = [];
+  const text = await runTurnStreaming(adapter, { model: 'm', messages: [] }, {
+    onProseChunk: () => {}, onProseSegmentEnd: () => {}, onBlockReady: () => {},
+    onStreamRestart: (e) => restarts.push(e),
+  });
+  return { text, restarts };
+}
+
+console.log('\n[1] the detector itself');
+{
+  ok(resolveUpstreamRestart(ANSWER, ANSWER) === 'tail', 'an exact re-delivery is a restart');
+  ok(resolveUpstreamRestart(ANSWER, ANSWER + ' Plus the rest.') === 'tail', 'a LONGER second copy wins — the first was probably cut short');
+  ok(resolveUpstreamRestart(ANSWER + ' Plus the rest.', ANSWER) === 'head', 'and a longer FIRST copy is the one kept');
+  ok(resolveUpstreamRestart(ANSWER, 'A genuinely different continuation with none of the same opening words at all.') === null,
+    'unrelated continuation is not a restart');
+  ok(resolveUpstreamRestart('short', 'short') === null, 'two short strings are a coincidence, not a restart');
+  // Repetition WITHOUT a shared opening must not trip it — that is the model stuttering, a different problem.
+  ok(resolveUpstreamRestart(ANSWER, 'Updating the UI layout and the test data based on your new flow, expanding testData.js first.') === null,
+    'a reworded restatement is NOT this bug and is left alone');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[2] end to end: a stall then a re-delivery shows ONE copy');
+{
+  const { text, restarts } = await run(stallingAdapter([ANSWER, ANSWER], 1));
+  ok(restarts.length === 1, `the restart is detected (${restarts.length})`);
+  ok(text === ANSWER, `the returned text is ONE copy (${text.length} chars vs ${ANSWER.length})`);
+  ok(restarts[0]?.droppedChars === ANSWER.length, `and it says how much it dropped (${restarts[0]?.droppedChars})`);
+  ok(restarts[0]?.gapMs >= 120, `with the gap that gave it away (${restarts[0]?.gapMs}ms)`);
+}
+
+console.log('\n[3] no stall, no interference');
+{
+  // The same doubled text WITHOUT a pause is left alone: a stall is required, so a model that genuinely repeats
+  // itself is a separate problem and not silently truncated here.
+  const { text, restarts } = await run(stallingAdapter([ANSWER, ANSWER], -1));
+  ok(restarts.length === 0, 'nothing is detected without a stall');
+  ok(text === ANSWER + ANSWER, 'and the text is untouched');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[4] a long pause in a NORMAL answer changes nothing');
+{
+  const parts = ['Reading the config now.', ' Then I will report what I find in the parser.'];
+  const { text, restarts } = await run(stallingAdapter(parts, 1));
+  ok(restarts.length === 0, 'a slow chunk is not a restart when the text does not repeat');
+  ok(text === parts.join(''), 'the answer arrives whole');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[5] the in-memory log records what ARRIVED, whether or not the mitigation fired');
+{
+  // The point of this log: every conclusion about the duplication so far has been inference from a description.
+  // This records the primitives — chunks, gaps, and how the text after each gap relates to the text before it —
+  // so the mechanism can be read off rather than assumed. A doubled answer with NO stall is the case the
+  // mitigation deliberately leaves alone, and it must still be VISIBLE.
+  const { streamDiagSnapshot, streamDiagClear, classifyRepeat } = await import('../src/agent/turn-stream.mjs');
+  ok(classifyRepeat(ANSWER, ANSWER) === 'exact', 'an identical re-delivery classifies as exact');
+  ok(classifyRepeat(ANSWER, ANSWER + ' Plus more.') === 'prefix', 'a re-delivery that continued classifies as prefix');
+  ok(classifyRepeat(ANSWER, 'Updating the refactored layout and expanding that test data, rewriting testData.js for the modal flow.') === 'similar',
+    'a REWORDED restatement classifies as similar — a different bug, and the log says which is which');
+  ok(classifyRepeat(ANSWER, 'Now running the test suite to see whether the parser regressed.') === 'new', 'ordinary continuation is new');
+
+  streamDiagClear();
+  await run(stallingAdapter([ANSWER, ANSWER], -1));   // doubled, NO stall → not mitigated, still recorded
+  const recs = streamDiagSnapshot();
+  ok(recs.length === 1, `a turn was recorded (${recs.length})`);
+  ok(recs[0].doubled === true, 'a doubled answer is flagged even with no pause to explain it');
+  ok(recs[0].mitigated === false, 'and it is NOT silently de-duplicated — that needs a stall');
+  ok(recs[0].chunks === 2 && recs[0].chars === ANSWER.length * 2, `with the primitives: ${recs[0].chunks} chunks, ${recs[0].chars} chars`);
+
+  streamDiagClear();
+  await run(stallingAdapter([ANSWER, ANSWER], 1));    // stall + re-delivery → mitigated
+  const r2 = streamDiagSnapshot()[0];
+  ok(r2.gaps.length === 1 && r2.gaps[0].after === 'exact', `the gap is recorded with what followed it (${JSON.stringify(r2.gaps)})`);
+  ok(r2.mitigated === true && r2.droppedChars === ANSWER.length, 'and the mitigation is recorded as having fired');
+  streamDiagClear();
+}
+
+console.log('\n[6] the real production shape: a long stall, then the plan written AGAIN in other words');
+{
+  // Measured on the developer's machine, snapshot #69:
+  //   17:36:36  69 chunks  7,484 chars  152.8s   gaps: 102.1s@1423
+  // A 102-SECOND stall mid-stream, then thousands more characters — and the chat showed the same plan three
+  // times in different wording. So it is NOT a byte-for-byte re-delivery (the theory the de-duplication was
+  // built on, which cannot fire here) and it is NOT the model streaming continuously either.
+  const { streamDiagSnapshot, streamDiagClear, repeatOverlapPct } = await import('../src/agent/turn-stream.mjs');
+  const PLAN_A = 'I will implement the requested features from the spec to complete the mock-up functionality. Geocoding and Radius Constraints: I will add the Patient Zips within X miles checkbox and heavy calculation warning to the search form, plus quick toggle buttons on the results header. Capacity Soft Stop: instead of a hard stop I will intercept the Submit click and show a warning modal allowing Override and Continue.';
+  const PLAN_B = 'I am going to implement the remaining features from the specification: Geocoding and Radius Constraints, the Capacity Soft Stop, and the Live Progress Bar. Geocoding: I will add the Patient Zips within 50 miles checkbox to the search modal form which triggers a warning about calculation times. Capacity Soft Stop: I will change the submit button so it performs a validation check and pops a warning modal.';
+  const NEXT_STEP = 'I will use a js block to securely inject these new HTML modals, styles, and logic into the single index.html file.';
+  const UNRELATED = 'Now running the test suite to check that nothing regressed in the parser after those edits.';
+
+  // The separation is what makes this measurable at all, and it is wide.
+  const restate = repeatOverlapPct(PLAN_A, PLAN_B);
+  const cont = repeatOverlapPct(PLAN_A, NEXT_STEP);
+  const unrel = repeatOverlapPct(PLAN_A, UNRELATED);
+  ok(restate >= 45, `a reworded restatement scores high (${restate}%)`);
+  ok(cont <= 25, `a same-topic CONTINUATION scores low (${cont}%) — the next step is not a restatement`);
+  ok(unrel <= 15, `unrelated text scores near zero (${unrel}%)`);
+  ok(restate - cont >= 25, `and they separate by a wide margin (${restate}% vs ${cont}%), which is what makes a number usable here`);
+
+  streamDiagClear();
+  await run(stallingAdapter([PLAN_A, PLAN_B], 1));
+  const r = streamDiagSnapshot()[0];
+  ok(r.gaps.length === 1, 'the stall is recorded');
+  ok(r.gaps[0].overlapPct >= 45, `with the overlap NUMBER, not just a label (${r.gaps[0].overlapPct}%)`);
+  ok(r.mitigated === false, 'and nothing is de-duplicated — this shape is reported, not acted on');
+}

+console.log('\n[7] the de-duplication is OFF unless asked for');
+{

+  // transform that changes model output on an unconfirmed theory must not run silently.
+  const src = await readFile(new URL('../src/agent/turn-stream.mjs', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
+  ok(/CB_DEDUPE_RESTART/.test(src), 'it is behind a flag');

=== add test/test-stream-server.mjs
new 4903c7037af6d85778841046
+// ONE STREAM THROUGH THE REAL SERVER (stream.mjs, stream-pass.mjs; ONE-STREAM-SPEC.md), against the gateway stand-in:
+//   [1] after a run the bookkeeper's pass marks it: a rule on the developer's message - in .code_boss/stream-marks.json;
+//       a pass call that times out is tried again
+//   [2] the next run's request carries the views; todos left and no hand-over: the agent is called back, once
+//   [3] a pass reads only up to the end of the run it is for, however long it waited
+//   [4] the developer's direction (<bookkeeper/>): the pass starts at once and the agent does NOT wait for it
+//   [5] over the goal, between runs, the lightest items fade
+//   [6] work done with the bookkeeper off goes to the upgrade: its own lane, oldest first, passes not held up by it
+//   [7] recall brings a faded item back
+//   [8] the Memory tab: the views, and the developer's edits to the marks; the goals from Settings
+//   [9] Clear: a break in the one stream
+//   [11] a certification asked for earlier holds its todos: no call-back over them
+// Run: node test/test-stream-server.mjs
+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-stream-home-'));
+process.env.CB_SESSION_TRANSPORT = '0';   // (standard since 2026-10-28; this test's model answers whole requests)
+process.env.CB_TEST_AGENT_FLAGS = '1';    // [6] works a stretch with the bookkeeper off, as a test may
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const { createHttpAdapter } = await import('../src/llm/openai-http.mjs');
+const { startGatewaySim } = await import('./_gateway-sim.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${l}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${l}\n`); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const PLAIN = 'gemini-3.1-pro-preview';
+
+// what the stand-in answers: the bookkeeper's pass by the newest developer message it is given; the agent by its newest turn
+const agentReqs = [], passReqs = [];
+// (the NEWEST such section: on a session the request holds the earlier passes' record turns too)
+const newDevs = (all) => [...all.slice(all.lastIndexOf('=== NEW SINCE THE LAST PASS')).matchAll(/\[(e\d+)\] developer\n([^\n]*)/g)].map((m) => ({ id: m[1], text: m[2] }));
+const script = (msgs) => {
+  const last = msgs[msgs.length - 1].content;
+  const all = msgs.map((m) => String(m.content || '')).join('\n');
+  const tag = (/--- you ([0-9a-z]{4}) ---\s*$/.exec(last) || [])[1] || 'xxxx';
+  const close = `\n/--- you ${tag} ---`;
+  // recall's check: the first of what it found bears on the work
+  if (/Found in the earlier work by the words of these/.test(all)) return '1' + close;
+  // the history helper: it answers, with where it found it
+  if (/THE AGENT ASKS: /.test(all)) { const d = [...all.matchAll(/\[(e\d+)\] developer[^\n]*\nAdd a delete subcommand/g)][0]; return `<answer>The developer asked for a delete subcommand in orders_cli (${d ? d[1] : 'not found'}) - found it.</answer>` + close; }
+  if (/Bring the record up to date for the new items/.test(all)) {
+    passReqs.push(all);
+    const ds = newDevs(all);
+    if (/EARLIER WORK, MARKED FOR THE FIRST TIME/.test(all)) {
+      // (by "question N": the masking filter takes "quick" for a name)
+      const q = ds.find((d) => /question 3$/.test(d.text));
+      const left = /newest of the work from before/.test(all) && ds.find((d) => /question 11$/.test(d.text));
+      return `<stream-ops>${q ? `<mark item="${q.id}" kind="rule" scope="workspace"/><summary item="${q.id}">Old rule three.</summary>` : ''}${left ? `<todo from="${left.id}">answer question 11 properly</todo>` : ''}</stream-ops>` + close;
+    }
+    const d = ds[ds.length - 1] || { id: '', text: '' };
+    if (/From now on use tabs/.test(d.text)) return `<stream-ops><mark item="${d.id}" kind="rule" scope="workspace"/><summary item="${d.id}">Use tabs, not spaces.</summary><developer-not-needed/></stream-ops>` + close;
+    if (/From now on/.test(d.text)) return `<stream-ops><mark item="${d.id}" kind="rule" scope="workspace"/><summary item="${d.id}">Start every commit message with the script name.</summary><developer-not-needed/></stream-ops>` + close;
+    if (/Add a delete/.test(d.text)) return `<stream-ops><mark item="${d.id}" kind="request"/><todo from="${d.id}" state="done">add delete</todo><todo from="${d.id}">test delete on a copy of the CSV</todo><developer-not-needed/></stream-ops>` + close;
+    if (/Propose a fix for the export crash/.test(d.text)) return `<stream-ops><mark item="${d.id}" kind="request"/><todo from="${d.id}">apply the export fix</todo><developer-not-needed/></stream-ops>` + close;
+    if (/Put the delete work on hold/.test(d.text)) return '<stream-ops><todo-set todo="2" state="held"/></stream-ops>' + close;
+    if (/todos you can do now/.test(all.slice(all.lastIndexOf('=== NEW SINCE')))) return '<stream-ops><todo-set todo="2" state="done"/><developer-not-needed/></stream-ops>' + close;
+    return '<stream-ops><developer-not-needed/></stream-ops>' + close;
+  }
+  agentReqs.push(all);
+  // (the newest turn to the agent: what follows the last "--- developer" heading)

+  if (/found it\./.test(now)) return 'You asked for a delete subcommand. <hand-over/>' + close;

+  if (/How many orders are there/.test(now)) return 'There are 522 orders. <no-hand-over/>' + close;
+  if (/Put the delete work on hold/.test(now)) return 'I\'m putting the delete work on hold. <bookkeeper/> <hand-over/>' + close;
+  if (/Remember to always run the linter/.test(now)) return '<bookkeeper/>' + close;
+  if (/the bookkeeper is recording it now - you do not wait/.test(now)) return 'Recording it. Nothing else was asked. <hand-over/>' + close;
+  if (/todos you can do now/.test(now)) return 'Tested delete on a copy: it works. <no-hand-over/>' + close;
+  if (/Add a delete/.test(now)) return 'Added the delete subcommand. <no-hand-over/>' + close;
+  return 'Understood. <hand-over/>' + close;

+/**
+ * ONE STREAM (src/agent/stream.mjs; ONE-STREAM-SPEC.md): the workspace's one conversation with the bookkeeper's marks
+ * on it. Each fact stored once; the views computed from it every time. No tasks, no groups (2026-10-27).
+ *
+ *   [1] units: a step is a call with its result; code_boss's notes are notes; meta calls are no unit
+ *   [2] the ops: parsed (the verdict anywhere), checked before applying (the one send-back), applied
+ *   [3] weight: kind floors and ceilings; superseded, dropped and stale copies weigh nothing
+ *   [4] fading: nothing under the goal; lightest first over it; a summary before fading; the recent kept; upTo; keep
+ *   [5] the history's events: gaps, summaries, done todos, dropped rules
+ *   [6] the views: what's open by request; what stands, given or not; work left
+ *   [7] the marks file: normalized, and a v1 file carried over
+ *   [8] the upgrade: oldest first, chunk by chunk, todos only on the last
+ *   [9] Clear: a break in the one stream
+ *   [10] the developer's edits from the Memory tab
+ *   [11] a conversation cleared before the stream: faded, marked by the upgrade, no todos
+ *   [12] a request never stands; the checks that read words send back once
+ *   [13] a rule changed or taken back later says so where it stands
+ * Run: node test/test-stream.mjs
+ */
+const s = await import('../src/agent/stream.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+let n = 0;
+const ev = (type, x = {}) => ({ id: `e${++n}`, type, ...x });
+const dev = (text) => ev('user-message', { content: text, modelContent: text });
+const note = (text, source = 'recall') => ev('user-message', { content: text, modelContent: text, source });
+const reply = (text) => ev('assistant-text', { content: text });
+const step = (out, cid, path = null) => [ev('tool-call', { callId: cid, verb: 'js', args: { command: 'js' } }), ...(path ? [ev('js-step', { callId: cid, phase: 'end', fn: 'read', kind: 'read', path })] : []), ev('tool-result', { callId: cid, verb: 'js', status: 'ok', content: out })];
+const unitsOf = (events) => new Map(s.streamUnits(events).map((u) => [u.id, u]));
+const ops = (body) => s.parseStreamOps(`<stream-ops>${body}</stream-ops>`);

+  ok(c.open.map((t) => t.id).join() === '4,5,6,7,8,9,10,3', `every open one - all 4 planned, not 3 - the newest request first, its steps in order (${c.open.map((t) => t.id).join()})`);
+  ok(c.done.map((t) => t.text).join() === 'done late,done mid,done early', `the last 3 finished, the latest done first, not by when they were made (${c.done.map((t) => t.text).join()})`);
+  ok(!c.open.concat(c.done).some((t) => t.state === 'dropped'), 'a dropped one is in neither');
+  ok(s.streamChecklist({ todos: [] }).open.length === 0 && s.streamChecklist(null).done.length === 0, 'nothing: empty lists');
+}
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

=== add test/test-streaming-md-probe.mjs
new 32aef3fd177f117252671e82
+/**
+ * Streaming markdown (incremental emit) probe. Drives createStreamingMd() directly
+ * in the page and asserts it emits DOM block-by-block as lines arrive — NOT all at
+ * the end:
+ *   - a table emits <thead> on the header+delimiter, then ONE <tr> per row line;
+ *   - a |-header line is HELD until the delimiter confirms it (no premature table);
+ *   - a fenced code block renders its lines WHILE still unclosed;
+ *   - list <li>s and headings append as their lines complete;
+ *   - the trailing partial line (bold/inline-code) renders live;
+ *   - the streamed result matches renderInline()'s structure (the finalize swap);
+ *   - mdInline() uses a NUL sentinel so " 5 " is never mangled.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-streaming-md-probe.mjs   (starts its own server)
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 28953;
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ✓', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  ✗', l); } };
+
+function findChrome() {
+  const c = process.platform === 'win32' ? [
+    (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+    'C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+    'C:\\Program Files (x86)\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+  ] : ['/usr/bin/google-chrome', '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome'];
+  return c.find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+}
+const mockAdapter = () => ({
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'done.' } }] }; },
+  async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'done.' } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return { data: [{ id: 'mock-model' }] }; },
+});
+
+async function main() {
+  const chrome = findChrome();
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP: Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-smd-'));
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8')).replace(
+    '</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'mock-model', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>'
+  );
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'test-key', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' },
+    defaultModel: 'mock-model', port: PORT, adapter: mockAdapter(), modelsList: [{ id: 'mock-model' }], initialProject: projectDir,
+  });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    const jsErrors = [];
+    page.on('pageerror', (e) => jsErrors.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => typeof window.createStreamingMd === 'function' && typeof window.renderInline === 'function', { timeout: 8000 })
+      .catch(() => {});
+
+    const globalsOk = await page.evaluate(() => typeof createStreamingMd === 'function' && typeof renderInline === 'function' && typeof mdInline === 'function');
+    ok(globalsOk, 'createStreamingMd / renderInline / mdInline are reachable');
+
+    const r = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      const host = document.createElement('div'); host.className = 'body'; document.body.appendChild(host);
+      const smd = createStreamingMd(host);
+      const T = (sel) => host.querySelectorAll(sel).length;
+      const out = {};
+
+      smd.feed('| A | B |\n');                 // header alone → HELD (needs delimiter)
+      out.tablesAfterHeader = T('table.md');
+      smd.feed('|---|---|\n');                  // delimiter → emit thead now
+      out.afterDelim = { tables: T('table.md'), th: T('table.md thead th'), rows: T('table.md tbody tr') };
+      smd.feed('| 1 | 2 |\n');
+      out.rowsAfter1 = T('table.md tbody tr');
+      smd.feed('| **x** | `c` |\n');            // inline formatting inside a cell
+      out.rowsAfter2 = T('table.md tbody tr');
+      out.cellBold = host.querySelector('table.md tbody tr:last-child td strong')?.textContent || '';
+      out.cellCode = host.querySelector('table.md tbody tr:last-child td code')?.textContent || '';
+      smd.feed('\n');                           // blank ends the table
+
+      smd.feed('## Section\n');
+      out.h4 = T('h4.md');
+      smd.feed('- one\n');
+      out.li1 = T('ul.md li');
+      smd.feed('- two\n');
+      out.li2 = T('ul.md li');
+      smd.feed('\n');
+
+      smd.feed('```js\n');
+      smd.feed('const x = 1;\n');               // a code line BEFORE the closing fence
+      out.codeWhileOpen = host.querySelector('pre.md code')?.textContent || '';
+      smd.feed('```\n');
+
+      smd.feed('Some **bold**');                // partial line (no newline) renders live
+      out.liveBold = host.querySelector('.md-p strong')?.textContent || '';
+      smd.feed(' and `code`.\n');
+      smd.finish();
+
+      // Parity: the same full source through renderInline() (the finalize swap).
+      const SRC = '| A | B |\n|---|---|\n| 1 | 2 |\n| **x** | `c` |\n\n## Section\n- one\n- two\n\n```js\nconst x = 1;\n```\nSome **bold** and `code`.';
+      const ref = document.createElement('div'); ref.className = 'body'; ref.innerHTML = renderInline(SRC);
+      const count = (el, sel) => el.querySelectorAll(sel).length;
+      out.parity = {
+        streamed: { tbl: count(host, 'table.md'), tr: count(host, 'table.md tbody tr'), li: count(host, 'ul.md li'), h: count(host, 'h4.md'), pre: count(host, 'pre.md'), strong: count(host, 'strong'), code: count(host, 'code') },
+        ref:      { tbl: count(ref, 'table.md'),  tr: count(ref, 'table.md tbody tr'),  li: count(ref, 'ul.md li'),  h: count(ref, 'h4.md'),  pre: count(ref, 'pre.md'),  strong: count(ref, 'strong'),  code: count(ref, 'code') },
+      };
+      out.finalCode = host.querySelector('pre.md code')?.textContent || '';
+      out.mdInlineSafe = mdInline('a 5 b');     // NUL sentinel → must NOT mangle " 5 "
+      return out;
+    });
+
+    ok(r.tablesAfterHeader === 0, `header line alone does NOT render a table yet (held for delimiter; got ${r.tablesAfterHeader})`);
+    ok(r.afterDelim.tables === 1 && r.afterDelim.th === 2 && r.afterDelim.rows === 0, `delimiter emits <thead> (2 th) with NO rows yet (${JSON.stringify(r.afterDelim)})`);
+    ok(r.rowsAfter1 === 1, `first data row appended (got ${r.rowsAfter1})`);
+    ok(r.rowsAfter2 === 2, `second data row appended incrementally (got ${r.rowsAfter2})`);
+    ok(r.cellBold === 'x' && r.cellCode === 'c', `inline **bold** + \`code\` render inside a cell (bold=${r.cellBold}, code=${r.cellCode})`);
+    ok(r.h4 === 1, `## heading emitted as <h4> (got ${r.h4})`);
+    ok(r.li1 === 1 && r.li2 === 2, `list items append one per line (${r.li1} → ${r.li2})`);
+    ok(r.codeWhileOpen === 'const x = 1;', `code block renders its line WHILE still unclosed (got ${JSON.stringify(r.codeWhileOpen)})`);
+    ok(r.liveBold === 'bold', `trailing partial line renders live with **bold** (got ${JSON.stringify(r.liveBold)})`);
+    ok(JSON.stringify(r.parity.streamed) === JSON.stringify(r.parity.ref), `streamed structure matches renderInline() (streamed ${JSON.stringify(r.parity.streamed)} vs ref ${JSON.stringify(r.parity.ref)})`);
+    ok(r.finalCode === 'const x = 1;', `final code text intact (got ${JSON.stringify(r.finalCode)})`);
+    ok(r.mdInlineSafe === 'a 5 b', `mdInline does not mangle " 5 " (NUL sentinel; got ${JSON.stringify(r.mdInlineSafe)})`);
+    // (the developer, 2026-10-23: "sometimes when the response is streaming the end token or a table row (as markdown) will show briefly and then get fixed")
+    const fl = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      const box = document.createElement('div'); document.body.appendChild(box);
+      const smd = createStreamingMd(box);
+      const seen = [];
+      for (const st of ['Here:\n| a | b', ' |\n|---|---|\n| 1 |', ' 2 |\n| 3 | 4', ' |\nafter\n']) {
+        smd.feed(st);
+        seen.push({ text: [...box.querySelectorAll('.md-p')].map((x) => x.textContent).join(' / '), rows: box.querySelectorAll('tbody tr').length, last: box.querySelector('tbody tr:last-child')?.textContent || '' });
+      }
+      box.remove();
+      return seen;
+    });
+    ok(fl.every((x) => !/\|/.test(x.text)), `a table never shows as text while it streams - not its first line waiting for |---|, not a row arriving (${fl.map((x) => x.text).join(' || ')})`);
+    ok(fl[1].rows === 1 && fl[1].last === '1' && fl[2].rows === 2 && fl[2].last === '34' && fl[3].rows === 2 && /after/.test(fl[3].text), `a row arriving is drawn as a row already (${fl.map((x) => x.rows + ':' + x.last).join(' ')})`);
+    ok(jsErrors.length === 0, `no JS errors (${jsErrors.join(' | ')})`);
+  } finally {
+    await browser.close();
+    server.close();
+  }
+  console.log(`\n${fail ? '✗' : '✓'} streaming-md: ${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error(e); process.exit(1); });

=== add test/test-stutter-collapse.mjs
new f068d8fe3b1f1a6f4f4334af
+/**
+ * A STUTTER — the model restating a point in different words, several times in one reply — becomes permanent
+ * once it is in the transcript: every prose segment is rebuilt as its own assistant message, and the flat
+ * transport re-sends the whole transcript every turn, so the model reads its own duplication and does more of
+ * it. Reported in production at four or five restatements per reply on a ~210k conversation, worsening through
+ * the day while NOTHING on the model path had changed between the good build and the bad one.
+ *
+ * This asserts what goes back to the MODEL. Every word still reaches the developer's chat.
+ * Run: node test/test-stutter-collapse.mjs
+ */
+import { isRestatement, collapseStutter, historyFromChatEvents } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const A = (content) => ({ role: 'assistant', content });
+
+console.log('\n[1] what counts as a restatement');
+{
+  ok(isRestatement('Doing the thing now.', 'doing the thing now'), 'identical once case/punctuation are normalized');
+  ok(isRestatement('Reading the LOE file to normalize the wording across options.',
+    'Reading the LOE file to normalize the wording across options. I will also fix 1a.'), 'one wholly contains the other');
+  // The shape actually seen: same opening, different tail.
+  ok(isRestatement('Setting up the checklist as requested and reverting OPTIONS.txt to its original state so we can walk through it.',
+    'Setting up the checklist as requested, and reverting OPTIONS.txt back to its original state so we do not jump ahead.'),
+    'the reworded lead-in — same opening eight words');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[2] what does NOT count (a false positive costs the model real content)');
+{
+  ok(!isRestatement('I will read the file.', 'I will write the file.'), 'one word different in a SHORT sentence is not a restatement');
+  ok(!isRestatement('Reading the config now.', 'The tests passed — 12 of 12.'), 'unrelated sentences');
+  ok(!isRestatement('', 'Doing the thing.'), 'an empty segment never matches');
+  ok(!isRestatement('Checking the build output for the failing case in the parser.',
+    'Checking the test output for the failing case in the parser.'), 'a shared shape with a different SUBJECT is kept');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[3] collapsing keeps the fuller text and never touches other roles');
+{
+  const out = collapseStutter([
+    { role: 'user', content: 'do it' },
+    A('Aligning the estimates so common components are worded identically.'),
+    A('Aligning the estimates so common components are worded identically, and pricing each unique element.'),
+    A('<remote-workspace><read path="f.txt"/></remote-workspace>'),
+    { role: 'user', content: 'result' },
+  ]);
+  ok(out.length === 4, `the two restatements became one (${out.length} messages)`);
+  ok(/pricing each unique element/.test(out[1].content), 'and the LONGER text survived — the shorter one added nothing');
+  ok(out[2].content.startsWith('<remote-workspace>'), 'the tool-call turn is untouched');
+  ok(out[0].role === 'user' && out[3].role === 'user', 'user turns are untouched');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[4] two DIFFERENT points in a row are both kept');
+{
+  const out = collapseStutter([A('Reading the config.'), A('The tests passed — 12 of 12.'), A('Now writing the endpoint.')]);
+  ok(out.length === 3, `nothing collapsed (${out.length})`);
+}
+
+console.log('\n[5] end to end: the rebuild no longer feeds the stutter back');
+{
+  const evs = [
+    { type: 'user-message', content: 'do the thing', taskId: 't', turn: 1 },
+    { type: 'assistant-text', content: 'Setting up the checklist and reverting OPTIONS.txt so we can go step by step.', taskId: 't', turn: 1 },
+    { type: 'assistant-text', content: 'Setting up the checklist and reverting OPTIONS.txt so we do not jump ahead.', taskId: 't', turn: 1 },
+  ];
+  const h = historyFromChatEvents(evs);
+  const assistants = h.filter((m) => m.role === 'assistant');
+  ok(assistants.length === 1, `one assistant turn goes back to the model, not two (${assistants.length})`);
+  ok(h[0].role === 'user', 'the developer message is still first');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[6] the blast radius: consecutive assistant messages only ever come from ONE reply');
+{
+  // This is what bounds a false positive. A collapse can only merge two segments the model wrote in the SAME
+  // reply — because between two replies there is always a user turn: the developer's message, or a tool result
+  // (role user). The worst case is losing one of two things said in one breath, never two answers given at
+  // different moments, however similar they read.
+  const evs = [
+    { type: 'user-message', content: 'first question', taskId: 't', turn: 1 },
+    { type: 'assistant-text', content: 'Reading the config now to see what is set.', taskId: 't', turn: 1 },
+    { type: 'tool-call', verb: 'read', args: { path: 'f.txt' }, taskId: 't', turn: 1 },
+    { type: 'tool-result', verb: 'read', status: 'ok', content: 'x', taskId: 't', turn: 1 },
+    { type: 'assistant-text', content: 'Reading the config now to see what is happening.', taskId: 't', turn: 2 },
+    { type: 'user-message', content: 'second question', taskId: 't', turn: 3 },
+    { type: 'assistant-text', content: 'Reading the config now to see what is set.', taskId: 't', turn: 3 },
+  ];
+  const h = historyFromChatEvents(evs);
+  const assistants = h.filter((m) => m.role === 'assistant' && !String(m.content).startsWith('<remote-workspace>'));
+  ok(assistants.length === 3, 'three separate replies stay three, however similar they read (' + assistants.length + ')');
+  let adjacent = 0;
+  for (let i = 1; i < h.length; i++) if (h[i - 1].role === 'assistant' && h[i].role === 'assistant') adjacent++;
+  ok(adjacent === 1, 'only the prose+tool-call pairing is adjacent — a user turn separates every reply (' + adjacent + ')');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[7] the prompt tells it to say things once, rather than relying on the cleanup');
+{
+  const { buildStaticPrompt } = await import('../src/agent/worker-prompt.mjs');
+  const P = buildStaticPrompt();
+  ok(/SAY EACH THING ONCE/.test(P), 'the rule exists — the collapse is a backstop, not the mechanism');
+  ok(/every turn is sent back to you/.test(P), 'and it gives the reason: a restatement is re-read on every later turn');
+  ok(!/so we don.t jump ahead/.test(P), 'and it no longer DEMONSTRATES a stutter with two near-identical sentences');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[8] the interrupt note has moved on — see test-interrupt-and-merge for its contract');
+{
+  const { QUERY_CATALOG } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+  // What this block used to assert — that the note drops its "CONTINUE the request" when a developer turn
+  // follows — was superseded within the day: the note no longer fires at all unless a TOOL was killed, and when
+  // it does it is prepended to the developer's message rather than sent as a turn. That contract lives in
+  // test-interrupt-and-merge.mjs. What stays here is the part that belongs to repetition: the developer can SEE
+  // the shape when it happens on an endpoint where it cannot be reproduced locally.
+  const q = QUERY_CATALOG.find((x) => x.id === 67);
+  ok(!!q, 'snapshot query #67 exists');
+  // The REAL payload shape: chatEvents at the top level. This fixture used to pass { chat: { events } }, which
+  // is what the query wrongly read — so the test agreed with the bug and both reported nothing on a live session.
+  const out = q.compute({ chatEvents: [
+    { type: 'user-message', content: 'change the flow and add 3x test data', turn: 1 },
+    { type: 'run-interrupted', reason: 'error' },
+    { type: 'user-message', content: 'continue', turn: 2 },
+  ] });
+  ok(/STACKED IMPERATIVES/.test(out.body), 'and it names the stacked-imperative shape when it sees one');
+}
+
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);

=== add test/test-subagent-git-creds.mjs
new 3fe16fb9f0940bbe5f505ac4
+/**
+ * A SUB-AGENT CLONES WITH THE DEVELOPER'S GIT CREDENTIALS, like the main agent does.
+ *
+ * clone() is a sub-agent's only git surface and its only network egress (the process runners are out of its snippet
+ * scope), and the whole point of its scratch sandbox is "review this repo URL". It was being handed null
+ * credentials — left over from when sub-agents were strictly read-only and could not clone at all — so a checkout
+ * of any internal repo built an unauthenticated URL and the sub-agent reported back that it had no credentials.
+ *
+ * This drives the REAL path: the real server, a real spawned sub-agent, the real clone() function, and a real git
+ * child process pointed at a fake remote here in the test that records what git presented. Two things are asserted
+ * and they pull in opposite directions, which is why both are here:
+ *   [1] the credentials DO reach the remote — the clone is authenticated
+ *   [2] the token does NOT reach the model — the tool redacts it out of the result the sub-agent reads
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-subagent-git-creds.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import http from 'node:http';
+
+// A clone must never sit at an interactive credential prompt in a test: make git fail instead of hanging.
+process.env.GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT = '0';
+process.env.GIT_ASKPASS = '';
+process.env.GCM_INTERACTIVE = 'never';
+
+// its own home; and the fake remote is the developer's GitLab (credentials go only there - gitCredEnv in tools.mjs)
+process.env.CB_HOME = (await import('node:fs')).mkdtempSync((await import('node:path')).join((await import('node:os')).tmpdir(), 'cb-sagcreds-home-'));
+process.env.CB_UPDATE_SOURCE = 'http://127.0.0.1:29655/group/code_boss';
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+const GIT_USER = 'dev-user';
+const GIT_TOKEN = 'glpat-SECRET-TOKEN-8891';
+const PORT = 29654;
+const GIT_PORT = 29655;
+const REMOTE = `http://127.0.0.1:${GIT_PORT}/group/repo.git`;
+
+// The fake remote. git asks for refs first; answer 401 so it retries with credentials, then record what it sent
+// and refuse — the point is what git PRESENTED, not a working clone.
+const seen = { unauth: 0, authHeaders: [] };
+const gitRemote = http.createServer((req, res) => {
+  const auth = req.headers.authorization || '';
+  if (!auth) {
+    seen.unauth++;
+    res.writeHead(401, { 'WWW-Authenticate': 'Basic realm="git"', 'content-type': 'text/plain' });
+    return res.end('auth required');
+  }
+  seen.authHeaders.push(auth);
+  res.writeHead(403, { 'content-type': 'text/plain' });
+  res.end('recorded');
+});
+
+// The scripted model. The main agent spawns a sub-agent; the sub-agent clones the fake remote and reports what it
+// got back verbatim, so the test can inspect what the MODEL was shown.
+const calls = [];
+let subResult = '';
+function script(isSub, full) {
+  if (isSub) {
+    // Its own snippet has run once the LATEST developer turn is a result envelope. The flat transport sends the
+    // whole transcript in one message, priming demo included (with its own results), so only the tail is read.
+    const latest = full.slice(full.lastIndexOf(String.fromCharCode(10) + '--- developer'));
+    if (/<remote-workspace-result/i.test(latest)) {
+      subResult = latest;
+      return 'Clone attempted.\nSTATUS: DONE';
+    }
+    return `Cloning it.\n<remote-workspace><js>return await clone(${JSON.stringify(REMOTE)});</js></remote-workspace>`;
+  }
+  if (/CLONE-TASK/.test(full) && !/Started sub-agent/.test(full)) {
+    // spawn_agent's schema names the task `prompt` (the worker prompt teaches `task`, which the impl drops — see the
+    // migration report); the schema spelling is the one that starts an agent.
+    return 'Handing it to a sub-agent.\n<remote-workspace><js>return await spawnAgent({ name: "cloner", prompt: ' + JSON.stringify('Clone ' + REMOTE + ' into your scratch dir and say what happened.') + ' });</js></remote-workspace>';
+  }
+  return 'OK.';
+}
+const adapter = {
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'summary' } }] }; },
+  async *completeStream(req) {
+    const full = String(req.messages[req.messages.length - 1].content);
+    calls.push(full);
+    const isSub = full.includes('YOU ARE A SUB-AGENT');
+    if (isSub) subResult = full;   // keep the latest prompt the sub-agent saw — it contains its own tool results
+    const reply = script(isSub, full);
+    if (reply) yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: reply } }] };
+  },
+};
+
+async function main() {
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-subcreds-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'readme.txt'), 'a project');
+  await new Promise((r) => gitRemote.listen(GIT_PORT, '127.0.0.1', r));
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html: '<html></html>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k',
+    gitlabCreds: { user: GIT_USER, token: GIT_TOKEN },
+    defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir,
+  });
+  const B = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+  const state = async () => (await fetch(B + '/api/state')).json();
+  const until = async (pred, ms = 40000) => { const t0 = Date.now(); while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) { const s = await state(); if (pred(s)) return s; await sleep(100); } return null; };
+
+  try {
+    await fetch(B + '/api/message', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ text: 'CLONE-TASK', model: 'm' }) });
+    // wait for the sub-agent's clone to have reached the remote (or for the run to settle, so a failure reports)
+    const got = await until(() => seen.authHeaders.length > 0 || seen.unauth > 0);
+    ok(!!got, 'the sub-agent actually ran a clone against the remote');
+
+    console.log('\n[1] the clone was AUTHENTICATED with the developer\'s configured credentials');
+    ok(seen.authHeaders.length > 0, `git presented credentials (${seen.authHeaders.length} authenticated request(s), ${seen.unauth} unauthenticated probe(s))`);
+    const decoded = seen.authHeaders.map((h) => { try { return Buffer.from(String(h).replace(/^Basic\s+/i, ''), 'base64').toString('utf8'); } catch { return ''; } });
+    ok(decoded.some((d) => d === `${GIT_USER}:${GIT_TOKEN}`), `and they are the session's own user + token, not something else (${decoded.map((d) => d.split(':')[0]).join(',') || 'none'})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[2] but the token was never shown to the model');
+    const shown = calls.filter((c) => c.includes('YOU ARE A SUB-AGENT'));
+    ok(shown.length > 0, `the sub-agent ran (${shown.length} model requests carried its prompt)`);
+    ok(!calls.some((c) => c.includes(GIT_TOKEN)), 'no request to the model — sub-agent or main — contained the token');
+    // the clone reaching the remote is not the sub-agent's NEXT model request having been made: under load (the full
+    // suite) the check ran in between and failed twice. Wait for the request that carries the clone's result.
+    for (let i = 0; i < 300 && !calls.some((c) => c.includes('YOU ARE A SUB-AGENT') && /await clone\(/.test(c) && /exit \d/.test(c)); i++) await sleep(100);
+    ok(calls.some((c) => c.includes('YOU ARE A SUB-AGENT') && /await clone\(/.test(c) && /exit \d/.test(c)), 'the sub-agent did see its own clone step');
+
+    console.log('\n[3] the project stays untouched — the clone was aimed at the scratch sandbox');
+    const stray = await import('node:fs/promises').then((m) => m.readdir(projectDir));
+    ok(!stray.includes('repo') && !stray.includes('repo.git'), `nothing was cloned into the project root (${stray.join(', ')})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+  finally {
+    await new Promise((r) => server.close(r));
+    await new Promise((r) => gitRemote.close(r));
+    await rm(projectDir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {});
+  }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();

=== add test/test-subagents-e2e.mjs
new 81b5c7e2fa7c5d272678858a
+/**
+ * Sub-agents through the real server, with a scripted model: the main agent spawns read-only helpers that work in the
+ * background, and their reports come back to it without ever keeping it busy.
+ *
+ *   [A] spawning returns at once — the main run ENDS while the sub-agent is still working; the report then starts a
+ *       run of its own, shown as a report row rather than as the developer typing. The sub-agent cannot write into
+ *       the project (its write is refused), its conversation is saved to disk, none of its steps appear in the main
+ *       chat, and no model request — the sub-agent's included — carries the SSN planted in the fixture.
+ *   [B] NEEDS-REPLY → the main agent answers with messageAgent() → the SAME conversation continues → stopAgent().
+ *   [E] a report that arrives while the main agent is busy is folded into THAT run, at its next step.
+ *   [C] an approval a sub-agent raises says which sub-agent is asking.
+ *   [F] closing the project ends its sub-agents.
+ *   [R] restarted, the project's sub-agents come back: one that was working takes its round up again and its report
+ *       reaches the main agent; one closed with its project does the same when it opens.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-subagents-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile, readdir, readFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-subagents-home-'));
+// A filter has to be ON for the masking checks below. They were written against piiscan and look for its tokens, and
+// the scripted model looks for made-up words the shipped filter (piishape) would mask - so piiscan runs here, alone.
+process.env.CB_MANDATORY_PLUGINS = '0';
+process.env.CB_PLUGINS_OFF = 'piishape';
+{
+  const { copyFile } = await import('node:fs/promises');
+  const pdir = path.join(process.env.CB_HOME, '.code_boss', 'plugins');
+  await mkdir(pdir, { recursive: true });
+  await copyFile(new URL('../examples/plugins/piiscan__1.mjs', import.meta.url), path.join(pdir, 'piiscan__1.mjs'));
+}
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const SSN = '482-11-9037';
+
+const gates = new Map();
+const gate = (name) => {
+  if (!gates.has(name)) { let release; const promise = new Promise((r) => { release = r; }); gates.set(name, { promise, release, released: false }); }
+  return gates.get(name);
+};
+const open = (name) => { const g = gate(name); g.released = true; g.release(); };
+
+const calls = [];          // every request body the model received, main and sub-agent alike
+const subSaw = {};         // what a sub-agent's request contained, keyed by scenario
+let fSignal = null, gSignal = null, rSignal = null;
+
+// one snippet, the way the model is taught to call a tool
+const js = (code) => `<remote-workspace><js>${code}</js></remote-workspace>`;
+const spawn = (name, task) => js(`return await spawnAgent({ name: ${JSON.stringify(name)}, task: ${JSON.stringify(task)} });`);
+
+function script(isSub, latest, full, signal) {
+  const has = (s) => latest.includes(s);
+  const result = has('<remote-workspace-result');
+  if (isSub) {
+    // the same work as the three tags it replaces: two reads of the project and one write into it, returned whole
+    if (has('SUBTASK-A') && !result) return gate('A').promise.then(() => 'Reading the validator and the fixture.\n' + js('const validator = await read("src/Validator.java"); const fixture = await read("data/people.csv"); const w = await write("evil.txt", "nope"); return { validator, fixture, w };'));
+    if (full.includes('SUBTASK-A') && result) { subSaw.aResults = latest; return 'SSNs are validated in src/Validator.java:3, by SSN_RE.\nSTATUS: DONE'; }
+    if (has('SUBTASK-B')) return 'There are two enrollment modules, portlet and batch. Which one?\nSTATUS: NEEDS-REPLY';
+    if (has('The portlet one.')) { subSaw.bRound2 = full; return 'Sponsors are handled in the portlet module.\nSTATUS: DONE'; }
+    // answers only once the main agent is BUSY (blocked in its next model call), so the report arrives mid-run and
+    // not in the gap before that call — where it would be folded in before the step the scenario is about
+    if (has('SUBTASK-E')) return gate('E-sub').promise.then(() => { setTimeout(() => open('E-main'), 400); return 'E answer.\nSTATUS: DONE'; });
+    if (has('code_boss restarted while you were working')) return full.includes('SUBTASK-R') ? 'R taken up again and finished.\nSTATUS: DONE' : 'F taken up again and finished.\nSTATUS: DONE';
+    if (has('SUBTASK-R')) { rSignal = signal; return new Promise((resolve) => signal?.addEventListener?.('abort', () => resolve(''), { once: true })); }
+    if (has('SUBTASK-F')) { fSignal = signal; return new Promise((resolve) => signal?.addEventListener?.('abort', () => resolve(''), { once: true })); }
+    if (has('SUBTASK-G')) { gSignal = signal; return new Promise((resolve) => signal?.addEventListener?.('abort', () => resolve(''), { once: true })); }
+    return 'Nothing to do.\nSTATUS: DONE';
+  }
+  if (has('<sub-agent-report id="ssn-validator"')) return 'The sub-agent reports: A-ACK.';
+  if (has('<sub-agent-report id="enrollment-modules" status="needs-reply"')) return 'Answering it.\n' + js('return await messageAgent({ id: "enrollment-modules", text: "The portlet one." });');
+  if (has('enrollment-modules is working on your message')) return 'Answered it.';
+  if (has('<sub-agent-report id="enrollment-modules" status="done"')) return 'Done with it.\n' + js('return await stopAgent({ id: "enrollment-modules" });');
+  if (has('Stopped: enrollment-modules')) return 'B-ACK.';
+  if (has('<sub-agent-report id="e-check"')) return 'Folded in: E-ACK.';
+  if (has('<sub-agent-report id="r-check"')) return 'R-ACK.';
+  if (has('<sub-agent-report id="f-check"')) return 'F-ACK.';
+  if (has('TASK-R') && !result) return 'Starting one.\n' + spawn('r-check', 'SUBTASK-R');
+  if (has('Started sub-agent r-check')) return 'Started R.';
+  if (has('TASK-A') && !result) return 'Starting a sub-agent.\n' + spawn('ssn-validator', 'SUBTASK-A: find where SSNs are validated under src/ and report file:line.');
+  if (has('Started sub-agent ssn-validator')) return 'A sub-agent is looking into it.';
+  if (has('TASK-B') && !result) return 'Asking a sub-agent.\n' + spawn('enrollment-modules', 'SUBTASK-B: which enrollment module handles sponsors?');
+  if (has('Started sub-agent enrollment-modules')) return 'Asked.';
+  if (has('TASK-E') && !result) return 'Starting one.\n' + spawn('e-check', 'SUBTASK-E');
+  if (has('Started sub-agent e-check')) { open('E-sub'); return gate('E-main').promise.then(() => 'Listing while it works.\n' + js('return await list(".");')); }
+  if (has('Started sub-agent log-reader')) return 'Started.';
+  if (has('TASK-G') && !result) return 'Starting one.\n' + spawn('g-check', 'SUBTASK-G');
+  if (has('Started sub-agent g-check')) return 'Waiting for it.\n' + js('return await waitAgent({ id: "g-check" });');
+  if (has('Still working: g-check')) return 'G-ACK.';
+  if (has('TASK-F') && !result) return 'Starting one.\n' + spawn('f-check', 'SUBTASK-F');
+  if (has('Started sub-agent f-check')) return 'Started F.';
+  if (result) return 'Nothing more.';
+  return 'OK.';

+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'summary' } }] }; },
+  async *completeStream(req) {

+    const reply = await script(full.includes('YOU ARE A SUB-AGENT'), latest, full, req.signal);
+    if (reply) yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: reply } }] };
+  },
+};

=== add test/test-subagents-manager.mjs
new 3430dc4b04d7bd50b605c5d2
+/**
+ * The sub-agent registry (src/agent/subagents.mjs), without a model: runRound is scripted, so every lifecycle rule
+ * is checked exactly — spawning does not block, a report is delivered once and only once, the cap queues rather
+ * than refuses, a message continues the same agent, stop ends it, and closing a project leaves nothing behind.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-subagents-manager.mjs
+ */
+import { createSubagentManager, parseSubagentStatus, subagentContext, SUBAGENT_WAIT_MAX_MS, formatSubagentList, formatSubagentDetail } from '../src/agent/subagents.mjs';
+import { tools } from '../src/agent/tools.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const KEY = 'D:/proj';
+
+/** A runRound whose rounds finish only when the test says so. */
+function scripted() {
+  const rounds = [];
+  const runRound = (agent, { message, signal }) => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    const round = { agent, message, signal, resolve, reject, store: subagentContext.getStore() };
+    rounds.push(round);
+    signal.addEventListener('abort', () => reject(Object.assign(new Error('aborted'), { name: 'AbortError' })), { once: true });
+    agent.events.push({ type: 'user-message', content: message });
+    agent.events.push({ type: 'tool-call', verb: 'read', args: { path: `src/${agent.id}.java` } });
+    agent.steps++;
+  });
+  return { rounds, runRound };
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  console.log('\n[1] the status line');
+  {
+    ok(parseSubagentStatus('Found it in Foo.java:12.\nSTATUS: DONE').status === 'done', 'DONE');
+    ok(parseSubagentStatus('Which module?\n\nSTATUS: NEEDS-REPLY').status === 'needs-reply', 'NEEDS-REPLY');
+    ok(parseSubagentStatus('x\n**STATUS: needs_reply**').status === 'needs-reply', 'markdown decoration and an underscore are tolerated');
+    ok(parseSubagentStatus('No such file.\nSTATUS: FAILED').status === 'failed', 'FAILED');
+    const p = parseSubagentStatus('The answer.\nSTATUS: DONE');
+    ok(p.answer === 'The answer.' && p.explicit, 'the status line is taken off the answer');
+    const mid = parseSubagentStatus('It logs "STATUS: FAILED" on error.\nThe fix is in Job.java.');
+    ok(!mid.explicit && mid.status === 'done', 'a status mentioned mid-report is not the status (the line must START with STATUS)');
+    // A REASON after the keyword is common and must not be lost — a sub-agent that cannot finish writes
+    // "STATUS: NEEDS-REPLY — git_clone is disabled". The old whole-line anchor recorded that as DONE, so a live
+    // fleet of ten sub-agents that all needed a reply were all marked done.
+    const reason = parseSubagentStatus('I cannot clone the repo in read-only mode.\nSTATUS: NEEDS-REPLY — git_clone is disabled, please switch mode');
+    ok(reason.explicit && reason.status === 'needs-reply', 'a NEEDS-REPLY with a trailing reason is still NEEDS-REPLY, not the default done');
+    ok(parseSubagentStatus('nope.\nSTATUS: FAILED - could not reach the host').status === 'failed', 'and a FAILED with a reason is FAILED');
+    ok(parseSubagentStatus('done.\nSTATUS: DONE. Everything checks out.').status === 'done', 'a DONE with a trailing sentence is DONE');
+    // The status line, then a trailing sentence: found by scanning the last few lines, not only the very last.
+    ok(parseSubagentStatus('answer.\nSTATUS: NEEDS-REPLY\nPlease disable Discuss mode so I can clone.').status === 'needs-reply', 'a sentence after the status line does not hide it');
+    // …but a sentence that merely mentions a status, near the end, is still not one (it does not start with STATUS).
+    ok(!parseSubagentStatus('The caller sets STATUS: DONE.\nAll good here.').explicit, 'a mention near the end is still not a status line');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[2] spawn returns at once; the report arrives when the round ends, exactly once');
+  {
+    const { rounds, runRound } = scripted();
+    const delivered = [];
+    const m = createSubagentManager({ runRound, onReport: (key, r) => delivered.push(r) });
+    const s = m.spawn({ key: KEY, prompt: 'Find where SSNs are validated.', name: 'ssn validation' });
+    ok(s.id === 'ssn-validation' && s.state === 'running' && !s.queued, `spawned and running under its name (${s.id}, ${s.state})`);
+    await sleep(5);
+    ok(rounds.length === 1 && rounds[0].message === 'Find where SSNs are validated.', 'the task is the first message of its round');
+    ok(delivered.length === 0, 'nothing reported while it works');
+    rounds[0].resolve({ content: 'In Validator.java:40.\nSTATUS: DONE', terminated: 'stop' });
+    await sleep(5);
+    ok(delivered.length === 1 && /status="done"/.test(delivered[0].text), 'one report, marked done');
+    ok(/In Validator\.java:40\./.test(delivered[0].text) && !/STATUS: DONE/.test(delivered[0].text), 'it carries the answer, without the status line');
+    ok(/what it did \(1 step\)/.test(delivered[0].text) && /read src\/ssn-validation\.java/.test(delivered[0].text), 'and one line per step it took');
+    const taken = m.takeReports(KEY);
+    ok(taken.length === 1 && m.takeReports(KEY).length === 0, 'taking the reports empties the queue');
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[3] a waiter claims the report — it is not delivered a second time');

+  console.log('\n[6] NEEDS-REPLY, then a message continues the SAME agent in a new round');
+  {
+    const { rounds, runRound } = scripted();
+    const delivered = [];
+    const m = createSubagentManager({ runRound, onReport: (k, r) => delivered.push(r) });
+    const { id } = m.spawn({ key: KEY, prompt: 'Review the enrollment module.' });
+    await sleep(5);
+    rounds[0].resolve({ content: 'There are two enrollment modules. Which one?\nSTATUS: NEEDS-REPLY' });
+    await sleep(5);
+    ok(delivered[0] && /status="needs-reply"/.test(delivered[0].text) && delivered[0].text.includes(`messageAgent({ id: "${id}"`), 'the report says it is waiting, and how to answer (in function form - the tags are gone)');
+    const r = m.message({ key: KEY, id, text: 'The portlet one.' });

+    rounds[1].resolve({ content: 'Done.\nSTATUS: DONE' });

+  {
+    const s1 = scripted();
+    const cleaned = [];
+    const m1 = createSubagentManager({ runRound: s1.runRound, cleanup: (r) => cleaned.push(r.id) });

+    m1.spawn({ key: KEY, prompt: 'Check the dates.', name: 'dates' });
+    await sleep(10);

+    await sleep(10);

+    m1.reapKey(KEY, { keepResumable: true });
+    ok(cleaned.join() === 'dates', `closed: the working one keeps its scratch, the finished one does not (${cleaned.join()})`);
+    // a new process
+    const s2 = scripted();
+    const m2 = createSubagentManager({ runRound: s2.runRound });
+    const resumed = m2.restore(KEY, snap);
+    await sleep(10);
+    const t = m2._agents.get('totals');
+    ok(resumed.join() === 'totals' && s2.rounds.length === 1 && s2.rounds[0].agent.id === 'totals', 'the one that was working takes its round up again; the done one does not run');
+    ok(/code_boss restarted while you were working/.test(s2.rounds[0].message) && /also look at tax/.test(s2.rounds[0].message), 'told it was interrupted, with the message it had not seen yet');
+    ok(t.round === 1 && t.roundEventStart === 0 && t.events.some((e) => e.type === 'run-interrupted' && e.reason === 'restart'), 'the same round, its earlier steps still part of it, marked interrupted');
+    ok(m2.conversation({ key: KEY, id: 'dates' }).events.length === 2 && m2.status({ key: KEY, id: 'dates' }).agent.state === 'done', 'the finished one is back with its conversation, for the panel');
+    ok(m2.pendingReportCount(KEY) === 1, 'its report is queued for the main agent again');
+    s2.rounds[0].resolve({ content: 'Totals are off by the tax.\nSTATUS: DONE' });
+    await sleep(10);
+    const reps = m2.takeReports(KEY);
+    ok(reps.length === 2 && reps.some((r) => r.agentId === 'totals' && /Totals are off by the tax/.test(r.text) && /steps="2"/.test(r.text)), `the resumed one's report reaches the main agent, counting the whole round (${reps.map((r) => r.text.split('\n')[0]).join(' | ')})`);
+    ok(m2.restore(KEY, snap).length === 0, 'restoring twice adds nothing');
+  }
+
+  // A DELIVERED REPORT IS WRITTEN DOWN AS DELIVERED (found live, 2026-10-24: after each restart the last report was
+  // delivered to the main agent again - "That duplicate report is a replay of the one already received" - because
+  // taking reports was no change, so the saved reports still held it)
+  console.log('\n[13b] taking or putting back reports is a change: what is saved no longer holds a delivered one');
+  {
+    const s1 = scripted();
+    const changes = [];
+    const m = createSubagentManager({ runRound: s1.runRound, onChange: (key) => changes.push(key) });
+    m.spawn({ key: KEY, prompt: 'Check the dates.', name: 'dates' });
+    await sleep(10);
+    s1.rounds[0].resolve({ content: 'Dates are fine.\nSTATUS: DONE' });
+    await sleep(10);
+    ok(m.snapshot(KEY).reports.length === 1, 'the report waits to be delivered');
+    const before = changes.length;
+    const taken = m.takeReports(KEY);
+    ok(taken.length === 1 && changes.length > before, `taking it is a change, so it is saved (${changes.length - before} change)`);
+    ok(m.snapshot(KEY).reports.length === 0, 'and what would be saved holds no report');
+    const mid = changes.length;
+    m.requeueReports(taken);
+    ok(changes.length > mid && m.snapshot(KEY).reports.length === 1, 'putting it back (a delivery that could not start) is one too');
+    const none = changes.length;
+    m.takeReports('D:/elsewhere');
+    ok(changes.length === none, 'taking nothing is no change');
+  }
+
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}

+/**
+ * Sub-agents in the real page (Puppeteer), with a scripted model:

+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
