new d4c71e09fba9b56ae0601670
+/**
+ * End-to-end probe simulating production usage patterns most likely to
+ * surface bugs in the streaming + multi-block architecture:
+ *
+ *   [A] cancel button hit during streaming
+ *   [B] multi-turn — second message after a multi-block first reply
+ *   [C] tool error in one block, success in another (in same response)
+ *   [D] code blocks / backticks inside prose segments
+ *   [E] markdown headers + bold in prose render after finalize
+ *   [F] long prose with many newlines wraps correctly
+ *   [G] no orphan running placeholders or stale streaming bubbles after
+ *       complex interactions
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-ui-production-probe.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm, writeFile, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29150;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+function ok(cond, label) {
+  if (cond) { pass++; console.log('  ok', label); }
+  else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', label); }
+}
+function findChrome() {
+  const c = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+             (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'];
+  return c.find(p => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+}
+// Content-based router. Each route is an array of CHUNK-LIST responses:
+//   routes['key'] = [ [chunks for first turn], [chunks for second turn] ]
+// First call matching that key returns the first sequence; second returns
+// the second; etc. The router picks the route by matching keys against
+// the LATEST user message in the conversation. Reviewer calls (which go
+// through `complete`) always return `<review-pass/>`.
+function routedAdapter(routes, perChunkDelayMs = 5) {
+  const callCounts = new Map();
+  // "MOST RECENT FIRST" USED TO BE THE MESSAGE ORDER. assembleConvo now sends ONE message carrying the whole
+  // labelled transcript, so scanning messages in reverse finds only one string and matches whichever key appears
+  // EARLIEST in it — the first thing the developer ever asked, on every turn after the first. Every route after
+  // the opening one silently ran the opening one's script.
+  //
+  // The current turn is the text after the LAST "--- developer ---" heading; that is what a route should match.
+  // Falls back to the whole content when the markers are absent, so a caller assembling its own array still works.
+  const currentTurn = (messages) => {
+    const users = (messages || []).filter((m) => m.role === 'user');
+    const last = String(users[users.length - 1]?.content || '');
+    const i = last.lastIndexOf('--- developer ---');
+    return i >= 0 ? last.slice(i) : last;
+  };
+  const findRoute = (messages) => {
+    const text = currentTurn(messages);
+    // LAST match, not first: the current turn can legitimately quote an earlier one (a tool result echoing it).
+    let best = null, at = -1;
+    for (const key of Object.keys(routes)) {
+      const k = text.lastIndexOf(key);
+      if (k > at) { at = k; best = key; }
+    }
+    return best ? { key: best, sequences: routes[best] } : null;
+  };
+  return {
+    async *completeStream(opts) {
+      const route = findRoute(opts?.messages);
+      if (!route) {
+        yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'final answer (no route).' } }] };
+        return;
+      }
+      const n = callCounts.get(route.key) || 0;
+      callCounts.set(route.key, n + 1);
+      const seq = route.sequences[Math.min(n, route.sequences.length - 1)];
+      for (const c of seq) {
+        await new Promise((res) => setTimeout(res, perChunkDelayMs));
+        yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: c } }] };
+      }
+    },
+    async complete() {
+      return { choices: [{ message: { content: '<review-pass/>' } }] };
+    },
+  };
+}
+async function sendMessage(page, text) {
+  await page.click('#input');
+  await page.evaluate(() => { document.getElementById('input').value = ''; });
+  await page.type('#input', text);
+  await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+  // Wait until the chat enters 'running' so a subsequent waitForIdle
+  // doesn't return prematurely on the previous turn's terminal status.
+  await page.waitForFunction(
+    () => window.__sessionRef?.status === 'running',
+    { timeout: 5000 },
+  ).catch(() => {});
+}
+async function waitForIdle(page, timeoutMs = 15000) {
+  await page.waitForFunction(
+    () => {
+      const s = window.__sessionRef?.status;
+      return s === 'idle' || s === 'done' || s === 'error';
+    },
+    { timeout: timeoutMs },
+  );
+  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 400));
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const chrome = findChrome();
+  if (!chrome) { console.error('Chrome not found'); process.exit(2); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-prod-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'real.txt'), 'real file content');
+
+  // Route table keyed by a unique phrase in each test's user message.
+  // Each route is a list of CHUNK-LISTS — one per agent turn. The first
+  // turn delivers the multi-block (if any); the second turn responds to
+  // the tool results with a no-tools final answer so the loop terminates.
+  // Done-final entries keep the loop from looping further when the agent

+  const FINAL = ['Done — nothing further needed.'];
+  const routes = {

+      Array(30).fill('').map((_, i) =>

+        i < 14 ? `chunk ${i} of an opening long enough to be shown while it is still streaming. ` : `more text after the block ${i}. `,
+      ),
+      FINAL,
+    ],
+    'first message': [

+        'Done.',

+    // has actually seen the results, which is where it sits now — the coverage is unchanged.

+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });

+    page.on('pageerror', (e) => jsErrors.push(e.message));

+    // ─── [A] cancel during streaming ────────────────────────────────────
+    console.log('\n[A] cancel hit during a long streaming response');

+        { timeout: 5000 },

+      await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
+      await waitForIdle(page);
+      const status = await page.evaluate(() => window.__sessionRef?.status);
+      const error = await page.evaluate(() => window.__sessionRef?.error);
+      // cancel now broadcasts status='idle' (not 'error') immediately via /api/cancel;
+      // the catch block clears session.error (not 'aborted' anymore — server sets idle, not error).

=== add test/test-ui-state-persist-e2e.mjs

+/**
+ * The standard file-backed UI-state store: every migrated toggle (edit/read-only, side panel, phase markers,
+ * review/auto-approve) persists through prefs.json → `ui`, hydrates from /api/state on connect, and survives a
+ * restart independent of the browser. Drives the real UI: flip each toggle off its default, confirm the server
+ * snapshot carries it, then CLEAR localStorage and reload — if a toggle is still non-default it can only have come
+ * from the server file. Run: node test/test-ui-state-persist-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+
+// Isolate the prefs file: CB_HOME must be set BEFORE server.mjs loads (it reads the config dir at module load), so
+// the persisted uiState is this run's temp dir, not the developer's real prefs.json.
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-uis-home-'));
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29177;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const adapter = { async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'ok' } }] }; }, async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; }, async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; } };
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.error('Chrome not found'); process.exit(2); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-uis-'));
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8'))
+    .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  const st = () => page.evaluate(async () => (await (await fetch('/api/state')).json()).uiState || {});
+  let page;
+  try {
+    page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1200, height: 900 });
+    const errs = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+    await sleep(300);
+
+    // The LIVE toggles in the current UI are edit/read-only and the side panel. (Phase-markers and review have JS
+    // wired to the same store but no rendered control right now, so there is nothing to click — they persist
+    // identically the day a control is added.)
+    console.log('\n[A] defaults, then flip the live toggles off their default');
+    const defaults = await page.evaluate(() => ({
+      editOff: document.getElementById('editToggle').classList.contains('off'),
+      collapsed: document.getElementById('sidePanel')?.classList.contains('collapsed'),
+    }));
+    ok(defaults.editOff === false && defaults.collapsed === true, `defaults: edit ON, side collapsed (${JSON.stringify(defaults)})`);
+
+    await page.click('#editToggle');                                   // edit → OFF (read-only)
+    await page.evaluate(() => toggleSidePanel());                      // side → expanded
+    await sleep(600);   // > the uiState write debounce
+
+    console.log('\n[B] both reached the server file (the shared uiState store)');
+    const server1 = await st();
+    ok(server1.editOff === true, `editOff persisted (${server1.editOff})`);
+    ok(server1.sideCollapsed === false, `sideCollapsed persisted (${server1.sideCollapsed})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[C] a RESTART with localStorage CLEARED restores both toggles from the server file');
+    await page.evaluate(() => { try { localStorage.clear(); } catch {} });
+    await page.reload({ waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+    await sleep(500);
+    const after = await page.evaluate(() => ({
+      editOff: document.getElementById('editToggle').classList.contains('off'),
+      collapsed: document.getElementById('sidePanel')?.classList.contains('collapsed'),
+    }));
+    ok(after.editOff === true, 'edit is STILL OFF after reload (from the file)');
+    ok(after.collapsed === false, 'the side panel is STILL expanded after reload');
+
+    console.log('\n[D] a plugin-shaped namespaced key round-trips through the same store');
+    await page.evaluate(() => uiState.set('plugin:demo:widgetOpen', true));
+    await sleep(500);
+    ok((await st())['plugin:demo:widgetOpen'] === true, 'a "plugin:<name>:<key>" value persists identically — plugins get persistence for free');
+
+    ok(errs.length === 0, `no page errors (${errs.slice(0, 2).join(' | ')})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { await browser.close(); try { server.close(); } catch {} await cleanupDir(projectDir); }

=== add test/test-ui-streaming-probe.mjs
new 4c355de4d309943c39b66b45
+/**
+ * End-to-end UI probe of streaming + multi-block rendering:
+ *   - chunks build up a streaming bubble (data-streaming attribute)
+ *   - the matching persisted assistant-text replaces the bubble's content
+ *     with the renderInline version
+ *   - separate segments produce separate bubbles (not merged)
+ *   - on reload, only the persisted finals are shown — no orphan streaming
+ *     bubbles, no missing prose
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-ui-streaming-probe.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29140;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+function ok(cond, label) {
+  if (cond) { pass++; console.log('  ok', label); }
+  else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', label); }
+}
+function findChrome() {
+  const c = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+             (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'];
+  return c.find(p => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+}
+
+// Adapter that yields the response chunk-by-chunk (so we exercise the
+// real stream parser) and then returns a final no-tool reply.
+function chunkedAdapter(responses) {
+  let r = 0;
+  return {
+    async *completeStream() {
+      if (r >= responses.length) throw new Error('script exhausted at call ' + r);
+      const chunks = responses[r++];
+      for (const c of chunks) {
+        // Small delay so the browser has time to render each chunk.
+        await new Promise((res) => setTimeout(res, 5));
+        yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: c } }] };
+      }
+    },
+    async complete() {
+      // Reviewer uses this path (non-streaming). Concatenate the current
+      // response's chunks and return as a single message.
+      if (r >= responses.length) throw new Error('script exhausted at call ' + r);
+      const text = responses[r++].join('');
+      return { choices: [{ message: { content: text } }] };
+    },
+  };
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const chrome = findChrome();
+  if (!chrome) { console.error('Chrome not found'); process.exit(2); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-uis-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'f.txt'), 'hello');
+
+  // Multi-block reply: prose1 + read block + prose2 + read block + prose3,
+  // then a pure-prose final answer.
+  const responses = [
+    [
+      'Starting the survey.\n',
+      '<remote-workspace><read path="f.txt"/></remote-workspace>\n',
+      'Middle prose between blocks.\n',
+      '<remote-workspace><read path="f.txt"/></remote-workspace>\n',
+      'Wrapping up.',
+    ],
+    ['All done — nothing further to do.'],
+    ['<review-pass/>'],
+  ];
+
+  const html = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8');
+  const mosaicSrc = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'agent', 'mosaic-layout.mjs'), 'utf8');
+  const mosaicClean = mosaicSrc
+    .replace(/^import\s+[^;]+;[ \t]*\r?\n?/gm, '')
+    .replace(/^export\s+(async\s+function|function|class|const|let|var)\b/gm, '$1');
+  const htmlWithConfig = html.replace('</head>',
+    '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script>' +
+    '<script>' + mosaicClean + '</script></head>');
+
+  const { server } = await startServer({
+    html: htmlWithConfig, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'test-key',
+    gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm',
+    port: PORT, adapter: chunkedAdapter(responses), modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }],
+    initialProject: projectDir,
+  });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1100, height: 800 });
+    const errs = [];
+    page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 400));
+
+    console.log('\n[A] send a message; observe streaming + multi-block rendering');
+    {
+      await page.type('#input', 'go');
+      await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+
+      // Wait for the agent to finish (status returns to idle / done).
+      await page.waitForFunction(
+        () => window.__sessionRef?.status === 'done' || window.__sessionRef?.status === 'idle',
+        { timeout: 15000 },
+      );
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 300));
+
+      // Count assistant bubbles (rows).
+      const assistantBubbles = await page.$$eval('.row.assistant', (els) =>
+        els.map((el) => el.querySelector('.body')?.textContent.trim() || '').filter(Boolean),
+      );
+      ok(assistantBubbles.length === 3,
+         `three assistant rows visible — "Wrapping up." is the TAIL after the last block and is dropped (got ${assistantBubbles.length}): ${JSON.stringify(assistantBubbles.map((s) => s.slice(0, 25)))}`);
+      ok(assistantBubbles[0].includes('Starting the survey'), 'first bubble: "Starting the survey"');
+      ok(assistantBubbles[1].includes('Middle prose'), 'second bubble: "Middle prose"');
+      ok(!assistantBubbles.some((b) => b.includes('Wrapping up')), 'the tail after the final block is not rendered at all');
+      ok(assistantBubbles[2].includes('All done'), 'third bubble: the next turn"s final answer');
+
+      // No assistant rows should still be marked as streaming — the
+      // matching assistant-text events should have removed the flag.
+      const stillStreaming = await page.$$('.row.assistant[data-streaming="true"]');
+      ok(stillStreaming.length === 0,
+         `no orphan data-streaming bubbles (got ${stillStreaming.length})`);
+
+      // Minimal-worker streams a turn's prose LIVE, then executes that turn's tool blocks sequentially AFTER the
+      // stream completes — so the tool-call rows BATCH after "Wrapping up" (this turn's last prose) and before
+      // "All done" (the next turn's answer), NOT interleaved between prose segments.
+      // A worked-but-unclosed task is now bordered by the terminal-wrap (F4): its rows are NESTED inside
+      // .task-wrap > .task-body, not direct #log children. Walk all the relevant rows in DOCUMENT order,
+      // wrapped or not, so the interleaving check still sees prose/tool/prose/tool/prose.
+      const order = await page.$$eval('#log .bubble, #log .row.assistant, #log .row.tool, #log .row-result', (els) =>
+        els.map((el) => {
+          if (el.classList.contains('bubble')) return 'user';
+          if (el.classList.contains('row') && el.classList.contains('assistant')) {
+            const txt = el.querySelector('.body')?.textContent.trim() || '';
+            return `assistant:${txt.slice(0, 20)}`;
+          }
+          if (el.classList.contains('row') && el.classList.contains('tool')) return el.classList.contains('meta-row') ? 'meta' : 'tool-call';   // meta = llm transparency receipts (excluded from the interleave contract)
+          if (el.classList.contains('row-result')) return 'tool-result';
+          return el.tagName + '.' + el.className;
+        })
+      );
+      // Find indices
+      const startIdx = order.findIndex((s) => s.includes('Starting the survey'));
+      const middleIdx = order.findIndex((s) => s.includes('Middle prose'));
+      const doneIdx0 = order.findIndex((s) => s.includes('All done'));   // the tail is dropped, so the turn ends here
+      ok(startIdx !== -1 && middleIdx !== -1, 'both surviving prose markers found — the tail after the last block is gone');
+      ok(startIdx < middleIdx, `prose order ok (${startIdx} < ${middleIdx})`);
+      // DOCUMENT ORDER. This used to assert the opposite — that both tool-calls landed after ALL the prose — which
+      // is what the reply looked like on screen: three messages, then the tools. The model wrote
+      // "prose · block · prose · block · prose", and a developer reading the transcript has to see that shape, or
+      // a single reply reads as the agent answering several times and only then acting. Blocks still execute after
+      // the stream ends; prose written after a block is held and released once that block's calls have run.
+      const doneIdx = doneIdx0;
+      const upToDone = order.slice(0, doneIdx === -1 ? undefined : doneIdx);
+      const toolIdx = upToDone.map((s, i) => (s === 'tool-call' ? i : -1)).filter((i) => i >= 0);
+      ok(toolIdx.length === 2, `both tool-calls render in this turn (got ${toolIdx.length})`);
+      ok(toolIdx[0] > startIdx && toolIdx[0] < middleIdx, `the first block runs between its lead-in and the next prose (${startIdx} < ${toolIdx[0]} < ${middleIdx})`);
+      ok(toolIdx[1] > middleIdx, `and the second after the middle prose (${middleIdx} < ${toolIdx[1]})`);
+    }
+
+    console.log('\n[B] reload — verify the chat looks the same from persisted events alone');
+    {
+      await page.reload({ waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });
+      await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 800));
+
+      const assistantBubbles = await page.$$eval('.row.assistant', (els) =>
+        els.map((el) => el.querySelector('.body')?.textContent.trim() || '').filter(Boolean),
+      );
+      ok(assistantBubbles.length === 3,
+         `three assistant rows after reload — the dropped tail was never persisted either (got ${assistantBubbles.length})`);
+      ok(assistantBubbles[0].includes('Starting the survey'), 'reload: first bubble preserved');
+      ok(assistantBubbles[1].includes('Middle prose'), 'reload: second bubble preserved');
+      ok(!assistantBubbles.some((b) => b.includes('Wrapping up')), 'reload: the tail is still absent — it was never stored, not merely hidden');
+
+      // No streaming attributes — they're ephemeral and shouldn't survive
+      // reload anyway, but let's verify nothing's polluting the DOM.
+      const stillStreaming = await page.$$('.row.assistant[data-streaming="true"]');
+      ok(stillStreaming.length === 0, 'no streaming bubbles after reload');
+
+      ok(errs.length === 0, `no page errors during the run (got ${errs.length}: ${errs.slice(0, 2).join('; ')})`);
+    }
+
+  } finally {
+    await browser.close();
+    server.close();
+    await cleanupDir(projectDir);
+  }
+
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e); process.exit(2); });

+/**
+ * A tool call using a verb that NO LONGER EXISTS (typically one a now-disabled/removed plugin used to provide)
+ * must NOT end the run silently. Reported: the agent emitted such a call, the chat showed only the prose before
+ * the block, no error reached the agent, and the run just stopped. Now the block yields a synthetic UNKNOWN-VERB
+ * call: a red error row in the chat naming the verb, an error result in the transcript so the model can correct
+ * itself, and the turn continues. Scripted model: turn 1 calls the missing verb, turn 2 answers in prose.
+ * Run: node test/test-unknown-plugin-verb-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { startServer } from '../src/server.mjs';
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29222;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+// browser_eval belonged to the browserdebug plugin, which is NOT installed here — exactly the reported shape.
+const TURNS = [
+  'Let me grab the page HTML. <remote-workspace><browser_eval label="s1">return document.title;</browser_eval></remote-workspace>',
+  'That plugin is not available, so I will answer from what I have. UNKNOWNVERB_FINAL',
+  // a SECOND run (new message) that hits the same unknown verb — its synthetic call id must differ from the first
+  // run's, or its error result lands beside the OLD row (turn counters restart every run).
+  'Trying once more. <remote-workspace><browser_eval label="s1">return 1;</browser_eval></remote-workspace>',
+  'Still not available. UNKNOWNVERB_FINAL2',
+];
+const requests = [];   // every request the model received — to prove the ERROR reached it
+let call = 0;
+const adapter = {
+  async *completeStream(req) { requests.push(req); const t = TURNS[Math.min(call++, TURNS.length - 1)]; yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: t } }] }; },
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP — Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-unkverb-'));
+  await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'README.md'), 'hello\n');
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8'))
+    .replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1200, height: 800 });
+    const errs = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+
+    console.log('\n[A] the run does NOT end at the unknown verb — it continues to the next turn');
+    await page.type('#input', 'what is the page title?');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.body.innerText.includes('UNKNOWNVERB_FINAL'), { timeout: 20000 }).catch(() => {});
+    await sleep(500);
+    ok(requests.length >= 2, `the model was called again after the unknown verb (calls: ${requests.length}) — the run did not end silently`);
+    ok(await page.evaluate(() => document.body.innerText.includes('UNKNOWNVERB_FINAL')), 'the corrected reply rendered after it');
+
+    console.log('\n[B] the ERROR reached the model as a tool result naming the verb');
+    // Read the message CONTENTS directly (a JSON.stringify of the request escapes the tag's quotes, so a regex for
+    // op="browser_eval" would miss it). Content may be a string or an array of text blocks.
+    const contentOf = (m) => typeof m?.content === 'string' ? m.content : Array.isArray(m?.content) ? m.content.map((b) => b?.text || '').join('\n') : '';
+    const second = ((requests[1] || {}).messages || []).map(contentOf).join('\n');
+    ok(/<remote-workspace-result op="browser_eval" status="error"/.test(second), 'the next request carries a <remote-workspace-result op="browser_eval" status="error">');
+    ok(/<browser_eval> is not a construct here/i.test(second) && /ONE way to call a tool/i.test(second), 'the result text names the tag and says there is one construct');
+    ok(/plugin/i.test(second) && /disabled or removed/i.test(second), 'and tells the agent a plugin may be disabled/removed');
+
+    console.log('\n[C] the chat shows the failed call as a RED error row (not nothing)');
+    const row = await page.evaluate(() => {
+      const r = [...document.querySelectorAll('.row.tool')].find((x) => /browser_eval/.test(x.textContent || ''));
+      if (!r) return null;
+      let res = r.nextElementSibling; while (res && !res.classList.contains('row-result')) res = res.nextElementSibling;
+      return { err: r.classList.contains('tool-err'), running: r.classList.contains('tool-running'), result: res ? (res.textContent || '').slice(0, 120) : '' };
+    });
+    ok(!!row, 'a browser_eval tool row is in the chat');
+    ok(row && row.err && !row.running, `it is marked as an error and not running (${JSON.stringify(row && { err: row.err, running: row.running })})`);
+    ok(row && /is not a construct here/i.test(row.result), `its result row shows the error (${JSON.stringify(row && row.result)})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[D] a SECOND run hitting the same unknown verb gets its OWN red row + result (no id collision)');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => { const s = (window.__sessionRef || {}).status; return s === 'idle' || s === 'done' || s == null; }, { timeout: 10000 }).catch(() => {});
+    await page.type('#input', 'try again');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => document.body.innerText.includes('UNKNOWNVERB_FINAL2'), { timeout: 20000 }).catch(() => {});
+    await sleep(500);
+    const runs = await page.evaluate(() => [...document.querySelectorAll('.row.tool')].filter((r) => /browser_eval/.test(r.textContent || '')).map((r) => {
+      const n = r.nextElementSibling;
+      return { cid: r.dataset.callId, err: r.classList.contains('tool-err'), resultBelow: !!(n && n.classList.contains('row-result') && n.dataset.callId === r.dataset.callId && /is not a construct here/i.test(n.textContent || '')) };
+    }));
+    ok(runs.length === 2, `two browser_eval call rows, one per run (${runs.length})`);
+    ok(runs.length === 2 && runs[0].cid !== runs[1].cid, `their synthetic call ids DIFFER (${runs.map((r) => r.cid).join(' vs ')})`);
+    ok(runs.length === 2 && runs.every((r) => r.err), 'BOTH call rows are red');
+    ok(runs.length === 2 && runs.every((r) => r.resultBelow), 'each error result sits directly beneath ITS OWN call row (not beside an older one)');
+
+    ok(errs.length === 0, `no page errors (${errs.slice(0, 2).join(' | ')})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { await browser.close(); try { server.close(); } catch {} await cleanupDir(projectDir); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();

=== add test/test-unlock-flow.mjs
new 2f7b33fd3193bb4e8cc343ae
+/**
+ * End-to-end repro of the production "freeze after unlock" + "freeze after
+ * api-key save" symptoms. Spins up:
+ *   - a tiny mock LLM endpoint that returns 401+unlock_url on the first
+ *     call and 200+models on every subsequent call (so we can validate
+ *     that the unlock dance unblocks the request)
+ *   - the real code_boss server (HTTP adapter, not the SDK), pointed at
+ *     the mock endpoint
+ *   - puppeteer-controlled Chrome
+ * then walks the unlock + first-run-key flows and asserts the UI moves
+ * forward instead of freezing.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-unlock-flow.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm, readFile, writeFile, mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { createServer } from 'node:http';
+import { tmpdir, homedir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+// NEVER the real key page from a dev machine. "Open unlock page" launches a browser at whatever UNLOCK_URL says,
+// and this suite clicks it twice per run. Set before the server module loads — a static import would be hoisted
+// above this line and read the env too early. The assertions below check the modal uses THIS rather than the URL
+// the mock 401 carried, which is the actual behaviour under test; the real value is a one-line constant.
+const TEST_UNLOCK_URL = 'http://127.0.0.1:9/unlock-page-under-test';
+process.env.CB_UNLOCK_URL = TEST_UNLOCK_URL;
+// ITS OWN HOME. This test ran against the developer's REAL ~/.code_boss: it deleted api-key.txt and gitlab-creds.json
+// there and saved its own fake values in their place (found 2026-10-10 - a developer's GitLab credentials were lost to it).
+// CB_HOME is set before the server module is loaded: the server fixes its home folder when it loads.
+process.env.CB_HOME = (await import('node:fs')).mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-unlockflow-home-'));
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const CB_PORT = 28897;
+const MOCK_PORT = 28898;
+const SCREENSHOTS = path.join(__dirname, 'unlock-flow-screenshots');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+// Records where the page WOULD send the developer, and lets nothing actually open. Both routes have to be
+// covered: /api/open-window is tried FIRST (its own Chrome window, via the server) and window.open is only the
+// fallback — stubbing the fallback alone still launches a real browser at the real key page, which is how this
+// suite came to be opening genai.mil on every run.
+async function stubOpen(page) {
+  await page.evaluate(() => {
+    window.__opened = [];
+    window.open = (u) => { window.__opened.push('tab:' + u); return null; };
+    const origFetch = window.fetch;
+    window.fetch = function (u, o) {
+      if (String(u).includes('/api/open-window')) {
+        try { window.__opened.push('window:' + JSON.parse(o.body).url); } catch {}
+        return Promise.resolve(new Response('{"ok":false}', { headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } }));
+      }
+      return origFetch.apply(this, arguments);
+    };
+  });
+}
+
+function ok(cond, label) {
+  if (cond) { pass++; console.log('  ok', label); }
+  else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', label); }
+}
+function findChrome() {
+  const candidates = [
+    'C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+    (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe',
+  ];
+  return candidates.find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+}
+
+// ── Mock LLM endpoint ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+// First /models call returns 401 with unlock_url. Subsequent calls return
+// 200 with a small models list. Tracks call count so the test can verify
+// the retry actually happened.
+function startMockEndpoint() {
+  const state = { modelsCalls: 0, completionsCalls: 0 };
+  const srv = createServer((req, res) => {
+    const url = new URL(req.url, `http://${req.headers.host}`);
+    if (url.pathname === '/models') {
+      state.modelsCalls++;
+      if (state.modelsCalls === 1) {
+        res.writeHead(401, { 'content-type': 'application/json' });
+        res.end(JSON.stringify({
+          error: { message: 'API key locked', unlock_url: `http://127.0.0.1:${MOCK_PORT}/unlock` },
+        }));
+        return;
+      }
+      res.writeHead(200, { 'content-type': 'application/json' });
+      res.end(JSON.stringify({ data: [{ id: 'mock-model' }] }));
+      return;
+    }
+    if (url.pathname === '/chat/completions') {
+      state.completionsCalls++;
+      res.writeHead(200, { 'content-type': 'application/json' });
+      res.end(JSON.stringify({
+        id: 'mock-cmpl',
+        choices: [{ message: { role: 'assistant', content: 'ok' }, finish_reason: 'stop' }],
+        usage: { prompt_tokens: 5, completion_tokens: 2, total_tokens: 7 },
+      }));
+      return;
+    }
+    res.writeHead(404); res.end();
+  });
+  return new Promise((resolve) => srv.listen(MOCK_PORT, () => resolve({ srv, state })));
+}

+// ── Suppress the home-dir api-key-file pickup so tests are deterministic ─
+// Our server reads ~/.code_boss/api-key.txt on startup. Tests should not be

+// constants — but that's invasive. Simpler: pass apiKey: 'test-key' for
+// the unlock test (which only needs to test unlock, not the first-run flow),
+// and clear the file for the first-run test.
+async function clearApiKeyFile() {
+  const f = path.join(process.env.CB_HOME, '.code_boss', 'secure', 'api-key.txt');   // this test's own home (above)
+  try { await rm(f, { force: true }); } catch {}
+}

=== add test/test-unlock-retry-rounds.mjs
new 4e36415b3e877f5f0e430cc0
+/**
+ * A FAILED unlock must offer the modal again, not end the run.
+ *
+ * Reported: the key page did not unlock, the developer clicked "I'm finished" anyway, and the chat came back with
+ * an error they could not get out of — no way to reopen the modal, so the only route was restarting the app. The
+ * unlock was ONE-SHOT per request (`if (401 && !unlocked)`), so clicking "I'm finished" burned the single
+ * attempt: the retry 401'd with the flag already set and fell through to a hard error.
+ *
+ * A locked key is a state the developer can fix and try again, so the modal comes back — bounded, so a key that
+ * will never unlock cannot loop forever. Both adapters, because production runs the openai one.
+ * Run: node test/test-unlock-retry-rounds.mjs
+ */
+import { createAnthropicAdapter } from '../src/llm/anthropic-http.mjs';
+import { createHttpAdapter } from '../src/llm/openai-http.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+const json = (body, status = 200) => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } });
+const LOCKED = { error: { unlock_url: 'http://127.0.0.1:9/unlock-page' } };
+const ANTH_OK = { content: [{ type: 'text', text: 'ok' }], usage: { input_tokens: 1, output_tokens: 1 } };
+const OAI_OK = { choices: [{ message: { role: 'assistant', content: 'ok' } }], usage: {} };
+
+// n401 = how many times the key is still locked before it finally works.
+function harness(make, okBody, n401) {
+  const unlockUrls = [];
+  let calls = 0;
+  const fetchImpl = async () => { calls++; return calls <= n401 ? json(LOCKED, 401) : json(okBody); };
+  const adapter = make({ baseURL: 'http://127.0.0.1:9/v1', apiKey: 'k', fetchImpl, onUnlock: (u) => { unlockUrls.push(u); return Promise.resolve(); } });
+  return { adapter, unlockUrls, calls: () => calls };
+}
+const ask = (adapter) => adapter.complete({ model: 'm', max_tokens: 8, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'hi' }] });
+
+for (const [name, make, body] of [['anthropic', createAnthropicAdapter, ANTH_OK], ['openai', createHttpAdapter, OAI_OK]]) {
+  console.log(`\n[${name}] one failed unlock, then a real one`);
+  {
+    const h = harness(make, body, 2);   // locked twice: the first "I'm finished" did not take
+    let err = null, out = null;
+    try { out = await ask(h.adapter); } catch (e) { err = e; }
+    ok(h.unlockUrls.length === 2, `the modal is offered AGAIN after the first unlock failed (offered ${h.unlockUrls.length}x)`);
+    ok(!err && out, `and the request goes through once the key really is unlocked${err ? ' — threw: ' + String(err.message).slice(0, 50) : ''}`);
+  }
+
+  console.log(`[${name}] a key that never unlocks stops, rather than looping`);
+  {
+    const h = harness(make, body, 99);   // never unlocks
+    let err = null;
+    try { await ask(h.adapter); } catch (e) { err = e; }
+    ok(!!err, 'it gives up with an error rather than asking forever');
+    ok(h.unlockUrls.length <= 3, `bounded at 3 rounds (offered ${h.unlockUrls.length}x)`);
+  }
+
+  console.log(`[${name}] a key that is fine is not asked about at all`);
+  {
+    const h = harness(make, body, 0);
+    await ask(h.adapter);
+    ok(h.unlockUrls.length === 0, 'no unlock prompt on a healthy request');
+  }
+}
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+

+/**
+ * A USER message containing code_boss-style tags (<remote-workspace>, <read …>, <phase>…) must render VERBATIM in
+ * the chat — the developer typed them, so they're literal text, not tool/signal tags to strip. Assistant output

+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';

+

+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29152;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+const MSG = 'Try <remote-workspace><read path="a.txt"/></remote-workspace> then <phase>plan</phase> and <write>x</write>';
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.error('Chrome not found'); process.exit(2); }

+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: htmlWithConfig, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'test-key', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter: { async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'hi' } }] }; } }, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: proj });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });

+

+    await page.evaluate((m) => addBubble('user', m), MSG);
+    await sleep(120);

+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);

=== add test/test-whole-conversation-summary.mjs
new 25539caa0479ebbe9580ffbf
+/**
+ * summarizeWholeConversation — the CHUNKED whole-conversation summarizer behind the manual "Compact now" /
+ * archive-and-reset. The whole point is recovering a conversation LARGER THAN THE MODEL WINDOW, so the adversarial
+ * assertion is: no single LLM call ever sends more than the window, regardless of conversation size.
+ * Run: node test/test-whole-conversation-summary.mjs
+ */
+import { summarizeWholeConversation, advanceCheckpoint, estTokens } from '../src/agent/compaction.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+
+// A message of ~n tokens (estTokens = chars/4, so n*4 chars).
+const msg = (role, n, tag) => ({ role, content: (tag || 'x') + ' ' + 'word '.repeat(Math.round(n * 4 / 5)) });
+
+// A spy adapter: records the token size of every call's payload, returns a short running brief.
+function spyAdapter({ failOnCall = -1 } = {}) {
+  const callSizes = [];
+  return {
+    calls: callSizes,
+    async complete({ messages }) {
+      const size = estTokens(messages.map((m) => String(m.content || '')).join(''));
+      callSizes.push(size);
+      if (callSizes.length === failOnCall) throw new Error('simulated model failure (context/500)');
+      return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'Running brief after ' + callSizes.length + ' part(s): tasks, decisions, files, state.' } }] };
+    },
+  };
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  const WINDOW = 5000;
+
+  // ── 1. a conversation MUCH larger than the window → chunked, no call exceeds the window ──
+  {
+    const msgs = [];
+    for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) msgs.push(msg(i % 2 ? 'assistant' : 'user', 1000, 'm' + i));   // ~30k tokens total
+    const total = estTokens(msgs.map((m) => m.content).join(''));
+    const a = spyAdapter();
+    const summary = await summarizeWholeConversation({ adapter: a, model: 'm', msgs, windowTokens: WINDOW, minTokens: 600 });
+    ok(total > WINDOW * 3, `1: the test conversation (~${total} tok) is well over the window (${WINDOW})`);
+    ok(!!summary, '1: returns a non-empty summary');
+    ok(a.calls.length >= 2, `1: it CHUNKED (multiple calls: ${a.calls.length})`);
+    const maxCall = Math.max(...a.calls);
+    ok(maxCall <= WINDOW, `1: NO single call exceeded the window (max call ${maxCall} ≤ ${WINDOW}) — this is what rescues a too-big conversation`);
+  }
+
+  // ── 2. a chunk call failing mid-way keeps the running brief (doesn't lose everything) ──
+  {
+    const msgs = [];
+    for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) msgs.push(msg('user', 1000, 'k' + i));
+    const a = spyAdapter({ failOnCall: 4 });
+    const summary = await summarizeWholeConversation({ adapter: a, model: 'm', msgs, windowTokens: WINDOW, minTokens: 600 });
+    ok(!!summary && /Running brief/.test(summary), `2: a failed chunk keeps the running brief so far ("${summary.slice(0, 40)}")`);
+  }
+
+  // ── 3. a small conversation → one chunk, one call ──
+  {
+    const msgs = [msg('user', 200, 'a'), msg('assistant', 200, 'b')];
+    const a = spyAdapter();
+    const summary = await summarizeWholeConversation({ adapter: a, model: 'm', msgs, windowTokens: 1000000, minTokens: 400 });
+    ok(a.calls.length === 1 && !!summary, `3: a small conversation is one call (${a.calls.length})`);
+  }
+
+  // ── 4. a SINGLE message larger than a chunk still gets summarized (it's its own chunk, sent alone) ──
+  {
+    const msgs = [msg('user', 100, 'intro'), msg('assistant', 4000, 'HUGE'), msg('user', 100, 'outro')];   // one 4k-tok msg, window 5k
+    const a = spyAdapter();
+    const summary = await summarizeWholeConversation({ adapter: a, model: 'm', msgs, windowTokens: WINDOW, minTokens: 400 });
+    const maxCall = Math.max(...a.calls);
+    ok(!!summary, '4: a conversation with one oversized message still summarizes');
+    ok(maxCall <= WINDOW + 500, `4: the oversized message rides in its own chunk, near but not wildly over the window (max ${maxCall})`);
+  }
+
+  // ── 5. empty / no adapter → '' (safe) ──
+  {
+    ok((await summarizeWholeConversation({ adapter: spyAdapter(), model: 'm', msgs: [], windowTokens: WINDOW })) === '', '5: empty conversation → empty summary');
+    ok((await summarizeWholeConversation({ adapter: {}, model: 'm', msgs: [msg('user', 100)], windowTokens: WINDOW })) === '', '5: no adapter.complete → empty summary (safe)');
+  }
+
+  // ── 6. AUTO path (advanceCheckpoint): a fold slice far bigger than the window is CHUNKED, so no summary call
+  //       exceeds the window (the reported "compaction fired but the summary call itself failed" bug). ──
+  {
+    const events = [];
+    for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) events.push({ id: i, content: 'evt' + i + ' ' + 'word '.repeat(800) });   // ~1000 tok each, ~30k total
+    const hist = (evs) => (evs || []).map((e) => ({ role: 'user', content: String(e.content || '') }));
+    const a = spyAdapter();
+    const adv = await advanceCheckpoint({ events, checkpoint: null, budget: 4000, windowTokens: WINDOW, adapter: a, model: 'm', historyFromChatEvents: hist });
+    ok(adv.compacted === true, `6: advanceCheckpoint compacted a huge conversation (${adv.before} → ${adv.after})`);
+    ok(a.calls.length >= 2, `6: the fold was CHUNKED (${a.calls.length} summary calls)`);
+    const maxCall = Math.max(...a.calls);
+    ok(maxCall <= WINDOW, `6: NO auto-path summary call exceeded the window (max ${maxCall} ≤ ${WINDOW}) — before, one call sent the whole fold and failed`);
+  }
+
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main().catch((e) => { console.error('FATAL', e?.stack || e); process.exit(2); });

new 898cf45df6539337cab5fb15
+/**
+ * The window title is the project's FOLDER NAME first, then where it lives — "3 - D:/1/2" for D:/1/2/3 — so a row
+ * of windows on the taskbar (or a title bar that shows only the first characters) tells the projects apart.
+ *   [A] with a project open, document.title === "<name> - <parent>"; [B] a very long parent is left-ellipsized at a
+ *   separator, the name stays whole; [C] a drive root parent reads "D:\"; [D] no project → "code_boss".
+ * Run: node test/test-window-title-e2e.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, readFile, writeFile, mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-title-home-'));
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29269;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const adapter = { async *completeStream() { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'ok' } }] }; }, async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; }, async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; } };
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.log('SKIP — Chrome not found'); process.exit(0); }
+  const base = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-title-'));
+  const projectDir = path.join(base, 'alpha_service');
+  await mkdir(projectDir, { recursive: true }); await writeFile(path.join(projectDir, 'README.md'), 'x');
+  const html = (await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8')).replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });

+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => /alpha_service/.test(document.title), { timeout: 8000 }).catch(() => {});
+
+    console.log('\n[A] name first, then the parent');
+    const t = await page.title();
+    ok(t.startsWith('alpha_service - '), `the title starts with the folder name (${JSON.stringify(t)})`);
+    const parentShown = t.slice('alpha_service - '.length);
+    const tailOf = (s) => s.replace(/^…/, '');
+    ok((parentShown === base) || (parentShown.startsWith('…') && base.endsWith(tailOf(parentShown)) && /^[\\/]/.test(tailOf(parentShown))), `and ends with the parent path, ellipsized at a separator when long (${JSON.stringify(t)})`);
+
+    console.log('\n[B]/[C]/[D] the formatter alone: long parent, drive root, no project');

+      const out = {};
+      cbSetWindowTitle('D:/1/2/3'); out.simple = document.title;
+      cbSetWindowTitle('D:\\projects\\3'); out.win = document.title;
+      cbSetWindowTitle('D:/root_project'); out.driveRoot = document.title;
+      cbSetWindowTitle('D:/' + 'very_long_segment_name/'.repeat(6) + 'leaf'); out.long = document.title;
+      cbSetWindowTitle('D:/1/2/3/'); out.trailing = document.title;
+      cbSetWindowTitle(null); out.none = document.title;
+      return out;
+    });
+    ok(r.simple === '3 - D:/1/2', `"D:/1/2/3" → ${JSON.stringify(r.simple)}`);
+    ok(r.win === '3 - D:\\projects', `backslashes too: ${JSON.stringify(r.win)}`);
+    ok(r.driveRoot === 'root_project - D:\\', `a project at the drive root: ${JSON.stringify(r.driveRoot)}`);
+    ok(/^leaf - …[\\/]/.test(r.long) && r.long.length < 60, `a long parent is left-ellipsized at a separator, the name whole: ${JSON.stringify(r.long)}`);
+    ok(r.trailing === '3 - D:/1/2', `a trailing separator is ignored: ${JSON.stringify(r.trailing)}`);
+    ok(r.none === 'code_boss', 'no project → code_boss');
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.stack || e); }
+  finally { await browser.close(); try { server.close(); } catch {} await cleanupDir(base); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
