+/**
+ * THE SESSION TRANSPORT AT ITS EDGES - adversarial and volume checks beside test-wire-session.mjs,
+ * test-adapter-network-retry.mjs, test-misplaced-close.mjs and test-quiet-run-steer.mjs. Nothing here calls a real model.
+ *
+ *   [shape]    wireHistoryShape / wireHistoryExtends / wireHistoryDiff: histories that grew, shrank, changed in place
+ *   [adopt]    sessionHas: only the first N turns are the session's; the rest go with the next message
+ *   [steps]    a run's steps on one session: rewrites, a forgotten session, a racing second plan, volume
+ *   [endWhy]   why a run's session cannot be continued
+ *   [net]      a request on a session (cbSingleShot): sent again only when it never left this machine - against a real
+ *              HTTP server, counting what arrived
+ *   [keywords] <keywords> after the model's own close, split across chunks at every boundary
+ *   [worker]   the real worker: a quiet recall run on a session, steers at the final step, Esc in a quiet run
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-wire-edges.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import http from 'node:http';
+
+const home = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-wire-edges-home-'));
+process.env.CB_HOME = home;   // never the real ~/.code_boss
+const { assembleConvo } = await import('../src/agent/engine.mjs');
+const { createWireSession, wireHistoryShape, wireHistoryExtends, wireHistoryDiff, wireHistorySig, WireSessionLost, wireLimits, validWireState, wireForgetLanes } = await import('../src/agent/wire-session.mjs');
+const { createHttpAdapter } = await import('../src/llm/openai-http.mjs');
+const { llmNetNeverSent } = await import('../src/llm/net-patience.mjs');
+const { runTurnStreaming, parseResponseSegments } = await import('../src/agent/turn-stream.mjs');
+const { runMinimalWorker } = await import('../src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const PLAIN = 'gemini-3.1-pro-preview';
+const U = (content) => ({ role: 'user', content });
+const A = (content) => ({ role: 'assistant', content });
+const within = (p, ms, what) => Promise.race([p, new Promise((_, j) => setTimeout(() => j(new Error(`timed out: ${what}`)), ms))]);
+
+// ── [shape] ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+console.log('\n[shape] a history that only grew keeps the session; any other change does not');
+{
+  const H = [U('fix the port'), A('reading it'), U('<remote-workspace-result>PORT = 8443</remote-workspace-result>'), A('It is 8443.')];
+  const shape = wireHistoryShape(H);
+  ok(wireHistoryExtends(shape, [...H, U('[code_boss: done here]'), U('[code_boss: and a second note]')]) === 4, 'two notes added after the run: the session has its first 4 turns');
+  ok(wireHistoryExtends(shape, H.slice(0, 3)) === null && /4 turns then, 3 now; first different at turn 4: was assistant "It is 8443\.", now nothing/.test(wireHistoryDiff(shape, H.slice(0, 3))), `a rewind (shrank): not an extension, and the log says where (${wireHistoryDiff(shape, H.slice(0, 3))})`);
+  const grewShrank = [...H.slice(0, 2), U('note')];
+  ok(wireHistoryExtends(shape, grewShrank) === null, 'grew and shrank to the same length (a turn dropped, a note added): not an extension');
+  ok(wireHistoryExtends(shape, [H[0], H[1], H[3], U('next')]) === null && /first different at turn 3/.test(wireHistoryDiff(shape, [H[0], H[1], H[3], U('next')])), 'a tool result dropped from the middle: not an extension, the log points at it');
+  ok(wireHistoryExtends(shape, [H[1], H[0], H[2], H[3], U('n')]) === null && /first different at turn 1/.test(wireHistoryDiff(shape, [H[1], H[0], H[2], H[3], U('n')])), 'two turns swapped: not an extension');
+  ok(wireHistoryExtends(shape, [U('(summary of earlier work)'), H[3], U('n')]) === null, 'compaction rewrote the front: not an extension');
+  ok(wireHistoryExtends(shape, [U('fix the port!'), ...H.slice(1), U('n')]) === null && /first different at turn 1: was user "fix the port", now user "fix the port!"/.test(wireHistoryDiff(shape, [U('fix the port!'), ...H.slice(1)])), 'an earlier message edited: not an extension, the log quotes both');
+  ok(wireHistoryExtends(shape, [...H, A('a reply the session never wrote')]) === null && wireHistoryExtends(shape, [...H, U('n'), A('x')]) === null, 'a turn of the model\'s after it, even behind a note: not an extension');
+  ok(wireHistoryExtends(shape, [...H, ...H]) === null, 'the same turns repeated (a replayed history): not an extension - assistant turns follow');
+  ok(wireHistoryExtends(shape, [...H, U(H[0].content)]) === 4, 'a note identical to an earlier turn is still just a note');
+  // the same to the eye, not the same bytes: the session holds the bytes it was sent
+  ok(wireHistoryExtends(wireHistoryShape([A('reading\nit')]), [A('reading\r\nit'), U('n')]) === null, 'CRLF where LF was: not the same turn');
+  ok(wireHistoryExtends(wireHistoryShape([U('café')]), [U('café'), U('n')]) === null && wireHistoryExtends(wireHistoryShape([U('a b')]), [U('a b'), U('n')]) === null, 'NFC vs NFD, a space vs a no-break space: not the same turn');
+  ok(wireHistoryExtends(wireHistoryShape([U('café')]), [U('café'), U('n')]) === 1, 'the same unicode text: the same turn');
+  // empty, missing and odd content
+  ok(wireHistoryExtends(wireHistoryShape([U(''), A('')]), [U(''), A(''), U('')]) === 2, 'empty turns compare as turns: an empty note after two empty turns extends them');
+  ok(wireHistoryExtends(wireHistoryShape([{ role: 'user' }]), [{ role: 'user', content: '' }, U('n')]) === 1, 'a turn with no content is the empty turn');
+  ok(wireHistoryExtends(wireHistoryShape([U('a')]), [{ role: 'user', content: 'a' }, { content: 'roleless' }]) === null, 'a turn with no role after it is not a user note');
+  ok(wireHistoryExtends([], [U('a')]) === null && wireHistoryExtends(null, [U('a')]) === null && wireHistoryExtends(undefined, []) === null, 'no shape: never an extension');
+  ok(wireHistoryDiff(undefined, [U('a')]) === 'the last run left no record of it' && wireHistoryDiff({}, [U('a')]) === 'the last run left no record of it', 'no shape: the log says so');
+  ok(wireHistoryExtends(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(shape)), [...H, U('n')]) === 4, 'a shape that went through the conversation file (JSON) still compares');
+  // a huge single turn, and a long conversation
+  const big = 'x'.repeat(3_000_000);
+  let t0 = Date.now();
+  const bigShape = wireHistoryShape([U(big)]);
+  ok(wireHistoryExtends(bigShape, [U(big), U('n')]) === 1 && wireHistoryExtends(bigShape, [U(big + 'y'), U('n')]) === null && bigShape[0][2].length === 60, `a 3 MB turn: fingerprinted, compared, its log words kept short (${Date.now() - t0} ms)`);
+  const long = []; for (let i = 0; i < 1200; i++) long.push(i % 2 ? A(`reply ${i} ` + 'r'.repeat(i % 97)) : U(`step ${i} ` + 's'.repeat(i % 89)));
+  t0 = Date.now();
+  const longShape = wireHistoryShape(long);
+  const ext = wireHistoryExtends(longShape, [...long, U('note 1'), U('note 2'), U('note 3')]);
+  const changed = long.slice(); changed[777] = A(changed[777].content + '.');
+  ok(ext === 1200 && wireHistoryExtends(longShape, [...changed, U('n')]) === null && /first different at turn 778/.test(wireHistoryDiff(longShape, changed)), `1200 turns: a note extends it, one changed character at turn 778 does not, and the log finds it (${Date.now() - t0} ms)`);
+  ok(Date.now() - t0 < 2000, 'and quickly');
+  ok(wireHistorySig(long) === wireHistorySig(long.map((m) => ({ ...m }))) && wireHistorySig(long) !== wireHistorySig(changed), 'the fingerprint is the content, not the objects');
+}
+
+// a scripted endpoint: every reply carries a handle; a request with none opens a new session
+const mkAdapter = ({ forgetAt = -1, failAt = -1, failWith = null, slowMs = 0 } = {}) => {
+  const sent = []; let sid = 'S1', n = 0;
+  return { sent, completeStream: async function* (req) {
+    n++; const me = n; sent.push(req);
+    if (me === failAt) throw (failWith || new Error('HTTP 502 Bad Gateway: upstream'));
+    if (!req.gemini_enterprise) sid = 'S' + me;
+    if (me === forgetAt) sid = 'FRESH';
+    const env = { gemini_enterprise: { session: sid, assist_token: 'T' + me } };
+    if (slowMs) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, slowMs));
+    yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'reply ' + me } }], ...env };
+    yield { choices: [{ delta: {}, finish_reason: 'stop' }], ...env };
+  } };
+};
+const read = async (ad, req) => { let t = ''; for await (const ch of ad.completeStream(req)) t += ch.choices?.[0]?.delta?.content || ''; return t; };
+const doc = (t) => assembleConvo({ staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: 'LIVE', messages: t });
+const step = async (w, ad, t, { keep = true } = {}) => {
+  const plan = await w.plan({ transcript: t, staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: 'LIVE', fullMessages: doc(t) });
+  const text = await read(w.wrap(ad), { model: PLAIN, messages: plan.messages, ...plan.extra });
+  w.replied({ replyChars: text.length });
+  if (keep) t.push(A(text));
+  return { plan, text };
+};
+// a session left by a run that ended on its reply, and the history it ended with
+const leftSession = async () => {
+  const ad = mkAdapter(); const t = [U('go')];
+  const w = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN });
+  await step(w, ad, t);
+  return { ad, t, state: await w.finish(t) };
+};
+
+// ── [adopt] ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+console.log('\n[adopt] sessionHas: only the first N turns are the session\'s');
+{
+  const { ad, t, state } = await leftSession();
+  ok(validWireState(state), 'a session to continue');
+  const notes = [U('[code_boss: note one]'), U('[code_boss: note two]')];
+  const hist = [...t, ...notes];
+  const has = wireHistoryExtends(wireHistoryShape(t), hist);
+  const w = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN, state, historyIntact: true, sessionHas: has });
+  w.adopt(hist);
+  const run = [...hist, U('the developer\'s message')];
+  const p = await w.plan({ transcript: run, staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: 'LIVE', fullMessages: doc(run) });
+  const c = p.mode === 'delta' ? p.messages[0].content : '';
+  const i1 = c.indexOf('note one'), i2 = c.indexOf('note two'), i3 = c.indexOf('the developer\'s message');
+  ok(p.mode === 'delta' && i1 >= 0 && i1 < i2 && i2 < i3 && !/reply 1/.test(c), `the notes and the message go on the session, in order, and nothing the session has (${p.mode}${p.reason ? ': ' + p.reason : ''})`);
+  // sessionHas past the end of the history (a stale count): everything is the session's - nothing invented
+  const w2 = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN, state, historyIntact: true, sessionHas: 99 });
+  w2.adopt(t);
+  const run2 = [...t, U('next')];
+  const p2 = await w2.plan({ transcript: run2, staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: 'LIVE', fullMessages: doc(run2) });
+  ok(p2.mode === 'delta' && /next/.test(p2.messages[0].content) && !/\bgo\b/.test(p2.messages[0].content.replace(/go on|goes|again/g, '')), 'sessionHas past the end: all of the history is the session\'s, the message alone goes');
+  // sessionHas = 0 would mean the session has nothing - it may not be adopted as having everything
+  const w0 = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN, state, historyIntact: true, sessionHas: 0 });
+  w0.adopt(t);
+  const p0 = await w0.plan({ transcript: run2, staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: 'LIVE', fullMessages: doc(run2) });
+  ok(p0.mode === 'full', `sessionHas 0: the session is not taken to hold the history (${p0.mode}: ${p0.reason})`);
+  // not intact: the state is not used at all, whatever sessionHas says
+  const w3 = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN, state, historyIntact: false, sessionHas: 1 });
+  w3.adopt(t);
+  const p3 = await w3.plan({ transcript: run2, staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: 'LIVE', fullMessages: doc(run2) });
+  ok(p3.mode === 'full' && p3.reason === 'no session yet', 'a history that is not intact: no session, whatever sessionHas says');
+  // a state of another model, or a broken one: not adopted
+  const w4 = createWireSession({ model: 'other-model', state, historyIntact: true }); w4.adopt(t);
+  const w5 = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN, state: { ...state, tag: 'NOT A TAG' }, historyIntact: true }); w5.adopt(t);
+  ok((await w4.plan({ transcript: run2, staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: 'LIVE', fullMessages: doc(run2) })).mode === 'full' && (await w5.plan({ transcript: run2, staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: 'LIVE', fullMessages: doc(run2) })).mode === 'full', 'another model\'s session, or a stored state with a bad tag: the whole document');
+  // adopted, but the first step finds the history changed under it (a fold before the first call)
+  const w6 = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN, state, historyIntact: true }); w6.adopt(t);
+  const folded = [U('(summary)'), U('next')];
+  const p6 = await w6.plan({ transcript: folded, staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: 'LIVE', fullMessages: doc(folded) });
+  ok(p6.mode === 'full' && /rewritten/.test(p6.reason), `adopted, then folded before the first call: the whole document (${p6.reason})`);
+  ok(ad.sent.length === 1, 'nothing above was sent on the handle by planning alone');
+}
+
+// ── [steps] ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+console.log('\n[steps] a run\'s steps on one session');
+{
+  // grew, then shrank mid-run (a compaction that dropped seen turns), then grew again
+  const ad = mkAdapter(); const t = [U('go')];
+  const w = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN });
+  await step(w, ad, t); t.push(U('r1')); await step(w, ad, t); t.push(U('r2'));
+  t.splice(1, 2);   // two turns the session has are dropped here
+  const s = await step(w, ad, t);
+  ok(s.plan.mode === 'full' && /rewritten/.test(s.plan.reason), `turns dropped mid-run: the whole document (${s.plan.reason})`);
+  t.push(U('r3'));
+  const s2 = await step(w, ad, t);
+  ok(s2.plan.mode === 'delta' && /r3/.test(ad.sent.at(-1).messages[0].content) && ad.sent.at(-1).gemini_enterprise.session === 'S3', 'and the next step rides the NEW session the whole send opened');
+  // a seen turn replaced by a new object with the same text (a rebuilt history): the same length and count - a rewrite?
+  const t2 = [U('go')]; const w2 = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN });
+  await step(w2, ad, t2); t2.push(U('r1'));
+  t2[0] = U('go');   // same text, a new object
+  const s3 = await step(w2, ad, t2);
+  ok(s3.plan.mode === 'full', `a seen turn swapped for an equal copy: not trusted as the same, the whole document (${s3.plan.reason})`);
+  // an empty user turn: nothing to frame, so nothing goes on the handle (the whole document leaves it out as well)
+  const t3 = [U('go')]; const w3 = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN });
+  await step(w3, ad, t3); t3.push(U(''));
+  const before3 = ad.sent.filter((r) => r.gemini_enterprise).length;
+  const s4 = await step(w3, ad, t3);
+  ok(s4.plan.mode === 'full' && /cannot be framed/.test(s4.plan.reason) && ad.sent.filter((r) => r.gemini_enterprise).length === before3, `an empty new turn: not sent on the session as an empty turn (${s4.plan.mode}: ${s4.plan.reason})`);
+}
+{
+  // the session is gone mid-conversation: a 404 on the follow-up, then the step again
+  const gone = Object.assign(new Error('HTTP 404 Not Found: {"error":"session not found"}'), { status: 404 });
+  const ad = mkAdapter({ failAt: 2, failWith: gone }); const t = [U('go')];
+  const w = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN });
+  await step(w, ad, t); t.push(U('r1'));
+  const p = await w.plan({ transcript: t, staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: 'LIVE', fullMessages: doc(t) });
+  let err = null; try { await read(w.wrap(ad), { model: PLAIN, messages: p.messages, ...p.extra }); } catch (e) { err = e; }
+  ok(err instanceof WireSessionLost && /404/.test(err.message) && err.cause === gone, 'a follow-up the endpoint answers 404 (session gone): a lost session, with the cause kept');
+  w.failed('the endpoint lost the session');
+  const again = await step(w, ad, t);
+  ok(again.plan.mode === 'full' && !ad.sent[2].gemini_enterprise && ad.sent.filter((r) => r.gemini_enterprise).length === 1, 'the step again: whole, on no handle; the handle was used once');
+  t.push(U('r2'));
+  const after = await step(w, ad, t);
+  ok(after.plan.mode === 'delta' && ad.sent.at(-1).gemini_enterprise.session === 'S3', 'and the session it opened carries on');
+  // a reply on another session after a whole send is not "lost" - only a follow-up's is
+  const ad2 = mkAdapter({ forgetAt: 1 }); const t2 = [U('go')];
+  const w2 = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN });
+  const first = await step(w2, ad2, t2);
+  ok(first.plan.mode === 'full' && first.text === 'reply 1' && w2.stats.lost === 0, 'a whole send answered on any session: that is the session from now on');
+}
+{
+  // TWO STEPS RACING ON ONE SESSION: the second plan waits for the first request to settle, and never goes on the handle
+  // while it is in flight
+  const ad = mkAdapter({ slowMs: 80 }); const t = [U('go')];
+  const w = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN });
+  await step(w, ad, t); t.push(U('r1'));
+  const p1 = await w.plan({ transcript: t, staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: 'LIVE', fullMessages: doc(t) });
+  const reading = read(w.wrap(ad), { model: PLAIN, messages: p1.messages, ...p1.extra });
+  const t2 = [...t, U('r2')];
+  let planned2At = 0; const started = Date.now();
+  const p2P = w.plan({ transcript: t2, staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: 'LIVE', fullMessages: doc(t2) }).then((p) => { planned2At = Date.now() - started; return p; });
+  await reading;
+  const p2 = await within(p2P, 3000, 'the second plan');
+  ok(p1.mode === 'delta' && planned2At >= 60, `the second plan waited for the first request to finish (${planned2At} ms)`);
+  ok(p2.mode === 'full' && /did not finish/.test(p2.reason), `and, the first never marked as replied, goes whole rather than on the handle (${p2.reason})`);
+}
+{
+  // VOLUME: a 600-turn history adopted, then 60 follow-ups with no limits - each carries only its own new turn
+  const lim = { ...wireLimits({}), maxFollowups: 0, maxChars: 0, idleMs: 0 };
+  const ad = mkAdapter();
+  const hist = []; for (let i = 0; i < 600; i++) hist.push(i % 2 ? A('a' + i + ' ' + 'z'.repeat(200)) : U('u' + i + ' ' + 'y'.repeat(200)));
+  const w0 = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN, limits: lim });
+  const t0h = [...hist];
+  await step(w0, ad, t0h);
+  const state = await w0.finish(t0h);
+  const w = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN, state, historyIntact: true, limits: lim });
+  w.adopt(t0h);
+  const t = [...t0h]; const modes = []; let maxSent = 0;
+  const tStart = Date.now();
+  for (let i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
+    t.push(U('result ' + i));
+    const s = await step(w, ad, t);
+    modes.push(s.plan.mode); maxSent = Math.max(maxSent, ad.sent.at(-1).messages[0].content.length);
+  }
+  ok(modes.every((m) => m === 'delta'), `60 follow-ups on a 600-turn history: every one on the session (${modes.filter((m) => m !== 'delta').length} not)`);
+  ok(maxSent < 2000 && !ad.sent.slice(2).some((r) => /u0 /.test(r.messages[0].content)), `each carries only its new turn (largest ${maxSent} chars)`);
+  ok(Date.now() - tStart < 5000, `in ${Date.now() - tStart} ms`);
+  const st = await w.finish(t);
+  ok(validWireState(st) && st.followups === 60, `and the run leaves the session to continue (${st && st.followups} follow-ups)`);
+}
+
+// ── [endWhy] ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+console.log('\n[endWhy] why a run\'s session cannot be continued');
+{
+  const ends = async (fn, opts = {}) => { const ad = mkAdapter(); const t = [U('go')]; const w = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN, ...opts }); await fn(w, ad, t); const s = await w.finish(t); return { s, why: w.stats.endWhy }; };
+  const a = await ends(async (w, ad, t) => { await step(w, ad, t); t.push(U('a nudge after the reply')); });
+  ok(a.s === null && /2 turn\(s\) the session does not have \(assistant, user\)/.test(a.why || ''), `a note after the last reply: said (${a.why})`);
+  const b = await ends(async (w, ad, t) => { await step(w, ad, t, { keep: false }); });
+  ok(b.s === null && /0 turn/.test(b.why || ''), `the last reply not kept: said (${b.why})`);
+  const c = await ends(async (w, ad, t) => { await step(w, ad, t); t.push(U('x')); const p = await w.plan({ transcript: t, staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: 'LIVE', fullMessages: doc(t) }); await read(w.wrap(ad), { model: PLAIN, messages: p.messages, ...p.extra }); w.replied({ stallCut: true }); t.push(A('partial')); });
+  ok(c.s === null && c.why === 'the last reply was cut before it ended', `a stall-cut last reply: said (${c.why})`);
+  const d = await ends(async (w, ad, t) => { await step(w, ad, t); w.failed('the run was stopped'); });
+  ok(d.s === null && d.why === 'the run was stopped', `stopped: said (${d.why})`);
+  const e = await ends(async () => {});
+  ok(e.s === null && e.why === 'no session', `no call made, no session: said (${e.why})`);
+  const f = await ends(async (w, ad, t) => { await step(w, ad, t); });
+  ok(validWireState(f.s) && f.why === undefined, 'a run that ended on its reply: a state, and no reason to end');
+  // a model whose replies carry no handle
+  wireForgetLanes();
+  const flat = { completeStream: async function* () { yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'no handle here' } }] }; } };
+  const t = [U('go')]; const w = createWireSession({ model: 'no-session-model' });
+  for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) { const p = await w.plan({ transcript: t, staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: 'LIVE', fullMessages: doc(t) }); const x = await read(w.wrap(flat), { model: 'no-session-model', messages: p.messages }); w.replied({}); t.push(A(x), U('n' + i)); }
+  t.pop();
+  const s = await w.finish(t);
+  ok(s === null && w.stats.endWhy === 'this model keeps no session', `a model that keeps no session: said so, not "no session" (${w.stats.endWhy})`);
+  wireForgetLanes();
+}
+
+// ── [net] ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+console.log('\n[net] a request on a session is sent again only when it never left this machine');
+{
+  const okBody = JSON.stringify({ choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' }, finish_reason: 'stop' }], gemini_enterprise: { session: 'S1', assist_token: 'T' } });
+  // a real server: `mode` decides what each request gets; every request that ARRIVED (body read) is counted
+  const mkServer = async () => {
+    const srv = { got: 0, mode: () => 'ok' };
+    srv.http = http.createServer((req, res) => {
+      let body = ''; req.on('data', (c) => { body += c; });
+      req.on('end', () => {
+        srv.got++;
+        const m = srv.mode(srv.got);
+        if (m === 'reset') { req.socket.destroy(); return; }
+        if (m === 'hang') return;
+        if (m === '429') { res.writeHead(429, { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'retry-after': '0' }); res.end('{"error":{"message":"rate"}}'); return; }
+        if (m === '503') { res.writeHead(503, { 'content-type': 'application/json' }); res.end('{"error":{"message":"down"}}'); return; }
+        if (m === 'half') { res.writeHead(200, { 'content-type': 'text/event-stream' }); res.write('data: {"choices":[{"delta":{"content":"hal"}}]}\n\n'); setTimeout(() => req.socket.destroy(), 20); return; }
+        res.writeHead(200, { 'content-type': 'application/json' }); res.end(okBody);
+      });
+    });
+    await new Promise((r) => srv.http.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', r));
+    srv.url = `http://127.0.0.1:${srv.http.address().port}/v1`;
+    srv.close = () => new Promise((r) => { srv.http.closeAllConnections?.(); srv.http.close(() => r()); });
+    return srv;
+  };
+  const srv = await mkServer();
+  const closed = await (async () => { const s = http.createServer(); await new Promise((r) => s.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', r)); const port = s.address().port; await new Promise((r) => s.close(r)); return port; })();
+  const call = async (adapter, { single = true, signal, stream = false } = {}) => {
+    const req = { model: 'm', messages: [U('hi')], ...(single ? { cbSingleShot: true } : {}), ...(signal ? { signal } : {}) };
+    if (!stream) return adapter.complete(req);
+    let t = ''; for await (const ch of adapter.completeStream(req)) t += ch.choices?.[0]?.delta?.content || ''; return t;
+  };
+  const mk = (extra = {}) => { const retries = []; const a = createHttpAdapter({ baseURL: srv.url, apiKey: 'x', netWaitMs: () => 5, onNetworkRetry: (i) => retries.push(i), ...extra }); return { a, retries }; };
+
+  // the connection refused for real - nothing reached anything - then the endpoint is back
+  for (const host of ['127.0.0.1', 'localhost']) {
+    srv.got = 0; srv.mode = () => 'ok'; let n = 0;
+    const { a, retries } = mk({ fetchImpl: (url, init) => (n++ === 0 ? fetch(url.replace(srv.url, `http://${host}:${closed}/v1`), init) : fetch(url, init)) });
+    let res = null, err = null; try { res = await call(a); } catch (e) { err = e; }
+    ok(!err && res?.choices?.[0]?.message?.content === 'ok' && n === 2 && srv.got === 1 && retries.length === 1, `refused by ${host} (a real ${host === 'localhost' ? 'AggregateError' : 'ECONNREFUSED'}): sent again on the session, and it arrived once (${err ? err.message.slice(0, 80) : srv.got + ' arrived'})`);
+  }
+  // it arrived, then the connection was reset: never sent again here
+  srv.got = 0; srv.mode = (k) => (k === 1 ? 'reset' : 'ok');
+  { const { a, retries } = mk(); let err = null; try { await call(a); } catch (e) { err = e; }
+    ok(err && srv.got === 1 && retries.length === 0, `arrived, then reset: not sent again on the session (${srv.got} arrived; ${err && String(err.cause?.cause?.code || err.cause?.code || err.message).slice(0, 40)})`); }
+  srv.got = 0;
+  { const { a } = mk(); const r = await call(a, { single: false }); ok(r?.choices?.[0]?.message?.content === 'ok' && srv.got === 2, `the same for a whole send (no session): tried again (${srv.got} arrived)`); }
+  // streamed: the stream broke half-way - not sent again, and the half is not taken for a reply
+  srv.got = 0; srv.mode = () => 'half';
+  { const { a } = mk(); let err = null, t = null; try { t = await call(a, { stream: true }); } catch (e) { err = e; }
+    ok(srv.got === 1 && (err || t !== null), `a streamed reply cut half-way: one request, not sent again (${srv.got} arrived; ${err ? 'error ' + (err.name || '') : 'ended with "' + t + '"'})`); }
+  // the rate limit / the LLM unavailable on a session
+  srv.got = 0; srv.mode = (k) => (k === 1 ? '429' : 'ok');
+  { const { a } = mk({ rateLimitMaxWaitMs: 5000 }); let r = null; try { r = await call(a); } catch {}
+    ok(r && srv.got === 2, `a 429 on a session is waited out and sent again - the endpoint refused it, nothing was added (${srv.got} arrived)`); }
+  srv.got = 0; srv.mode = (k) => (k === 1 ? '503' : 'ok');
+  { const { a } = mk(); let err = null; try { await call(a); } catch (e) { err = e; }
+    ok(err && err.status === 503 && srv.got === 1, `a 503 on a session: not sent again here - its caller takes the step on a new session (${srv.got} arrived)`); }
+  // STOPPED while the request waits for its answer: never "reconnecting", never sent again, whatever the stop carried
+  for (const [label, reason] of [['Esc (no reason)', undefined], ['a hedge\'s loser (a reason)', new Error('the other request answered first')], ['a stall-cut (a named reason)', Object.assign(new Error('stall-cut'), { name: 'StallCut' })]]) {
+    for (const single of [true, false]) {

+// The session transport through the REAL server (the developer, 2026-10-13: "I'm going to need it to work on existing
+// conversations"): startServer, the real HTTP adapter and every wrapper around it (filter, hedge, activity), against
+// the stand-in gateway with a scripted model. What the GATEWAY received is the assertion: whole conversation or a
+// message on the session.
+//   1. off (the default): every request is the whole conversation - the app as it was
+//   2. switched on over an EXISTING conversation: one whole send opens a session, then follow-ups
+//   3. the next message rides the same session; the conversation file is in the format it always was
+//   4. the handle is saved beside the conversation and survives a restart of the app
+//   5. a reset, and switching to a model that keeps no session, both fall back to the whole conversation
+// Run: node test/test-wire-session-server.mjs
+process.env.CB_BOOKKEEPER ??= '0';   // (the bookkeeper is on by default since 2026-10-28; this test's scripted model answers the agent only)

+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const PLAIN = 'gemini-3.1-pro-preview', TOOLS = 'gemini-3.8-flash-withtools';

+// A scripted model: it reads a file when asked about the port, and otherwise answers from what it can SEE - so an
+// answer that needs an earlier turn is only right if the endpoint (or the request) really holds that turn.
+const script = (msgs) => {
+  const last = msgs[msgs.length - 1].content;
+  const tag = (/--- you ([0-9a-z]{4}) ---\s*$/.exec(last) || [])[1] || 'xxxx';
+  const close = `\n/--- you ${tag} ---`;
+  const all = msgs.map((m) => m.content).join('\n');
+  const turns = [...last.matchAll(/--- developer [0-9a-z]{4} ---\n([\s\S]*?)\n\/--- developer [0-9a-z]{4} ---/g)].map((m) => m[1]);
+  const newest = turns[turns.length - 1] || '';
+  // (9: a step first, so Esc lands on a run that did something, then a slow reply to stop mid-call)
+  if (/remote-workspace-result/.test(newest) && /take your time/i.test(all) && !/Done, slowly/.test(all)) return new Promise((r) => setTimeout(() => r('Done, slowly.' + close), 3000));
+  if (/take your time/i.test(newest)) return 'Looking first.\n<remote-workspace><js>return await read("src/config.ts");</js></remote-workspace>' + close;
+  // (10: the side agent reads the main conversation with mainChat())
+  if (/remote-workspace-result/.test(newest) && /THE MAIN CONVERSATION:/.test(newest)) return (/is lighthouse/.test(newest) ? 'Found it in the main chat.' : 'Not in the main chat.') + close;
+  if (/look it up in the main chat/i.test(newest)) return 'Looking.\n<remote-workspace><js>return await mainChat({ search: "lighthouse" });</js></remote-workspace>' + close;
+  if (/remote-workspace-result/.test(newest)) return 'The port is 8443.' + close;
+  if (/what port/i.test(newest)) return 'Reading the config.\n<remote-workspace><js>return await read("src/config.ts");</js></remote-workspace>' + close;
+  // (the question is recognised by its ending: the request mask may turn any other word of it into a token)
+  if (/what is it[?]/i.test(newest)) return (/harbor/.test(all) ? 'It is harbor.' : 'I was never told a codeword.') + close;
+  return 'Noted.' + close;
+};
+

+ * The session transport (the developer, 2026-10-13: "convert it to use the plain model's session conversation").

+  const result = markFiltered({ role: 'user', content: '<remote-workspace-result op="read" status="ok">export const PORT = 8443;</remote-workspace-result>' });
+  const typed = { role: 'user', content: 'and now change it to 9000' };
+  const d = assembleDelta({ tag, liveTail: '', note: '', messages: [result, typed] });
+  const c = d[0].content;
+  ok(d.length === 1 && d[0].role === 'user', 'one user message, like the whole document');
+  ok(c.startsWith(`=== THE CONVERSATION CONTINUES (${tag}) ===`) && !/RULES/.test(c) && !/system-reminder/.test(c), 'no rules and no live state when neither changed: a header saying the opening still stands');
+  ok(c.includes(`--- developer ${tag} ---\n${result.content}\n/--- developer ${tag} ---\n\n--- developer ${tag} ---\n${typed.content}\n/--- developer ${tag} ---`), 'each new turn under the session\'s own headings, in order');
+  ok(c.endsWith(`\n\nReminder: write your reply once, then close your turn with "/--- you ${tag} ---" on its own line and stop.\n\n--- you ${tag} ---`), 'the closing reminder and the opened turn are LAST, exactly as in the whole document');
+  const r = flatTrustedRanges(c);
+  const trusted = (r || []).map(([a, b]) => c.slice(a, b)).join('');
+  ok(r && trusted.includes('THE CONVERSATION CONTINUES') && trusted.includes(result.content) && !trusted.includes(typed.content) && trusted.includes('Reminder: write your reply once'), 'the framing and an already-filtered result are code_boss\'s own; what the developer typed is still filtered');
+  const withLive = assembleDelta({ tag, liveTail: 'CHECKLIST: 1 of 3', liveTrustedLines: new Set(), messages: [typed] })[0].content;
+  ok(withLive.startsWith('<system-reminder>\nThe live state below REPLACES the one you were given before.\nCHECKLIST: 1 of 3\n</system-reminder>\n\n=== THE CONVERSATION CONTINUES'), 'changed live state rides in front, marked as a replacement');
+  ok(assembleDelta({ tag, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: `a file that says --- developer ${tag} --- in it` }] }) === null, 'a new turn that contains the tag cannot be framed safely: no delta (the whole document is sent, which re-derives the tag)');
+  ok(assembleDelta({ tag, messages: [] }) === null && assembleDelta({ tag: '', messages: [typed] }) === null, 'nothing to send, or no tag: no delta');
+  const noted = assembleDelta({ tag, note: 'Anything you wrote AFTER the last block was dropped.', messages: [typed] })[0].content;
+  ok(/still stands, in\nfull:[^\n]*\nAnything you wrote AFTER the last block was dropped\.\nThe last turn below/.test(noted), 'a note about the previous reply sits in the header, not after the reminder');
+}
+
+// ── [unit] ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+console.log('\n[unit] when the whole document, when only what is new');
+{
+  // a scripted endpoint: replies carry a handle; `forget` makes it answer on a new session
+  const mkAdapter = ({ handle = true, forgetAt = -1, failAt = -1, noEnvOnDelta = false } = {}) => {
+    const sent = []; let sid = 'S1', n = 0;
+    return { sent, completeStream: async function* (req) {
+      n++; sent.push(req);
+      if (n === failAt) throw new Error('HTTP 502 Bad Gateway: upstream');
+      if (!req.gemini_enterprise) sid = 'S' + n;
+      if (n === forgetAt) sid = 'FRESH';
+      const env = handle && !(noEnvOnDelta && req.gemini_enterprise) ? { gemini_enterprise: { session: sid, assist_token: 'T' + n } } : {};
+      yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'reply ' + n } }], ...env };
+      yield { choices: [{ delta: {}, finish_reason: 'stop' }], ...env };
+    } };
+  };
+  const read = async (ad, req) => { let t = ''; for await (const ch of ad.completeStream(req)) t += ch.choices?.[0]?.delta?.content || ''; return t; };
+  const doc = (transcript) => assembleConvo({ staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: 'LIVE', messages: transcript });
+  // one step of a worker: plan, send, keep the reply
+  const step = async (w, ad, transcript, { staticPrompt = 'RULES', liveTail = 'LIVE', keep = true } = {}) => {
+    const plan = await w.plan({ transcript, staticPrompt, liveTail, fullMessages: assembleConvo({ staticPrompt, liveTail, messages: transcript }) });
+    const text = await read(w.wrap(ad), { model: PLAIN, messages: plan.messages, ...plan.extra });
+    w.replied({ replyChars: text.length });
+    if (keep) transcript.push({ role: 'assistant', content: text });
+    return { plan, text };
+  };
+
+  {
+    const ad = mkAdapter(); const t = [{ role: 'user', content: 'first' }];
+    const w = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN });
+    const s1 = await step(w, ad, t);
+    ok(s1.plan.mode === 'full' && s1.plan.reason === 'no session yet' && !ad.sent[0].gemini_enterprise && /RULES/.test(ad.sent[0].messages[0].content), 'the first step is the whole document, with no handle');
+    t.push({ role: 'user', content: 'RESULT-A' });
+    const s2 = await step(w, ad, t);
+    ok(s2.plan.mode === 'delta' && ad.sent[1].gemini_enterprise.session === 'S1' && ad.sent[1].cbSingleShot === true, 'the second goes on the handle the first reply carried, marked never-to-be-sent-twice');
+    ok(/RESULT-A/.test(ad.sent[1].messages[0].content) && !/first|RULES|reply 1/.test(ad.sent[1].messages[0].content), 'and carries only the new turn: not the rules, not the first message, not the model\'s own reply');
+    ok(ad.sent[1].gemini_enterprise.assist_token === 'T1', 'the envelope sent back is the latest one received');
+    t.push({ role: 'user', content: 'RESULT-B' });
+    await step(w, ad, t);
+    ok(ad.sent[2].gemini_enterprise.assist_token === 'T2' && /RESULT-B/.test(ad.sent[2].messages[0].content) && !/RESULT-A/.test(ad.sent[2].messages[0].content), 'each follow-up carries the turns since the one before');
+    const state = await w.finish(t);
+    ok(validWireState(state) && state.env.session === 'S1' && state.followups === 2 && state.model === PLAIN, `a run that ends on a reply the session wrote leaves a state to continue (${state && state.followups} follow-ups)`);
+
+    // the next run, same history
+    const t2 = [...t, { role: 'user', content: 'second message' }];
+    const w2 = createWireSession({ state, historyIntact: true, model: PLAIN }); w2.adopt(t);
+    const n1 = await step(w2, ad, t2);
+    ok(n1.plan.mode === 'delta' && /second message/.test(ad.sent[3].messages[0].content) && !/RESULT-B/.test(ad.sent[3].messages[0].content), 'the next message continues the session: only the message itself is sent');
+    const w3 = createWireSession({ state, historyIntact: false, model: PLAIN }); w3.adopt(t);
+    ok((await w3.plan({ transcript: t2, staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: 'LIVE', fullMessages: doc(t2) })).mode === 'full', 'a history that is not what the last run ended with: the whole document');
+    const w4 = createWireSession({ state, historyIntact: true, model: TOOLS }); w4.adopt(t);
+    ok((await w4.plan({ transcript: t2, staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: 'LIVE', fullMessages: doc(t2) })).mode === 'full', 'another model: the whole document');
+  }
+  {
+    // the limits
+    const ad = mkAdapter(); const t = [{ role: 'user', content: 'go' }];

+    const modes = [];
+    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { modes.push((await step(w, ad, t)).plan.mode); t.push({ role: 'user', content: 'R' + i }); }
+    ok(modes.join() === 'full,delta,delta,full,delta,delta', `after the allowed follow-ups the whole document is sent again, on a new session (${modes.join()})`);
+    ok(!ad.sent[3].gemini_enterprise && ad.sent[4].gemini_enterprise.session === 'S4', 'and the follow-ups after it ride the NEW session');

+    const t2 = [{ role: 'user', content: 'go' }]; await step(w2, ad, t2); t2.push({ role: 'user', content: 'x' }); clock += 61000;

+    ok(big.plan.mode === 'full' && /characters on this session/.test(big.plan.reason), `too much sent on one session: the whole document (${big.plan.reason})`);
+  }
+  {
+    // the conversation here is no longer "what the session has, plus more"
+    const ad = mkAdapter(); const t = [{ role: 'user', content: 'go' }];
+    const w = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN });
+    await step(w, ad, t); t.push({ role: 'user', content: 'r1' }); await step(w, ad, t); t.push({ role: 'user', content: 'r2' });

+    // a reply thrown away and asked for again (the transient-backend-error path): nothing was added
+    const again = await step(w, ad, t, { staticPrompt: 'RULES (discuss mode)', liveTail: 'LIVE with a checklist', keep: false });
+    const redo = await step(w, ad, t, { staticPrompt: 'RULES (discuss mode)', liveTail: 'LIVE with a checklist' });
+    ok(again.plan.mode !== undefined && redo.plan.mode === 'full' && /thrown away/.test(redo.plan.reason), 'a reply that was thrown away and asked for again: the session holds a turn we do not, so the whole document');
+  }
+  {
+    // the endpoint forgot the session
+    const ad = mkAdapter({ forgetAt: 2 }); const t = [{ role: 'user', content: 'go' }];
+    const w = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN });
+    await step(w, ad, t); t.push({ role: 'user', content: 'r1' });
+    const plan = await w.plan({ transcript: t, staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: 'LIVE', fullMessages: doc(t) });
+    let released = '', err = null;
+    try { for await (const ch of w.wrap(ad).completeStream({ model: PLAIN, messages: plan.messages, ...plan.extra })) released += ch.choices?.[0]?.delta?.content || ''; } catch (e) { err = e; }
+    ok(err instanceof WireSessionLost && released === '', 'a reply that comes back on ANOTHER session is refused before a word of it is released');
+    w.failed('the endpoint lost the session');
+    const after = await step(w, ad, t);
+    ok(after.plan.mode === 'full' && !ad.sent[2].gemini_enterprise, 'and the step is taken again with the whole document');
+    ok(w.stats.lost === 1 && w.stats.full === 2 && w.stats.delta === 1, 'counted: one lost, two whole, one on the session');
+  }
+  {
+    // a follow-up that fails outright is the same thing: the whole document, and nothing sent twice on the handle

+    const w = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN });
+    await step(w, ad, t); t.push({ role: 'user', content: 'r1' });
+    const plan = await w.plan({ transcript: t, staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: 'LIVE', fullMessages: doc(t) });
+    let err = null; try { await read(w.wrap(ad), { model: PLAIN, messages: plan.messages, ...plan.extra }); } catch (e) { err = e; }
+    ok(err instanceof WireSessionLost && /HTTP 502/.test(err.message), 'a follow-up that fails is reported as a lost session, with the cause');
+    // a reply with no handle at all on a follow-up cannot be verified

+  }

+    w.replied({}); w.kept({ fabricated: true }); t.push({ role: 'assistant', content: 'x' }, { role: 'user', content: 'r5' });
+    ok((await step(w, ad, t)).plan.reason === 'the last reply invented a tool result', 'a reply that invented a tool result: the session believes it, so the whole document');
+    ok((await w.finish([...t, { role: 'user', content: 'a nudge after the last reply' }])) === null, 'a run that did not end on the session\'s own reply leaves nothing to continue');
+  }
+  {
+    // the reply is read to its end even when the caller stops reading; Esc cuts at once
+    let ended = false, aborted = false;
+    const slow = { completeStream: async function* (req) {
+      const env = { gemini_enterprise: { session: 'S1', assist_token: 'T' } };
+      yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: 'done\n/--- you ---' } }], ...env };
+      await new Promise((r, j) => { const t = setTimeout(r, 120); req.signal?.addEventListener('abort', () => { clearTimeout(t); aborted = true; j(Object.assign(new Error('aborted'), { name: 'AbortError' })); }); });
+      yield { choices: [{ delta: {}, finish_reason: 'stop' }], ...env }; ended = true;
+    } };
+    const t = [{ role: 'user', content: 'go' }];
+    const w = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN });
+    const plan = await w.plan({ transcript: t, staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: 'LIVE', fullMessages: doc(t) });
+    const ac = new AbortController();
+    const it = w.wrap(slow).completeStream({ model: PLAIN, messages: plan.messages, signal: ac.signal })[Symbol.asyncIterator]();
+    await it.next(); ac.abort(); await it.return?.();   // the worker stops at the model's closing line
+    w.replied({ footerCut: true }); t.push({ role: 'assistant', content: 'done' }, { role: 'user', content: 'r1' });
+    const next = await w.plan({ transcript: t, staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: 'LIVE', fullMessages: doc(t) });
+    ok(ended && !aborted && next.mode === 'delta', 'a reply we stopped reading is still read to its end, and the session is kept');
+    const userAc = new AbortController();
+    const w2 = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN, userSignal: userAc.signal }); ended = false; aborted = false;
+    const t2 = [{ role: 'user', content: 'go' }];
+    const p2 = await w2.plan({ transcript: t2, staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: 'LIVE', fullMessages: doc(t2) });
+    const ac2 = new AbortController(); userAc.signal.addEventListener('abort', () => ac2.abort());
+    const it2 = w2.wrap(slow).completeStream({ model: PLAIN, messages: p2.messages, signal: ac2.signal })[Symbol.asyncIterator]();
+    await it2.next(); userAc.abort(); await it2.return?.(); await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 30));
+    ok(aborted && !ended, 'Esc cuts the request at once');
+  }
+  ok(wireHistorySig([{ role: 'user', content: 'a' }, { role: 'assistant', content: 'b' }]) === wireHistorySig([{ role: 'user', content: 'a' }, { role: 'assistant', content: 'b' }]) && wireHistorySig([{ role: 'user', content: 'a' }]) !== wireHistorySig([{ role: 'user', content: 'a ' }]), 'the history fingerprint is the same for the same turns and different for any change');
+  ok(!validWireState(null) && !validWireState({ env: {}, tag: 'ab12', model: 'm' }) && validWireState({ env: { session: 's' }, tag: 'ab12', model: 'm' }), 'a stored state needs a handle, a tag and a model');
+  {
+    // TURNS ADDED AFTER THE RUN ENDED (live, 2026-11-02: the bookkeeper's "done here" note at the end of the history made
+    // every next message send the whole conversation): the session goes on, and the added turns go with the message
+    const { wireHistoryShape, wireHistoryExtends, wireHistoryDiff } = await import('../src/agent/wire-session.mjs');
+    const h = [{ role: 'user', content: 'go' }, { role: 'assistant', content: 'done it' }];
+    const shape = wireHistoryShape(h);
+    const note = { role: 'user', content: '[code_boss: done here - the fix]' };
+    ok(wireHistoryExtends(shape, [...h, note]) === 2, 'the history plus a note after it: the session has its first 2 turns');
+    ok(wireHistoryExtends(shape, h) === null && wireHistoryExtends(shape, [h[0], { role: 'assistant', content: 'done it!' }, note]) === null, 'the same history, or one changed in place: not an extension');
+    ok(wireHistoryExtends(shape, [...h, { role: 'assistant', content: 'a turn the session never wrote' }]) === null, 'a turn of the model\'s added after it: not an extension');
+    ok(/first different at turn 3: was nothing, now user "\[code_boss: done here/.test(wireHistoryDiff(shape, [...h, note])), `the log says where it changed (${wireHistoryDiff(shape, [...h, note])})`);
+    // the next run on it: the note is new, and goes on the session with the developer's message
+    const ad = mkAdapter(); const t = [{ role: 'user', content: 'go' }];
+    const w = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN });
+    await step(w, ad, t);
+    const state = await w.finish(t);
+    const t2 = [...t, note];
+    const w2 = createWireSession({ model: PLAIN, state, historyIntact: true, sessionHas: wireHistoryExtends(wireHistoryShape(t), t2) });
+    w2.adopt(t2);
+    t2.push({ role: 'user', content: 'next message' });
+    const p = await w2.plan({ transcript: t2, staticPrompt: 'RULES', liveTail: 'LIVE', fullMessages: doc(t2) });
+    ok(state && p.mode === 'delta' && /done here - the fix/.test(p.messages[0].content) && /next message/.test(p.messages[0].content) && !/\bgo\b/.test(p.messages[0].content.replace(/go on|goes/g, '')), `the next message goes on the session, with the note before it (${p.mode}${p.reason ? ': ' + p.reason : ''})`);
+  }
+}
+
+// ── [worker] ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+console.log('\n[worker] the real worker and adapter against the stand-in gateway');
+{
+  const proj = path.join(dir, 'proj'); await (await import('node:fs/promises')).mkdir(path.join(proj, 'src'), { recursive: true });
+  await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'src', 'config.ts'), 'export const PORT = 8443;\n');
+  // A scripted model that behaves like one: it answers the LAST thing in what it was given.
+  const tagIn = (msgs) => (/--- you ([0-9a-z]{4}) ---\s*$/.exec(msgs[msgs.length - 1].content) || [])[1] || 'xxxx';
+  const script = (msgs) => {
+    const last = msgs[msgs.length - 1].content, tag = tagIn(msgs);
+    const close = `\n/--- you ${tag} ---`;
+    const all = msgs.map((m) => m.content).join('\n');
+    // the newest developer turn in what this model can see
+    const turns = [...last.matchAll(/--- developer [0-9a-z]{4} ---\n([\s\S]*?)\n\/--- developer [0-9a-z]{4} ---/g)].map((m) => m[1]);
+    const newest = turns[turns.length - 1] || '';
+    if (/remote-workspace-result/.test(newest)) {
+      if (/edited src.config.ts/.test(newest)) return 'Done: the port is 9000 now.' + close;
+      if (/PORT = 8443/.test(newest) && /change the port to 9000/.test(all) && !/edit\("src\/config\.ts"/.test(all)) return `I will change it.\n<remote-workspace><js>return await edit("src/config.ts", "export const PORT = 8443;", "export const PORT = 9000;");</js></remote-workspace>` + close;
+      if (/PORT = 8443/.test(newest)) return 'The port is 8443.' + close;
+      return 'Done: the port is 9000 now.' + close;
+    }
+    if (/what port/i.test(newest) || /change the port/i.test(newest)) return `Reading the config.\n<remote-workspace><js>return await read("src/config.ts");</js></remote-workspace>` + close;
+    if (/which port did you report/i.test(newest)) return (/The port is 8443\./.test(all) ? 'I reported 8443.' : 'I have no record of reporting a port.') + close;
+    return 'OK.' + close;
+  };
+  const run = async ({ gw, model = PLAIN, text, history = [], wire, events, fetchImpl }) => {
+    const adapter = createHttpAdapter({ baseURL: gw.url, apiKey: 'x', maxRetries: 1, ...(fetchImpl ? { fetchImpl } : {}) });
+    let out = null, stats = null;
+    const r = await runMinimalWorker({
+      adapter, model, projectDir: proj, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: text }], history, maxTurns: 8,
+      onChatEvent: (e) => { if (events) events.push(e); },
+      ...(wire ? { wire, onWireState: (s, st) => { out = s; stats = st; } } : {}),
+    });
+    return { r, state: out, stats };
+  };
+
+  // 1. one message, three steps: whole, then two follow-ups
+  {
+    const gw = await startGatewaySim({ backend: script });
+    const { r, state, stats } = await run({ gw, text: 'what port does the server use?', wire: { state: null } });
+    const L = gw.log.filter((e) => e.lane === 'plain');
+    ok(/8443/.test(r.content || ''), `the run reached its answer (${String(r.content).slice(0, 40)})`);
+    ok(L.length === 2 && !L[0].sentSession && L[1].sentSession === L[0].session && L[1].session === L[0].session, `two requests: the whole conversation, then one on its session (${L.map((e) => (e.sentSession ? 'session' : 'whole') + ':' + e.readChars).join(' ')})`);
+    ok(L[0].readChars > 4000 && L[1].readChars < L[0].readChars / 3 && /remote-workspace-result/.test(L[1].last) && /PORT = 8443/.test(L[1].last) && !/You are code_boss|THE CONVERSATION SO FAR/.test(L[1].last), 'the follow-up is the tool result and its framing, not the prompt and not the conversation');
+    ok(/THE CONVERSATION CONTINUES/.test(L[1].last) && /Reminder: write your reply once[^\n]*\n\n--- you [0-9a-z]{4} ---$/.test(L[1].last), 'framed as a continuation, ending on the reminder and the opened turn');
+    ok(gw.sessions.get(L[0].session).turns.length === 4, 'the session over there holds the whole exchange: document, reply, result, reply');
+    ok(validWireState(state) && stats.full === 1 && stats.delta === 1 && stats.lost === 0, 'and the run leaves a session the next message can continue');
+
+    // 2. the next message continues it; the model answers from what the SESSION holds
+    const history = [{ role: 'user', content: 'what port does the server use?' }, { role: 'assistant', content: '<remote-workspace><js>return await read("src/config.ts");</js></remote-workspace>' }, { role: 'user', content: '<remote-workspace-result op="read" status="ok">export const PORT = 8443;</remote-workspace-result>' }, { role: 'assistant', content: 'The port is 8443.' }];
+    const before = gw.log.length;
+    const second = await run({ gw, text: 'which port did you report?', history, wire: { state, historyIntact: true } });
+    const L2 = gw.log.slice(before);
+    ok(L2.length === 1 && L2[0].sentSession === L[0].session && L2[0].readChars < 1200 && /which port did you report/.test(L2[0].last), `the next message is ONE small request on the same session (${L2[0]?.readChars} chars)`);
+    ok(/I reported 8443/.test(second.r.content || ''), 'and the model answers from the conversation the session kept, which was not sent again');
+    ok(validWireState(second.state) && second.state.followups === 2, 'the session carries on to the message after');
+
+    // 3. the same with the history changed underneath: whole document
+    const third = await run({ gw, text: 'which port did you report?', history, wire: { state: second.state, historyIntact: false } });
+    ok(!gw.log[gw.log.length - 1].sentSession && gw.log[gw.log.length - 1].readChars > 4000 && /8443/.test(third.r.content || ''), 'a history that changed since the last run: the whole conversation, on a new session');
+    await gw.close();
+  }
+
+  // 4. feature off, and a model with no session: unchanged
+  {
+    const gw = await startGatewaySim({ backend: script });
+    await run({ gw, text: 'what port does the server use?' });
+    ok(gw.log.length === 2 && gw.log.every((e) => !e.sentSession && e.readChars > 4000), 'with the feature off every request is the whole conversation, as before');
+    const n = gw.log.length;
+    const tools = await run({ gw, model: TOOLS, text: 'what port does the server use?', wire: { state: null } });
+    ok(gw.log.slice(n).every((e) => e.lane === 'tools' && !e.sentSession) && tools.state === null && tools.stats.delta === 0, 'on a model that keeps no session the feature is inert: whole conversation every step, nothing stored');
+    await gw.close();
+  }
+
+  // 5. the endpoint forgets the session between steps
+  for (const mode of ['fresh', 'error']) {
+    const gw = await startGatewaySim({ backend: script, unknownSession: mode });
+    const events = [];
+    let dropped = false;
+    const orig = gw.sessions.get.bind(gw.sessions);
+    gw.sessions.get = (k) => { if (!dropped && gw.log.length === 2) { dropped = true; gw.sessions.delete(k); } return orig(k); };
+    const { r, stats } = await run({ gw, text: 'please change the port to 9000', wire: { state: null }, events });
+    const L = gw.log;
+    const onDisk = await readFile(path.join(proj, 'src', 'config.ts'), 'utf8');
+    ok(/9000/.test(onDisk) && /9000/.test(r.content || ''), `[${mode}] the run still finishes its work after the endpoint forgot the session`);
+    ok(stats.lost === 1 && L[1].sentSession && !L[2].sentSession && L[2].readChars > 4000, `[${mode}] the lost step was taken again with the whole conversation (${L.map((e) => (e.sentSession ? 's' : 'W') + e.status).join(' ')})`);
+    ok(events.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && /edit[(]/.test(JSON.stringify(e.args || {}))).length === 1 && events.some((e) => e.type === 'tool-result' && e.verb === 'retry' && /session/.test(String(e.content))), `[${mode}] nothing from the reply of the wrong session ran, and the retry is visible as a muted row`);
+    await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'src', 'config.ts'), 'export const PORT = 8443;\n');
+    await gw.close();
+  }
+
+  // 6. a follow-up that fails at the gateway is not sent twice on the handle
+  {
+    const gw = await startGatewaySim({ backend: script });
+    gw.state.failNext.push({ status: 502, times: 1 });   // consumed by the FIRST request: prove the opener still retries
+    const { r } = await run({ gw, text: 'what port does the server use?', wire: { state: null } });
+    ok(/8443/.test(r.content || '') && gw.log[0].status === 502 && gw.log[1].status === 200 && !gw.log[1].sentSession, 'a failed opener is retried by the adapter as before');
+    let calls = 0, gw2 = null;
+    gw2 = await startGatewaySim({ backend: (m, o) => { if (++calls === 1) gw2.state.failNext.push(502); return script(m, o); } });   // the request AFTER the opener fails
+    const two = await run({ gw: gw2, text: 'what port does the server use?', wire: { state: null } });
+    const L = gw2.log;
+    ok(/8443/.test(two.r.content || '') && L[1].status === 502 && L.filter((e) => e.sentSession).length === 1 && !L[2].sentSession, `a follow-up that fails is sent ONCE on the handle; the step is then taken with the whole conversation (${L.map((e) => (e.sentSession ? 's' : 'W') + e.status).join(' ')})`);
+    // (the developer, 2026-11-02: "when we retry, we should switch to a new session ... in case the issue is the
+    // session") - a 503 on a follow-up was retried on the same handle; now the step goes to a new session, whole
+    let calls3 = 0, gw3 = null;
+    gw3 = await startGatewaySim({ backend: (m, o) => { if (++calls3 === 1) gw3.state.failNext.push(503); return script(m, o); } });
+    const three = await run({ gw: gw3, text: 'what port does the server use?', wire: { state: null } });
+    const L3 = gw3.log;
+    ok(/8443/.test(three.r.content || '') && L3[1].status === 503 && L3.filter((e) => e.sentSession).length === 1 && !L3[2].sentSession && L3[2].session !== L3[0].session, `a follow-up the LLM was unavailable for (503) is not sent again on its handle: a new session, whole (${L3.map((e) => (e.sentSession ? 's' : 'W') + e.status).join(' ')})`);
+    // a follow-up whose connection never opened cannot have reached the session: the adapter sends it again on the
+    // same handle, and no whole send follows (the developer, 2026-11-02, a new session costing the whole conversation:
+    // "yes build it")
+    const gw4 = await startGatewaySim({ backend: script });
+    let posts = 0, refused = 0;
+    const refuseSecond = async (url, init) => { if (init && init.method === 'POST' && ++posts === 2) { refused++; const e = new TypeError('fetch failed'); e.cause = { code: 'ECONNREFUSED' }; throw e; } return fetch(url, init); };
+    const four = await run({ gw: gw4, text: 'what port does the server use?', wire: { state: null }, fetchImpl: refuseSecond });
+    const L4 = gw4.log;
+    ok(/8443/.test(four.r.content || '') && refused === 1 && L4.length === 2 && !L4[0].sentSession && L4[1].sentSession && L4[1].sentSession === L4[0].session && L4[1].status === 200, `a follow-up whose connection was refused: sent again on the SAME session, nothing whole (${L4.map((e) => (e.sentSession ? 's' : 'W') + e.status).join(' ')})`);
+    await gw.close(); await gw2.close(); await gw3.close(); await gw4.close();
+  }
+}
+
+// ── [handle] where the handle comes back ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+console.log('\n[handle] a gateway whose STREAMED frames carry no handle (not measured on the real one)');
+{
+  const { wireForgetLanes, wireLaneOf } = await import('../src/agent/wire-session.mjs');
+  const proj = path.join(dir, 'proj2'); await (await import('node:fs/promises')).mkdir(path.join(proj, 'src'), { recursive: true });
+  await writeFile(path.join(proj, 'src', 'config.ts'), 'export const PORT = 8443;\n');

+    const newest = turns[turns.length - 1] || '';
+    const body = /remote-workspace-result/.test(newest) ? (/list|src/.test(newest) && !/PORT/.test(newest) ? 'Now the file.\n<remote-workspace><js>return await read("src/config.ts");</js></remote-workspace>' : 'The port is 8443.') : 'Looking.\n<remote-workspace><js>return await list("src");</js></remote-workspace>';
+    return body + `\n/--- you ${tag} ---`;
+  };
+  const run = async (gw, model, wire) => {
+    const adapter = createHttpAdapter({ baseURL: gw.url, apiKey: 'x', maxRetries: 1 });

+    return { r, state: out, stats };

+  wireForgetLanes();

+  ok(/8443/.test(a.r.content || ''), 'the run still reaches its answer');
+  ok(L.join(' ') === 'W~ W. s.', `streamed opening with no handle, then the whole conversation UNSTREAMED (its reply carries one), then on the session, unstreamed (${L.join(' ')})`);
+  ok(wireLaneOf(PLAIN) === 'plain' && validWireState(a.state) && a.state.viaPlain === true, 'remembered for the model, and in what the next run is handed');
+  const n = gw.log.length;

+// ── [live-parts] a follow-up carries only the parts of the live state that changed (build 2522) ──
+console.log('\n[live-parts] only the parts of the live state that changed');

=== add test/test-worker-blank-nudge.mjs
new 6713337bd3f9c8553114b4b2
+/**
+ * A BLANK REPLY, OR ONE THAT IS ONLY THE ENDPOINT'S FOLLOW-UP FOOTER, IS NUDGED - not ended with "I don't have a
+ * written reply for that", which the developer then answered by typing "continue".
+ *
+ *   [1] the footer as the whole reply (stream left open, as the endpoint does): nudged at once - no waiting for the
+ *       stall-cut - and the run continues to the real answer; the closer never appears
+ *   [2] a blank reply with no tools before it: nudged, then answered
+ *   [3] blank every time: two nudges, then the old closer (bounded)
+ *   [4] a real reply followed by the footer: the reply is the answer, no nudge, no waiting
+ *   [5] the footer right after a tool turn: nudged (its own wording, not "read the results above")
+ *   [6] a blank reply after a tool turn is still nudged as before
+ * Run: node test/test-worker-blank-nudge.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+process.env.CB_HOME = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-blanknudge-home-'));
+const { runMinimalWorker } = await import('../src/agent/minimal-worker.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const FOOTER = '```suggested_questions\n"What is the specific purpose of the `code_boss` persona in this context?"\n"How should Gemini Enterprise respond to simulated conversational benchmarks?"\n```\n[DONE]\n\n';
+const hangUntilAbort = (signal) => new Promise((_, rej) => { const die = () => rej(Object.assign(new Error('aborted'), { name: 'AbortError' })); if (signal?.aborted) return die(); signal?.addEventListener?.('abort', die, { once: true }); });
+
+// a script of replies; { text, hang } - `hang` leaves the stream open after the text, as the endpoint does
+function streamingAdapter(script) {
+  let i = 0; const requests = [];
+  return {
+    requests,
+    async *completeStream(req) {
+      requests.push((req.messages || []).map((m) => (typeof m.content === 'string' ? m.content : '')).join('\n'));
+      const r = script[Math.min(i++, script.length - 1)];
+      if (r.text) yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: r.text } }] };
+      if (r.hang) await hangUntilAbort(req.signal);
+    },
+    async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  };
+}
+async function run(script, message = 'do the thing') {
+  const dir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-blanknudge-'));
+  const events = []; const adapter = streamingAdapter(script); const t0 = Date.now();
+  try {
+    const result = await runMinimalWorker({
+      adapter, model: 'm', signal: { aborted: false }, messages: [{ content: message }], projectDir: dir,
+      requireBranch: () => 'ok', requireApproval: () => true, onChatEvent: (e) => events.push(e), onPhase: () => {}, onLog: () => {},
+    });
+    return { result, events, adapter, ms: Date.now() - t0 };
+  } finally { await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }).catch(() => {}); }
+}
+const nudges = (ev) => ev.filter((e) => e.type === 'tool-call' && e.verb === 'nudge').map((e) => String(e.args?.command || ''));
+const texts = (ev) => ev.filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text').map((e) => String(e.content || ''));
+const CLOSER = /I don't have a written reply for that/;
+
+console.log('\n[1] the footer as the whole reply');
+{
+  const r = await run([{ text: FOOTER, hang: true }, { text: 'Here is the real answer.' }]);
+  ok(r.ms < 3000, `it did not wait for the stall-cut (${r.ms}ms; the default is 8000)`);
+  ok(nudges(r.events).length === 1 && /follow-up questions instead of an answer/.test(nudges(r.events)[0]), `one nudge, saying what happened (${nudges(r.events).join(' | ')})`);
+  ok(/only a follow-up-questions block/.test(r.adapter.requests[1] || ''), 'and the model was told, in its next request');
+  ok(!/suggested_questions|\[DONE\]/.test(texts(r.events).join(' ')), 'none of the footer reached the chat');
+  ok(r.events.some((e) => e.type === 'retract-assistant'), 'the fumbled turn is hidden from the chat');
+  ok(/Here is the real answer/.test(r.result.content) && !CLOSER.test(r.result.content), `the run ends on the real answer (${JSON.stringify(r.result.content).slice(0, 60)})`);
+  ok(r.adapter.requests.length === 2, `two model calls (${r.adapter.requests.length})`);
+}
+
+console.log('\n[2] a blank reply, no tools before it');
+{
+  const r = await run([{ text: '' }, { text: 'Answered now.' }]);
+  ok(nudges(r.events).length === 1 && /empty reply — asked to answer/.test(nudges(r.events)[0]), `nudged (${nudges(r.events).join(' | ')})`);
+  ok(/came back empty/i.test(r.adapter.requests[1] || ''), 'the model is told its reply was empty');
+  ok(/Answered now/.test(r.result.content) && !CLOSER.test(r.result.content), 'and the run ends on the answer, not the closer');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[3] blank every time is bounded');
+{
+  const r = await run([{ text: '' }]);
+  ok(nudges(r.events).length === 2, `two nudges (${nudges(r.events).length})`);
+  ok(CLOSER.test(r.result.content), 'then the old closer, so a run never ends silent');
+  ok(r.adapter.requests.length === 3, `three model calls in all (${r.adapter.requests.length})`);
+}
+
+console.log('\n[4] a real reply followed by the footer');
+{
+  const r = await run([{ text: 'A REST API lets programs talk to a server over HTTP.\n\n' + FOOTER, hang: true }]);
+  ok(r.ms < 3000 && nudges(r.events).length === 0, `no nudge and no waiting (${r.ms}ms, ${nudges(r.events).length} nudges)`);
+  ok(r.result.content.trim() === 'A REST API lets programs talk to a server over HTTP.', `the answer is the reply without the footer (${JSON.stringify(r.result.content).slice(0, 80)})`);
+  ok(r.adapter.requests.length === 1, 'one model call');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[5] the footer right after a tool turn');
+{
+  const r = await run([
+    { text: 'Checking.\n<remote-workspace><js>return "step done"</js></remote-workspace>' },
+    { text: FOOTER, hang: true },
+    { text: 'The step is done.' },
+  ]);
+  const n = nudges(r.events);
+  ok(n.length === 1 && /follow-up questions/.test(n[0]), `nudged with the footer wording (${n.join(' | ')})`);
+  ok(!/Read the results above/.test(r.adapter.requests[2] || ''), 'not the "read the results above" text, which a footer reply has not earned');
+  ok(/The step is done/.test(r.result.content), 'and the run finishes on the real answer');
+}
+
+console.log('\n[6] a blank reply after a tool turn is still nudged');
+{
+  const r = await run([
+    { text: 'Checking.\n<remote-workspace><js>return "step done"</js></remote-workspace>' },
+    { text: '' },
+    { text: 'Finished.' },
+  ]);
+  ok(nudges(r.events).length === 1 && /empty reply after tool results/.test(nudges(r.events)[0]), `the existing nudge (${nudges(r.events).join(' | ')})`);
+  ok(/Finished/.test(r.result.content) && !CLOSER.test(r.result.content), 'and it ends on the answer');
+}
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);

new 902eaaefeb65ff5dba4ab4b0
+// Unit test for worker-guards.mjs — churn detection, intent-to-act, turn ceiling.
+import { createWorkerGuards, churnToolSignature, TURN_CEILING } from '../src/agent/worker-guards.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, label) => { if (c) { pass++; process.stdout.write(`  ok ${label}\n`); } else { fail++; process.stdout.write(`  FAIL ${label}\n`); } };
+
+// ── churnToolSignature: order-independent, arg-normalized ──
+const a = [{ verb: 'readFile', args: { path: 'x' } }, { verb: 'listDir', args: { path: '.' } }];
+const b = [{ verb: 'listDir', args: { path: '.' } }, { verb: 'readFile', args: { path: 'x' } }];
+ok(churnToolSignature(a) === churnToolSignature(b), 'signature is order-independent');
+ok(churnToolSignature([{ verb: 'readFile', args: { path: 'x', b: 1 } }]) === churnToolSignature([{ verb: 'readFile', args: { b: 1, path: 'x' } }]), 'signature is arg-order-independent');
+ok(churnToolSignature([]) === '' && churnToolSignature(null) === '', 'empty calls → empty signature (never a churn)');
+ok(churnToolSignature(a) !== churnToolSignature([{ verb: 'readFile', args: { path: 'y' } }]), 'different args → different signature');
+
+// ── createWorkerGuards: churn = same non-empty signature twice in a row ──
+const g = createWorkerGuards();
+ok(g.noteTurn({ turn: 1, calls: a }).churn === false, 'turn 1: no churn (nothing before)');
+ok(g.noteTurn({ turn: 2, calls: b }).churn === true, 'turn 2: SAME signature as turn 1 → churn');
+ok(g.noteTurn({ turn: 3, calls: [{ verb: 'writeFile', args: { path: 'z' } }] }).churn === false, 'turn 3: different signature → no churn');
+ok(g.noteTurn({ turn: 4, calls: [] }).churn === false, 'turn 4: empty (talk-only) is never churn');
+ok(g.noteTurn({ turn: 5, calls: [] }).churn === false, 'turn 5: two empties in a row still not churn');
+
+// ── ceiling ──
+ok(g.noteTurn({ turn: 999, calls: [{ verb: 'readFile', args: { path: 'q' } }] }).ceiling === false, 'turn 999: below ceiling');
+ok(g.noteTurn({ turn: 1000, calls: [{ verb: 'x', args: {} }] }).ceiling === true, 'turn 1000: at ceiling');
+// 1000, not 50: long autonomous runs are the point; the churn guard is the runaway protection, this is the backstop.
+ok(TURN_CEILING === 1000, 'default ceiling is 1000');
+
+// THE INTENT-TO-ACT GUARD WAS REMOVED and its cases went with it. The line that used to live here —
+//   isIntentWithoutAction("Shall I build this?", false) === true
+// was the bug written down as a passing test: a question pinned as an announcement. An agent told to ask before
+// touching anything, which then asked, was answered by the harness with "you described what you would do but did
+// not do it". Nothing replaces it — the remaining guards are structural, not readings of intent.
+
+process.stdout.write(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed\n`);
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+
=== add test/test-worker-nudge-retract-e2e.mjs
new c7ae75739564d8f3375ea9d7
+/**
+ * End-to-end (bug review #6 / the nudge branch): a false EXECUTION-REFUSAL fumble ("I cannot execute the build…")
+ * triggers the auto-nudge; the fumble is RETRACTED from the chat (developer never sees it) and the re-run produces
+ * the real answer. (The nudge fumble is KEPT in model history — verified separately in the unit tests; here we
+ * assert the UI hiding.) Drives the real worker + UI. Run: node test/test-worker-nudge-retract-e2e.mjs
+ *
+ * The nudge under test is OFF by default (2026-08-14) — it could not tell a false refusal from a true "I can't
+ * run it until you tell me X", and a misfire retracts a legitimate question. This test turns it ON explicitly, so
+ * it keeps testing the retract MACHINERY (which every other nudge shares) rather than silently testing nothing.
+ */
+process.env.CB_EXEC_REFUSAL_NUDGE = '1';   // must be set before the worker module reads it
+import { mkdtemp, readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { cleanupDir } from './_helpers.mjs';
+import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
+// Loaded DYNAMICALLY, after the env is set: static imports are hoisted above every statement here, and the
+// worker reads CB_EXEC_REFUSAL_NUDGE once at module load — so a plain import would evaluate it too early and
+// this test would quietly assert nothing.
+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+
+const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+const ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');
+const PORT = 29145;
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const chrome = ['C:\\Program Files\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe', (process.env.LOCALAPPDATA || '') + '\\Google\\Chrome\\Application\\chrome.exe'].find((p) => { try { return p && existsSync(p); } catch { return false; } });
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+
+function chunkedAdapter(responses) {
+  let r = 0; const next = () => responses[Math.min(r++, responses.length - 1)];
+  return { async *completeStream() { for (const c of next()) { await sleep(4); yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: c } }] }; } }, async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: next().join('') } }] }; } };
+}
+
+async function main() {
+  if (!chrome) { console.error('Chrome not found'); process.exit(2); }
+  const projectDir = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-nudge-'));
+  const REFUSAL = 'I cannot execute the build directly in your workspace, so please run it yourself and paste the output.';
+  const responses = [
+    [REFUSAL],                                          // turn 1 → false exec-refusal fumble (triggers the nudge)
+    ['I ran it — the build actually SUCCEEDED in 4s.'], // re-run after the nudge → the real answer
+    [''],
+  ];
+  const html = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'src', 'ui.html'), 'utf8');
+  const htmlWithConfig = html.replace('</head>', '<script>window.SERVER_CONFIG=' + JSON.stringify({ defaultModel: 'm', baseURL: '(mock)' }) + ';</script></head>');
+  const { server } = await startServer({ html: htmlWithConfig, baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'test-key', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter: chunkedAdapter(responses), modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: projectDir });
+  const browser = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: chrome, headless: 'new', args: ['--no-sandbox'] });
+  try {
+    const page = await browser.newPage();
+    await page.setViewport({ width: 1100, height: 800 });
+    const errs = []; page.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(e.message));
+    await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/`);
+    await page.waitForSelector('#chat:not(.hidden)', { timeout: 15000 });
+    await sleep(300);
+
+    console.log('\n[A] the false refusal is retracted from the chat; the real answer survives');
+    await page.type('#input', 'build the project');
+    await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
+    await page.waitForFunction(() => window.__sessionRef?.status === 'done' || window.__sessionRef?.status === 'idle', { timeout: 20000 });
+    await sleep(500);
+
+    const bubbles = await page.$$eval('.row.assistant', (els) => els.map((el) => el.querySelector('.body')?.textContent.trim() || '').filter(Boolean));
+    ok(bubbles.some((b) => /actually SUCCEEDED/.test(b)), `the real answer is shown (bubbles: ${JSON.stringify(bubbles.map((s) => s.slice(0, 30)))})`);
+    ok(!bubbles.some((b) => /cannot execute the build/i.test(b)), 'the false-refusal fumble was RETRACTED from the chat');
+    ok(errs.length === 0, `no page errors (${JSON.stringify(errs.slice(0, 2))})`);
+  } catch (e) { fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e?.message || e); }
+  finally { await browser.close(); try { server.close(); } catch {} await cleanupDir(projectDir); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
+}
+main();
+
